- `markdown/`: `parseMarkdown(text)` (`markdown-parser.ts`) turns a news or project body into blocks (paragraph with line breaks, heading level 2 or 3, list, quote) of inline nodes (text, bold, italic, link, break), the subset in `docs/api-contract.md` ("`content` — public CMS"); a link keeps only an `http(s)`, `mailto:` or site-relative address, everything else, HTML included, stays text. `<app-markdown [text]>` renders the tree with its own elements through a recursive `ng-template` and `{{ }}` bindings (no `innerHTML`), in the prose look (70 ch, serif headings); a plain click on a link to a page of the site (site-relative or the site's own origin, not under `/api/` and not a file with an extension) opens it through the router, so it adds no browser entry (own history, #431), and every other link, or one clicked with a modifier key, is followed by the browser. `<app-markdown-toolbar [target]>` above a textarea: the `role="group"` "Formátování textu" with Tučně, Kurzíva, Nadpis, Odrážky, Číslování and Odkaz, each wrapping the selection or prefixing its lines (a placeholder word selected when nothing was), then focusing the textarea and dispatching `input` so `ngModel` follows; the field's hint says how to format and that the editor's "Náhled" shows the look (the body-only preview under the textarea went with the editors' whole preview, Jakub 2026-10-24). Texts under `ui.markdown.*`; specs beside each.
- `file-button/`: `<app-file-button inputId [accept] [describedBy] [disabled] (chosen)>` is a file choice that looks like a button: a visually hidden `input type=file` and its `<label class="ui-button">` with the projected text (the focus ring of the input drawn on the label); `chosen` emits the `File` and the input is reset, so the same file can be chosen again (the photo in the news and project editors, the signature scan).
- `download-progress/`: `<app-download-progress [progress] [compact] (cancelled)>` shows a `DownloadProgressState` (`running` with `step` of `steps`, its text and an optional `detail`, `ready`, `done`, `cancelled`) in an always rendered `role="status"` line (its own typography: `--text-sm`, `--text-xs` when `compact` as in the payments table, neutral while running, ready and cancelled, the success colour once done, `overflow-wrap: anywhere`; the caller sets the alignment on the host), a 12 rem `<progress>` (at most the width it gets, named by the text, with the `detail` as its `aria-valuetext` and shown beside it, hidden from the accessibility tree, so a detail that changes often, such as the page, is not announced) while running and "Zrušit" once it has run for 8 s (`CANCEL_OFFERED_AFTER_MS`); it has no button of its own for `ready`, because the button that started the download opens the ready file.
- `diagnostics-download/`: `<app-diagnostics-download source>` (`account-page` or `receipt-pdf`) says to attach the diagnostic file to a message about trouble with the site, that it holds only the state of the site on this device and goes nowhere by itself, links `mailto:info@nfnn.cz?subject=Diagnostický soubor` (the subject only; under a receipt PDF failure, `source="receipt-pdf"` (`underReceipt`), the lead is "K e‑mailu nám prosím přiložte diagnostický soubor. …" without the link and the saved and ready-to-share lines leave out the address, because the failure line right above names it), in a `PUBLIC` session asks to delete the file after sending it, and one button (works offline; `aria-disabled` while busy, so it keeps the focus) prepares the file through `Diagnostics.prepare` ("Připravujeme soubor…") and saves it (`save`, then a `role="status"` line "Soubor <name> je ve stažených souborech. Přiložte ho prosím k e‑mailu na info@nfnn.cz.") or, where `needsSecondTap` says so (an iPhone or iPad that can share the file, as `PdfDelivery` decides for the receipt), becomes "Sdílet diagnostický soubor" and opens the share sheet at the second tap (a closed sheet keeps it ready, a failed share saves it); a failure is an alert that asks to write without it.
- `focal-point/` (slice 2.23b): `FocalPoint` (`{x, y}` in whole percent), `PHOTO_SHAPES` (each shape the site crops a content photo into, with its ratio and the position it used before a point existed: `newsCard`, `projectCard`, `articleTop`, and since 2.26 `newsFeatured` (12:5), `newsFeaturedMedium` (3:2) and `newsFeaturedTall` (4:5) for the leading news of Aktuality as wide, middle and upright as the card stands on a computer, a tablet and a phone, `newsCardSide` (1:1) for an older news card with its photo beside the text and `storyTile` for a story of the year; 2.23a adds the wide band and the square), `objectPosition(point, shape)` and the directive `img[appFocalPoint][focalShape]`, which binds the image's `object-position`; the news card, the project card and the article top use it, so a photo without a point keeps its earlier crop.
- `avatar/`: `<app-avatar [name] [src] [size] [srcset] [sizes] [loading]>` is the one place that draws a person's round picture: the portrait (`alt=""`, `width`/`height` = `size`, cover-cropped to a circle) or, without one, the initials of the first two words of `name` in capitals (Jakub 2026-10-31: "ZZ" for Zdeňka Znamenáčková); the whole host is `aria-hidden`, because the name always stands beside it. The parent's class sets its width, height and the `font-size` token of the initials; the theme gives the circle, the light surface behind it and the serif. Used by the home "Kdo fond vede" block and `/sprava/lide-fondu` (the list and the portrait preview).
- `dialog/`: `<app-dialog labelledBy [wide] [tall] [grouped] [presentation] [pushesHistory] [dismissible] [depth] [showsClose] (dismissed) (steppedBack)>` (`[depth]` above 0 keeps that many steps of the dialog's own in the history through `DialogHistory.showStep`, so Back, the app's edge swipe and `history.back()` first emit `steppedBack` and leave the dialog open, and a page that keeps its depth after `steppedBack` gets its step pushed again; `[showsClose]="false"` hides the × while a step is shown) (`[dismissible]="false"` while a step runs: Esc is refused, × is disabled, the backdrop and Back do nothing, and a close the browser forces anyway opens it again) on a native `<dialog>` opened with `showModal()` (focus kept inside, page inert, focus to the `autofocus` control; opened by a finger or a mouse, its title takes the focus (the element `labelledBy` names, given `tabindex="-1"`, no ring, `preventScroll`; the dialog itself when the title is not inside it), whatever control asks for `autofocus`, because a ring after a tap is a bug (Jakub 2026-11-06, design plan J3), Safari on the iPhone would otherwise ring the × after every tap and, focusing it while the sheet still slides in, scroll the dialog so that the sheet stopped short of the screen's bottom edge, and opened by the keyboard the browser focuses the `autofocus` control or its first control as before (`core/input/input-modality.ts`, `InputModality.lastPressByPointer()`, listening from the app initializer `inputModalityAtStart`); a sheet's dialog clips instead of hiding its overflow, so nothing can scroll it; at `bp.down(nav)` every sheet's × is a round button of `--sheet-close-size` (the size of the header's Zpět) on `--color-sheet-close`, `--sheet-close-inset` from the top and the right edge, with an X icon in place of the × glyph, and a `[tall]` sheet (the account sheet, after the Music app's account sheet on iOS, Jakub 2026-11-06) fills the screen up to `--sheet-top-gap` under the top safe area whatever its content and starts its content on the row of the ×; a `[grouped]` dialog lays its panel on `--color-sheet-grouped`, so its `.ui-list--grouped` lists stand as inset cards with `--sheet-group-gap` between them (the account sheet, after the Music app's account sheet on iOS in the style of NFNN, Jakub 2026-11-06: the logo as its heading, named Účet, on the row of the round ×; a card of the donor (portrait or initials, name, Údaje a zabezpečení) that leads to Údaje a zabezpečení; the other sections as one card without icons, the variable symbol as a note under it, Správa for an administrator, Přidat na plochu as a blue action and Odhlásit se as a centred red row, each its own card; the foundation's line under them as quiet `--color-sheet-secondary` text; every measure a `--sheet-*` token taken from the Music app: 40 px round ×, 18 px side inset, 47 px rows in the body size, 24 px cards 32 px apart, no visible grabber on a `[tall]` sheet); Esc, × and backdrop emit `dismissed`; focus returns to the opener on destroy; full-screen up to 480 px). `presentation` (`dialog-presentation.ts`, plan v3 2.22) is `auto` (the default), `centered`, `sheet` or `full`, written to `data-presentation`: `auto` takes `DIALOG_PRESENTATION`, an injection token that an area shell may provide and that is `centered` (today's dialog) unless one does, so no dialog changes its look until the app shells of 2.23 and 2.24 provide `sheet`; at `bp.down(nav)` a sheet is anchored to the bottom (`--sheet-max-width`, at most the screen height less `--space-12`, its own scroll, top corners `--radius-sheet`, `--shadow-sheet`, the bottom padding above the safe area, a grabber with `aria-hidden` across the top of the sheet (`--space-8` high, the visible bar its `::before`), which a primary pointer drags down (2.24, `sheet-drag.ts`, `SheetDrag`: the panel follows the pointer through the CSS `translate` property, since the entrance animation holds `transform`, and closes like × once it moved at least the larger of 72 px and a quarter of its height, or on a flick of 0.5 px/ms after 24 px, otherwise it springs back; a finger still moving up as it lets go has changed its mind, and the sheet springs back however far it went (#382)); a finger also pulls the sheet by its content as on iOS (#382, below `nav` only, touch events because Safari cancels pointer events once it scrolls): `canPullSheetFrom` lets a touch pull only when the panel and every element between it and the touched one are scrolled to their top, no text is selected, and it did not start on the focused text field, a `textarea`, `select`, slider, `canvas`, `contenteditable` or `app-zoomable`, which keep their own gestures, and `pullIntent` decides after 10 px: mostly down (more than 1.2 times sideways) pulls the sheet, anything else (up, sideways, or a finger that rested 300 ms first) is left to the content for the rest of that touch, a second finger springs the sheet back, and a pulling touch calls `preventDefault`, so the content neither scrolls nor bounces and the keyboard directive below sees no scroll (an open keyboard stays until the sheet closes, the experts' choice for #382, so a sheet that springs back keeps its field and caret); the backdrop's veil and blur fade with the share of the sheet pulled away (`--dialog-pull`, also set from `[pulledAway]` by the zoom viewer); every close of a sheet below `nav` (a release far enough or a flick, ×, Esc, the backdrop, Back, the home-screen app's swipe, `history.back()`) slides it out as a native iOS sheet does: the dialog takes `dialog--leaving`, the panel goes from where it is (where the finger left it, or its resting place) to `translate: 0 100%` over `--duration-sheet` with `--ease-sheet` while the veil and blur fade out with it, the panel is inert meanwhile (so a second press, a backdrop tap or a new pull does nothing) and the dialog itself holds the focus (no ring), and only when the panel's `translate` transition has finished (`getAnimations()` read after the render that starts it, since the dragging class turns transitions off until then; at the latest its duration plus 1 s, so a slide the browser never runs, as in a hidden tab, cannot hold the dialog) it emits `dismissed`, so the page removes it, the history entry goes back as before and the focus returns to the opener on destroy (while the modal is open the page under it is inert and cannot take the focus); Esc is kept from hiding the dialog at once (`cancel` prevented while it may be), and a close the browser forces during the slide ends it at once; Back during the slide adds no entry; a page that keeps it open gets it back in place, and after a Back asks for its entry again; under `prefers-reduced-motion` it closes at once without the slide; above `nav` and for `centered`, `full` and `viewer` dialogs a close is immediate as before; a dialog that is not dismissible, and one whose page sets `[unsavedInput]` (an opt-in for input that took effort and is kept nowhere else; no dialog sets it yet, since the registration keeps its draft and the other sheets hold a code or a tick), only stretches under the finger with growing resistance (`rubberBand`, at most 96 px) and springs back, as iOS does under `isModalInPresentation`; a release of the second kind that would have closed it opens a nested sheet `role="alertdialog"` "Zahodit rozepsané údaje?" with "Pokračovat v psaní" (focused, also Esc, Back and its own pull) and "Zahodit", which closes the sheet; ×, Esc, the backdrop and Back close without asking, as on iOS, where only the gesture is guarded; the × stays the way that needs no gesture, WCAG 2.5.7), the side padding at least the side safe area, and the action row `.ui-dialog__actions` (`_dialog.scss`) sticky at the bottom of the sheet above the bottom safe area, so the buttons of a long sheet stay in reach, the × kept, WCAG 2.5.7) and `full` fills the screen; above `nav` both are the centered dialog. While the on-screen keyboard is open (`core/layout/soft-keyboard.ts`; `keyboard-avoidance.ts`, the directive `KeyboardAvoidance` on the `<dialog>`, #359) the dialog keeps the part of the screen the keyboard covers as its bottom padding (`--keyboard-covered-height`, `_dialog.scss`; the full-screen phone dialog fills it with the surface colour, a sheet sits above the keyboard), so its whole content can be scrolled above the keyboard, and the focused field is scrolled the least way into the visible part (16 px clear of its edges), together with its form's submit button when both fit, on focus and whenever the visible part changes; a one-finger drag that scrolls the dialog with the keyboard open (one that starts on a text field, uses two fingers or comes after the finger let go does not) moves the focus to the panel (`tabindex="-1"` until the focus leaves it), which closes the keyboard as a native form sheet does (#395) and keeps the focus inside the dialog. Every dialog keeps a history entry (2.22): on open it calls `DialogHistory.open` with a listener of its own, Back over that entry emits `dismissed` (a sheet after its slide), and when the page keeps the dialog open (a running restore, erasure, pairing or withdrawal refuses to close) it asks for the entry again after the next render, so the following Back still lands on the dialog and not on the previous page; on destroy it gives its own entries back with `close(listener)`, leaving those of a dialog opened over it. `[pushesHistory]="false"` is set by the two dialogs whose history is kept elsewhere: the sign-in dialog, whose `LoginDialogService` adds entries for its steps and hands its entry over to the page it was opened for, and the unsaved-changes dialog, which exists only while a navigation waits on its guard (an entry pushed then would race the router restoring the address when the visitor stays). The panel ends with a visually hidden `role="status"` region, rendered with the dialog and empty, which shows `DialogAnnouncer.message()` (`dialog-announcer.ts`, root): while a modal dialog is open the page behind it is inert and out of the accessibility tree, so a message from behind it (the chunk-load bar) is announced only from inside the open dialog; `announce(text)` returns the function that withdraws it, which leaves a later announcement standing. `dialog-history.ts` (`DialogHistory`, root service) gives every dialog a step back in the site's own history (`core/navigation/own-history.ts`; the browser's own Back leaves the site since #431): `open(listener)` pushes one entry for the same address (state key `nfnnDialogDepth`), deferred until a running navigation ends (`router.currentNavigation()` is null), so a guard-opened dialog adds it on the page it redirected to; `showStep(depth, listener?)` adds or takes back entries for further steps of that dialog (the sign-in dialog names its own, so a dialog over it keeps its entry on top); Back over its own entry calls the listener's `stepBack()` or `close()`, and a jump past the page closes the dialog and leaves the navigation to the router; `close(listener?)` takes that dialog's entries back with `history.go(-n)` (without a listener, the top dialog's), and Back tells the listeners only once the stack is settled, so a listener that closes itself on Back adds no entry; an entry that carries the depth when no dialog owns one (a reload with a dialog open, Forward onto the entry of a closed dialog) is never left as a dead Back press: `app.config.ts` starts `DialogHistory` before the first page opens (`dialogHistoryAtStart`), which takes such an entry back unseen by the router, and a dialog opened on such an entry adopts it instead of adding another; it keeps a stack of open dialogs (2.24), each with its own entries: a dialog opened over another adds its entry on top, Back closes or steps back only the top one, a lower dialog that closes by itself takes back only its own entries and leaves the one above open, a jump back past several closes them top first, and a dialog that closes while another opens in the same moment (the pairing dialog giving way to "Mezitím se to změnilo") leaves exactly the new one's entry; `LoginDialogService` passes its own listener, so the sign-in dialog leaves another dialog's entry alone; a navigation a page starts itself while a dialog is open (`sendToSignIn` after a 401) leaves the dialog's entry under the new page, one extra Back press, until such a navigation hands the entry over like the sign-in dialog does; the fold at start strips the mark from the entry before it goes back, so the router's first navigation never copies it to the page below; if that traversal never arrived, the next Back would be taken as the fold's; the tests press Back from the page (`history.back()`), so the home-screen app's swipe over the entry a refused close asks for again is on Jakub's device checklist; `handOver(url)` replaces the top entry with the page a guard-opened dialog was opened for, which the router then replaces again (the router replaces an entry whose address is already the target), so Back from that page returns to the page under the dialog; `release()` forgets the entries without traversing (a link that opens another page in their place with `replaceUrl`), and any imperative navigation forgets them too, so closing later never leaves the page. Its popstate listener sits on `window` in the capture phase and is registered when the module loads, before Angular's `Location` registers its own: Chrome runs listeners on `window` in registration order, and the dialog's own entries must never reach the router, whose same-address `NavigationSkipped` would make `public-shell` move the focus to the page heading. `testing/fake-dialog-history.ts` records the calls for specs.
- `needs-connection/` (plan v3 2.7): `[appNeedsConnection]` on a control that needs the server (every submit button, every button without a `type`, every `ui-button--danger`/`--danger-soft` button and every button whose `(click)` calls a method of its component that subscribes, `firstValueFrom` or `lastValueFrom`, directly or through other methods of the component, checked by `scripts/needs-connection.mjs` in `npm run lint`; its exceptions in `WORKS_WITHOUT_CONNECTION`, a whole template or `template#handler`, each with its reason and reported once it names no control (a `template#handler` entry also clears a danger button whose click calls only listed handlers): the receipt code reader of `/overit`, which checks offline, the sheet's "Zkusit znovu", which is the probe, sign-out from the account sheet, which forgets the session on the device without the server, and the two panel switches of the first setup, and the dialog that deletes the data on this device, which is local and signs out the same way): while `Connectivity.offline()` it sets `aria-disabled="true"` (keeping a value it had), the class `needs-connection-offline`, and a hint `span.ui-needs-connection` "Potřebuje připojení" after the control, named in its `aria-describedby`; the control stays focusable and its click (capture phase) and the submit of its form (capture phase at the document) are swallowed, so nothing is sent and the text typed stays.
- `zoom-viewer/` (plan v3 2.23d, Jakub 2026-10-31): `<app-zoomable [content]>` wraps a photo or a QR code in a button named "Zvětšit fotku: <description>" ("Zvětšit fotku" without one) or "Zvětšit: <QR label>" (`aria-haspopup="dialog"`, `cursor: zoom-in`, no other visible change) that opens `<app-zoom-viewer [content] (closed)>`: an `<app-dialog presentation="viewer">` (full screen at every width on `--color-surface-inverse`, the × and the tools in `--color-on-inverse`, safe areas kept) holding the content as `ZoomContent` (`zoom-content.ts`: `{ kind: 'photo', src, alt, width?, height? }`, the largest stored width, uncropped, or `{ kind: 'qr', matrix, label }`, drawn again by `app-qr-code`) fitted to the stage (a size container, `touch-action: none`). The viewer zooms itself, because the page cannot (Accessibility, `frontend.md`): two fingers pinch around their middle and pan, a double tap or double click zooms to 250 % at the point and back, the wheel (and a trackpad pinch, which Chrome sends as a wheel with Ctrl) zooms under the pointer without scrolling the page, `+`/`=`, `-`, `0` and the arrows act on the focused stage (`role="group"`, named by the hidden title "Zvětšená fotka" or "Zvětšený QR kód" and described by a hidden hint), and the buttons Oddálit, the level ("150 %", which returns to 100 %) and Přiblížit, each step announced through `DialogAnnouncer` ("Přiblíženo na 150 %"). At 100 % one finger swipes it away as in Photos (#382): the content follows the finger (sideways too), the dark background fades through the dialog's `[pulledAway]`, and a release that `SheetDrag` reads as closing closes the viewer as × does, while a short or upward swipe eases back; zoomed in, the finger pans instead, a second finger turns the swipe into a pinch, and a mouse never swipes. From 100 % to 400 %; dragging stops at the content's edge (`zoom-geometry.ts`: `zoomAround`, `panBy`, `clampView`, `zoomStep`, the content's own layout size, so a transform never feeds back). Today it wraps the photo of a news or project page (`content-article`), the About photo (`about`) and the QR code of Přispět (`payment-panel`); the QR codes inside dialogs (the 1 Kč add-account payment, the second-factor recovery) are not wrapped, since a dialog over a dialog waits on `DialogHistory`'s stack (2.18 / 2.24).

## Layout

`chunk-load-notice/`: rendered by `app.ts` after the `router-outlet` while `ChunkLoadRecovery.failure()` is set, with the text "Stránku se nepodařilo otevřít. Web byl mezitím možná aktualizován nebo vypadlo připojení. Zkuste ji načíst znovu." When the first page failed (`initial`; the router has activated nothing, so no shell is on screen), a page of its own: `main#obsah.ui-panel-page` with a `.ui-panel`, the `h1` "Stránku se nepodařilo otevřít" (focused; the tab title becomes "Stránku se nepodařilo otevřít | Nová naděje – nadační fond" until the router sets the next page's title), the rest of the text as the lead, the button "Načíst znovu" and the link "Přejít na úvod" (`href="/"`, a full load). After a later navigation, a bar over the page (`.chunk-load-bar.ui-notice--alert.ui-notice--floating`, fixed at the bottom, at most `--content-max` wide) whose button "Načíst znovu" takes the focus and is `aria-describedby` the message, so the message is announced once, with the focus move; the bar is no `role="alert"`, because an alert would announce the same text again and a screen reader that cuts speech short on the focus move could drop it; when the focus cannot reach the button (an open modal `dialog` makes the page behind it inert and leaves it out of the accessibility tree, so no region of the bar would be announced either), the bar hands the message, read from its own rendered text so the one i18n id `layout.chunk-load.message` serves both, to `DialogAnnouncer`, whose region inside the open dialog announces it, and withdraws it when the bar goes; the notice itself renders no status region, so a page's own status region stays the only one; while it shows, `:root` carries `has-floating-notice` and `--floating-notice-height` (measured, kept by a `ResizeObserver`; the themes set it to `0rem`), which add that room below the page (the public footer's own `padding-bottom`, so its colour reaches the bar, or `body`'s on a page without it) and to `scroll-padding-bottom`, and a `focusin` listener scrolls a control the bar would hide above it in the next animation frame, after a left field has added its error line (Chromium does not scroll an element that is already in the viewport behind a fixed bar), leaving controls in an open `dialog` alone, so the bar never covers the end of the page or the focus (WCAG 2.4.11). That room-keeping lives in `shared/ui/floating-notice/floating-notice-room.ts` (`keepRoomForFloatingNotice`), shared with `signed-out-notice/`: rendered by `public-shell/` after the footer (where signing out lands; outside the donor and administration shells, whose pages keep their own status regions), an always rendered `role="status"` region that, while `core/auth/signed-out-notice.ts` (`SignedOutNotice`, root; `show(reason)`, `signed-out` or `device-data-deleted`) is shown, holds a `.ui-notice--status.ui-notice--floating` bar "Odhlásili jste se." with "Zavřít" (focus to `main#obsah`); the account sheet's "Odhlásit se" shows it once the navigation to `/` has finished, and the next navigation, a new sign-in or "Zavřít" takes it away.

`offline-status/` (plan v3 2.7): rendered by `app.ts` after the update notice. An always rendered polite region (`aria-live="polite"`, no `role="status"`, through `DialogAnnouncer` while a dialog is open) says "Jste offline. Zobrazujeme údaje z 12:30.", "Server fondu teď neodpovídá." and "Připojení je zpět."; while `Connectivity.offline()` a pill button fixed at the bottom centre ("Offline · údaje z 12:30", or "Offline", in the warning colours, above the floating notice through `.has-floating-notice`) keeps room for itself (`offline-pill-room.ts`: `html.is-offline` and its measured height in `--offline-pill-height`, `0rem` in the theme, so `scroll-padding-bottom`, the bottom padding of the page or the footer and the sticky `.ui-action-bar`, raised above it, keep content and the focused control clear of it) and opens a sheet (`app-dialog presentation="sheet"`): "Jste offline" with "Bez připojení můžete jen prohlížet. The landing page takes no such bottom padding, because its hero is exactly one screen and padding made it scroll and showed a dark band under the photo in the installed app (Jakub 2026-11-10): the hero's text keeps the room instead (`--floating-room` in `hero.scss`, the pill's or the floating notice's height and `--space-3` each), so the pill and the notice stack between the text and the tab bar over the photo; `e2e/first-screen.spec.ts` checks it offline on the phone screens. Zkontrolujte Wi-Fi nebo mobilní data." or "Server neodpovídá" with "Připojení k internetu máte, ale server fondu teď neodpovídá. Zkoušíme to znovu sami.", the age of the data ("Zobrazujeme údaje z …, nemusí být aktuální.") with what waits ("Odeslat zprávu, přihlásit se nebo cokoli změnit půjde, až se připojíte.", or "… až server zase odpoví.") and "Zkusit znovu" (`Connectivity.tryAgain()`; while it tries `aria-disabled`, so the focus stays, "Zkoušíme se připojit…" or "Zkoušíme spojení se serverem…", then in its status line "Pořád se nedaří připojit. Za chvíli to zkusíme znovu sami." or "Server pořád neodpovídá. …" in the warning colour, or "Připojení je zpět." with a "Zavřít" button in its place that takes the focus); while the sheet is open only its own status line speaks, while another dialog is open the announcement goes through `DialogAnnouncer`; texts under `layout.offline.*`. A dialog whose opener is gone when it closes (here the pill) returns the focus to `main#obsah`.

`update-notice/` (plan v3 2.3): rendered by `app.ts` after the chunk-load notice; an always rendered polite live region (`aria-live="polite"`, `aria-atomic="true"`, deliberately no `role="status"`, so a page's own status region stays the only one, as with the chunk-load notice, and the specs and journeys that read the page's status keep finding exactly one) that, while `ServerVersion.mustUpdate()` or `AppUpdates.mustReload()` and neither the chunk-load notice (`ChunkLoadRecovery.failure()`) nor the signed-out notice is shown, holds a bar `.ui-notice--warning.ui-notice--floating` with "Web má novou verzi. Než budete cokoli odesílat, načtěte ji." (`layout.update.message`) and the button "Načíst novou verzi" (`layout.update.load`, `aria-describedby` the message), which loads the current page in the newest build (`AppUpdates.loadNewVersion(router.url)`). It never takes the focus; when a modal `dialog` is open as it appears it hands its text to `DialogAnnouncer`. It keeps room below the page with `keepRoomForFloatingNotice`, as the other floating bars do, and waits while one of them is shown, so two bars never lie on each other. `shared/ui/floating-notice/floating-notice-room.ts` keeps a list of the bars on screen per document and keeps room for the latest, so when one bar hands over to another (this one to the chunk-load bar, the signed-out bar to this one) the page keeps its room until the last bar goes.

App shell of plan v3 2.23 (the decisions of 2.1, variant E V1): every public page and the donor area share one header and, at every width, one floating tab bar, and the donor area keeps its side panel on wide screens. `app-header/` (`app-header`): a `header` (the `banner`, `tabindex="-1"` without an outline) laid over the top of the page (`position: absolute`, the safe area added at the top, and its row aligned with `.ui-container`: `--space-8` at the sides, `--space-5` up to `tablet`, or the side safe area when larger), so it scrolls away with the page and has no bar of its own behind it: on the left the logo to `/` (`/images/logo-on-dark.svg` over a photo or a dark head, `/images/logo.svg` over a light one, chosen by the route's `head`), on the right, signed out, "Přihlásit se" (a secondary pill in the glass of the back button and the tab bar, so the filled blue stays the page's own call to action, design plan 3.1; opens the sign-in; after it the focus moves to the button "Účet" that took its place; the logo may shrink and "Přihlásit se" keeps one line in `--text-header-action`, `--text-sm` capped by the viewport, so at 200 % text on a 320 px phone and at the largest iPhone text the row still fits and the header keeps its height) and, signed in, a round button named "Účet" (`aria-haspopup="dialog"`) that shows the portrait of an administrator linked to a person of the foundation (`account-sheet/account-portrait.ts`, `AccountPortrait`: `PeopleApi.mine()` asked once per signed-in administrator, kept by the donor's id, so the portrait of one account never shows for the next one signed in on the same tab, `?w=480`, the smallest variant; an error keeps the initials), otherwise the initials of the name (`core/auth/initials.ts`: the first letters of the first and the last word, `aria-hidden`, while a visually hidden "Účet" names the button) or a person icon without a name, and opens the account sheet; a count the account holds for the donor (`core/layout/account-badges.ts`, `AccountBadges`: the donor area puts there the bank accounts with a question about an older symbol, the count the side panel shows, and clears it when the area closes) stands at the button's corner, `aria-hidden`, and its description ("1 otázka čeká na odpověď") joins the button's name, except on the page that holds it, and the sheet's row of that section shows the same count. A page deeper than a tab root shows the fixed round back button "Zpět" (`aria-label`, the glass of the tab bar) before the logo, at the left edge of the content column; on wide screens the donor area leaves it out (`backOnWide: false`), because its side panel leads around. Wherever the back button shows, it stands alone without the logo (Jakub 2026-11-05 for the donor area and the administration, 2026-11-06 for every page, #431: the two clashed side by side), and the account button or "Přihlásit se" keeps to the right; the logo shows only on a page without the back button (a tab root, the landing page, and the donor area on a wide screen, where the side panel leads around). Once the page has scrolled, an edge at the top covers what scrolls under the status bar and the back button, as the scroll edge effect of iOS does, so no line of text runs under the clock or the button (`app-header/scroll-edge.ts`, `followScrollEdge`, started by the header: the header's `::before` on every page; on a tab root only the top safe area (or, in an iPad window, `--window-controls-inset-block`) plus `--space-2` and the fade, and nothing at all where both are 0, as in a browser on a computer (design plan 3.1, R2); on a page with the back button `--header-height` and the top safe area opaque, then fading out over `--scroll-edge-fade` (24 px), in the colour of the ground just under its lower edge (`--scroll-edge-tone`, read with `elementsFromPoint` in the next animation frame after each scroll, resize or change of the device's appearance: the first opaque background on the way up that spans the whole width of the screen, so the dark head and the white body each run on under it, and a card or a see-through button is passed over for the ground around it; until 2026-11-07 it took the card's colour, which on the grouped ground of the donor area and the administration stood out as a band of the card with a hard lower edge, Jakub's report from the home-screen app), and the header's `::after` under it with `backdrop-filter: blur(var(--blur-bar))`, masked to fade out only over the last third of `--scroll-edge-fade`, so what fades out under the edge is blurred rather than half shown, as the soft scroll edge effect of iOS; both pointer-transparent, pseudo-elements outside the accessibility tree, the edge solid with a hairline and no blur under `prefers-contrast: more` and `prefers-reduced-transparency: reduce`, `Canvas` with a `CanvasText` hairline under `forced-colors: active`, and hidden at the top of the page, so an unscrolled page looks as before; `e2e/back-button-edge.spec.ts` checks on Údaje a zabezpečení in both appearances that the edge takes the ground, not the card, blurs, and follows a change of appearance; a dark tone marks the header `app-header--edge-dark`). Below `nav`, on a page with the back button, once the page `h1` (`main#obsah h1`) has scrolled above the header row, its text stands centred in that edge, between the back button and the account button, on one line with an ellipsis, `--text-lg` semibold in `--font-sans`, fading in over `--duration-fast` (none under `prefers-reduced-motion`, the global rule), in `--color-text` or `--color-on-inverse` on a dark edge, and `aria-hidden`, since the `h1` still names the page (`app-header/large-title.ts`, `followLargeTitle`, checked in the next frame after a scroll, a resize and each navigation; Jakub 2026-11-06, design plan J5, compatible with "no top bar"); the account button is fixed there too (`app-header--pinned-account`), so it stays beside the back button instead of scrolling away. The logo keeps at least 96 px. The first section of every public page starts `--page-top` (the top safe area, `--header-height` and `--space-4`) or more below the top (`.ui-intro`, `.ui-panel-page`, the 404, O nás and Přispět), so its eyebrow keeps clear of the logo. The global `scroll-padding-top` adds the top safe area, so a focused control lands below the edge (#352). `account-sheet/` (`app-account-sheet`, inside `<app-dialog>`, `presentation` `sheet` while `CompactLayout.isCompact()`, a centred dialog otherwise): the head with the portrait or the initials, the `h2` with the donor's name ("Účet" without one) and "Variabilní symbol …", a `nav` of `ui-list` rows (Můj přehled, Moje dary, Potvrzení o daru, Bankovní účty, Údaje a zabezpečení, and for an administrator "Správa" with the badge "jen správci"), "Přidat na plochu" while `AppInstall.way()` offers a way (Jakub 2026-10-31 13:38: it stays in the sheet whatever "Teď ne" on the install card decided; with the browser's prompt the row closes the sheet and asks the browser, on an iPhone or in Safari on a Mac it unfolds the install card's instruction, `aria-expanded`) and "Odhlásit se" (`ui-button--danger-soft`, "Odhlašujeme…" while it runs; then `/` and the signed-out notice), and under it, because the phone footer stands only on O nás (Jakub 2026-10-31 14:59), `info@nfnn.cz`, "Kontakt" (`/o-nas#kontakt`), the registry line with the IČO and "Zásady ochrany osobních údajů" (the footer's i18n ids). The row of the section open carries `aria-current="page"`. A row or link hands the sheet's history entry over to the page it opens (`DialogHistory.handOver`, which answers whether the sheet owned an entry) and navigates with `replaceUrl` and the next app depth, so Back from that page returns where the sheet was opened; without an owned entry it navigates as a new entry, and to the address already open it only closes. The header closes the sheet when the session ends. `tab-bar/` (`app-tab-bar`): the `nav` "Hlavní sekce" with four links in a grid, O nás `/o-nas`, Aktuality `/aktuality`, Pomáháme `/projekty`, Přispět `/prispet` (`core/navigation/app-tabs.ts`, `APP_TABS`, `tabOf(url)` matches a tab's path and everything under it), each an inline SVG icon over its label, a filled glyph as iOS draws its tab bar (`fill="currentColor"`, no stroke; Jakub 2026-11-11), drawn for the site, never taken from SF Symbols: O nás the logo's star filled (`FOUNDATION_STAR` of `shared/ui/foundation-star/foundation-star.ts`, the one drawing the administration's section rows use too, in a square view box), Aktuality a page with three cut-out lines, Pomáháme a heart, Přispět a gift whose ribbon is the gap between its halves, all on a 24 grid at `--tab-icon-size` (`rem`, so it grows with the text); the current tab carries `aria-current="page"` at its root and `"true"` deeper in it, the pill (`--color-tab-pill`, its rim `--color-tab-pill-rim`: none in `nova-nadeje`, so the pill fills the bar up to `--tab-pill-inset` as an iOS tab bar does, and a tap just inside the bar's edge still lands on a tab), and a bold label, so it is told apart by the pill and the weight, not by colour alone; hidden (`visibility: hidden`, out of the accessibility tree, its room kept) while the on-screen keyboard is open (`SoftKeyboard.isOpen()`, #359), as the keyboard covers a native tab bar; fixed at the bottom, centred, at most `--tab-bar-max-width` wide, `--tab-bar-gap-browser` above the bottom in a browser and, in an installed app (`display-mode: standalone`), the larger of `--tab-bar-gap` and the safe area less `--tab-bar-home-indicator-overlap`; its columns are as wide as their labels need (a label breaks only between words), and when the bar is narrower than four labels need at the root text size (`bp.container-down(tab-bar, tabs)`, 13 rem) it lays the tabs out two by two, so at 200 % text it grows instead of breaking a word; `tab-bar-room.ts` (`keepRoomForTabBar`) writes the bar's measured height into `--tab-bar-rendered-height` on the root (a `ResizeObserver`; the latest bar shown wins, the theme's `--tab-bar-height` when none), which `--tab-bar-clearance` is computed from; the page keeps `--tab-bar-clearance` free under its content at every width (`.with-tab-bar` except on the landing page, the hero, the donor area's column, the administration's column while it shows the bar; `e2e/tab-bar-room.spec.ts`; a `.ui-panel-page` takes it into its own dark padding and gives it back with a negative margin, so no white band shows under it) and in `scroll-padding-bottom` (`styles.scss`, `html:has(app-tab-bar)`), and a floating notice and `.ui-action-bar` sit above the bar (`_notice.scss`, `_action-bar.scss`: `bottom: var(--tab-bar-clearance)` wherever the bar shows). The administration shows the bar only below `nav` (Jakub 2026-11-06, design plan J1: above it the side panel leads), and a route with `tabBar: false` hides it there too: the full-screen news and project editors, so their action bar stands at the bottom of the screen. A tab's link is the address it was last left at (`core/navigation/tab-memory.ts`, `TabMemory`: the last URL seen in each tab and the current tab, followed on every `NavigationEnd`), so a tab opens where the visitor left it, and the current tab pressed on a page deeper in it returns to its root, as an iOS tab bar pops its stack (`BackTarget.toTabRoot`: one step back in the history when the entry behind is that root, otherwise the root replaces the page, so Back never returns to the page left, #394); a plain click on the tab at its root scrolls to the top (smoothly, unless `prefers-reduced-motion`), modifier clicks open the link as a browser does. A finger can also slide along the bar, as on the tab bar of iOS 26, where a long press turns the selection into a lens that follows the finger across the tabs (`tab-bar/finger-across-tabs.ts`, `followFingerAcrossTabs`, started after the first render): the bar is `touch-action: none`, so the page never scrolls under the finger and the back swipe leaves it alone; a passive `pointerdown` of a touch or pen pointer on the bar (never a mouse) starts listening on the window to `pointermove`, `pointerup` and `pointercancel` of that pointer (the browser captures a touch pointer to its target by itself); a move of at most 10 px is still a tap, the link's click; beyond it the bar measures the tabs once and, in one `requestAnimationFrame` per frame, moves its pill (`.tab-bar-pill`, `aria-hidden`, under the list, the tab's size, through the `translate` property only, enlarged by `--tab-pill-drag-scale`) with the finger along the row of the tab under it, clamped to the row, while the current tab's own pill and colour give way and the tab under the finger (`tabUnderFinger`: the nearest tab to the finger held inside the bar, so beyond either end it is the end tab, also in the two-row layout at large text) takes `--color-tab-active`; letting go opens that tab where it was left (`TabMemory.urlFor`, through the router, so no browser entry is added), and the pill stands on it until the navigation ends (a refused navigation returns it); letting go over the tab already open, more than 24 px above the bar (the finger slid off it upwards) or on `pointercancel` opens nothing; a click arriving within 400 ms of a drag is swallowed. Under `prefers-reduced-motion` the pill jumps from tab centre to tab centre without the enlargement; under `forced-colors` it is a `Highlight` outline and the tab under the finger turns `Highlight`. The links, names and roles stay as they are, so keyboard and screen readers use the tabs as before, and no haptic tap is pretended (the web has none on iOS). `e2e/tab-bar-drag.spec.ts` drags in Chromium with real touch input (CDP), where it also proves the page does not scroll, and in WebKit with touch `PointerEvent`s. `core/navigation/page-transitions.ts` (`PageTransitions`, started by `pageTransitionsAtStart`; Jakub 2026-11-12, the design of the iOS UX designer and the iOS app developer): on a phone layout (`CompactLayout`) a page with Zpět slides in from the right over the page it was opened from, and leaves to the right over the page Zpět, a step back in the site's history or the swipe of the home-screen app returns to, which comes back from `--page-parallax` under a dim (`--color-page-dim`) that clears, as an iOS navigation stack pushes and pops; `page-transition-kind.ts` (`pageTransitionFor`) decides: `pop` for a `popstate` navigation or one `BackTarget` starts in place of a step back (`info: BACK_NAVIGATION`) from a page with a `parent`, `push` for any other navigation to a page with a `parent` within the same tab (or outside the tabs, as the account), nothing for the first page, a tab switch, a page without `parent`, a `replaceUrl`, a change of only the query or fragment, a dialog open or still sliding away (`dialog--leaving`), a wide layout or `prefers-reduced-motion`. Nothing that moves is live: when the guards let the navigation go (`GuardsCheckEnd`), a still copy of the page left (`still-copy.ts`: `cloneNode` of the shell host marked `page-layer`, without ids outside drawings, `name`, `autofocus`, `for`, `form`, frames and media, so it neither duplicates an id nor joins a radio group) covers the screen in an overlay (`aria-hidden`, `inert`, appended to `body`, `styles/_page-transition.scss`) at the page's scroll position, kept in its own scroller inside a box that moves, so its fixed and sticky parts sit where they did; the router then renders the next page beneath it, and after the router's `Scroll` (its scroll restored) and two frames a copy of that page joins the overlay and both copies move by CSS animations of `transform` and `opacity` only (their end is awaited after a style read, so WebKit lists them) (`--duration-page`, `--ease-page`, the leading edge's `--shadow-page-edge`), after which the overlay goes and the live page is there, its `h1` focused by the shell as before. Copies keep the tab bar only when one of the two pages lacks it; otherwise the overlay stays under the live tab bar, which does not move. Animations inside a copy end at once, a cancelled, failed or skipped navigation removes the overlay, and `REMOVE_STYLES_ON_COMPONENT_DESTROY` is off (`keptPageStyles` in `app.config.ts`), so a closed page's component styles stay for its copy. While a transition runs, its copies hold the same classes and texts as the live page, so an e2e locator by class or text at phone size scopes to `page.getByRole('main')` (the overlay is `aria-hidden`). `core/navigation/back-target.ts` (`BackTarget`, started at app start with `TabMemory` by `navigationAtStart` in `app.config.ts`): counts the app's own history depth in the history state (`nfnnAppDepth`, through `Location`, so it survives a reload: the first navigation reads it back; popstate takes the restored entry's depth, `replaceUrl` and `skipLocationChange` keep it, every other navigation adds one); each entry also keeps the path of the entry behind it (`nfnnAppBehind`); `back(parent)` goes back in the history while the app has an entry of its own behind and that entry is in the same tab (or either page is outside the tabs, as the donor area), and otherwise opens the route's `parent` with `replaceUrl`, so a page opened from a link outside never leaves the site on Zpět and a deep page a tab reopened never returns into another tab; `destination(parent)` names that choice before it is made (`{ kind: 'step', depth, path }` with the depth and path of the entry behind, or `{ kind: 'parent', path }`), and `back` acts on it, so whatever shows where Zpět leads (the page under the back gesture) shows the page it opens; `returningByButton` tells the shell that the history step came from Zpět, so the public shell focuses the new page's `h1` as after any app navigation (a browser Back keeps the browser's focus). `core/navigation/edge-swipe-back.ts` (`EdgeSwipeBack`, started by `edgeSwipeBackAtStart` in `app.config.ts`): only in the app opened from the home screen of an iPhone or iPad (`navigator.standalone` with a touch screen), a swipe to the right from anywhere on the screen, as in iOS 26, is one step back as in a native app (issue #431, Jakub 2026-11-06). Safari has nothing of the site to go back to there (the site keeps its own history), so the system offers no back swipe and no touch is cancelled: passive `touchstart`, `touchmove`, `touchend` and `touchcancel` on the document follow one touch by its identifier, and scrolling, taps and the edge work natively. Each touch is read by `core/navigation/back-swipe.ts` (`BackSwipe`, a plain class, Jakub 2026-11-12): one finger takes the page once it leaves the 10 px of a tap moving right and more across than down (1.5 : 1), and from then on only its sideways position counts (`offset`, never left of where it landed nor past the width); a finger that moves up or down first, moves left, or rests more than 500 ms before it moves (a long press that selects text) is no swipe. On release (`release`, a `SwipeSettle`) it projects where the page would come to rest, its offset plus the finger's last speed for 500 ms (a speed older than 100 ms counts as rest), and goes back when that is past half the screen's width, the page moved at least 40 px and the finger was not heading back left at 0.3 px/ms or faster; otherwise the page slides back. The settle runs from the offset to the width or to 0 in the remaining distance at the finger's speed, at least as fast as a whole width in 350 ms and within 150–350 ms, on a `cubic-bezier` whose start slope keeps the finger's speed. A touch that starts on a field (`input`, `select`, `textarea`, `contenteditable`), on an element with computed `touch-action: none` (the zoom viewer's stage, the sheet's grabber, the tab bar) or inside a box that scrolls sideways and has more to show (`overflow-x` `auto` or `scroll`, wider content: the year stories, the log lines) is left to it. Once the finger takes the page, on a phone layout without reduced motion, no open dialog and a page with a `parent`, `PageTransitions.startSwipe(BackTarget.destination(parent))` lays the page under the finger: a still copy of the live page moves with the finger (`follow`, `translateX` of the offset) over the page Zpět leads to, which moves from `--page-parallax` to its place under a dim that clears as the finger goes across; that page is the still copy `PageTransitions` kept when the visitor left it by a push (one per depth of the site's own history, at most three, `KEPT_PAGES`, in memory only, dropped from the depth left onwards), or, when none is kept (after a reload, or Zpět opens the `parent` in place of the page), the plain ground of the page. On release the copies settle by the `SwipeSettle` through Web Animations (`settle`), and only after a settle that completes does the step back below follow; a settle back, a `touchcancel` or a `popstate` during the swipe slides the page back and nothing else happens; a page with unsaved input (`DirtyForms.any()`) slides back first and the step back then asks as Zpět does. After a completed settle the overlay stays, the destination's copy in place, while the router opens the page beneath it (no second slide), and goes after the router's `Scroll` and two frames, or after 5 s at most. A step back is then what Zpět does: with a `dialog` open, a step back in the history (`DialogHistory` closes the top dialog or its last step; the sign-in dialog's steps are kept by `LoginDialogService`), except while a navigation waits (`Router.currentNavigation()`: the unsaved-changes dialog of a guard), when the last open dialog gets a `cancel` and closes unless it refuses, as with Esc; without a dialog `BackTarget.back(parent)` with the route's `parent` (so it never leaves the tab's stack and the shell focuses the new page's `h1`), and nothing on a tab root. A `popstate` or `pagehide` between the touch's start and end means the app already went back, so no second step follows. In a Safari tab, Chrome on iOS, Android and on a computer it is off: the swipe and the browser's Back belong to the browser there and leave the site. Playwright's WebKit reports `maxTouchPoints` 0 and cannot perform the system gesture, so the swipe and Safari's behaviour are checked on the iOS simulator and the device (#431). `core/navigation/route-chrome.ts` (`RouteChrome.current`) reads the deepest route's data: `parent` (inherited; the article and project pages name their list, the privacy policy `/o-nas`, `/darce` and `/overit` `/`, so the verification page opened from Potvrzení has Zpět back to it), `head` (`photo`, `dark` by default, `light` for `/o-nas`, `/prispet` and `/darce`), `tabBar` (false for `/pripojeni`, `/prvni-nastaveni` and the administration's news and project editors), `landing` (`/`, whose route runs its guard on every navigation, so an in-app link to an old `/#…` address redirects too), `phoneFooter` (`/o-nas`), `backOnWide` (false for `/darce` and `/sprava`). `index.html` sets `viewport-fit=cover`, so the safe areas are the page's to keep (the header, the tab bar and `.ui-container`'s side padding take `env(safe-area-inset-*)`, the top through `--safe-area-top`, above). An app opened from the home screen of an iPad in a window smaller than the screen (iPadOS 26 and later, "Windowed Apps", Stage Manager, a tiled window) has the system's window controls floating over its top-leading corner, and WebKit tells the page nothing about them (measured on iPadOS 27.2: every `env(safe-area-inset-*)` but the bottom is 0 in a window, and there is no `titlebar-area-*` or `navigator.windowControlsOverlay`), so `core/layout/window-controls.ts` (`WindowControls.start()` from the app initializer `windowControlsAtStart`) marks the root `has-window-controls` while `navigator.standalone` is `true` on a touch screen that is no iPhone (a Mac's Dock app has no touch, a Safari window keeps the controls in its own toolbar) and the window (`outerWidth` × `outerHeight`) is smaller than the screen, following `resize`; a maximized window shows no controls over the page (its top lies under the status bar) and has the screen's area, in either orientation. `styles.scss` then sets `--window-controls-inset-inline` and `-block` (`0px` in the theme) to the theme's `--window-controls-room-inline` and `-block` (74 px and 52 px in `nova-nadeje`: the collapsed controls end about 62 px from the window's leading edge and 41 px from its top), and, as iPadOS moves a navigation bar's leading items past the controls rather than below them, the header's leading padding, the back button and the skip link keep at least the inline room, and the donor area's sticky side panel keeps its first row below the block room; the controls expanded by a tap are a passing overlay and get no room, and nothing else needs it (a full-screen dialog starts its content 56 px down and closes at the top-right, a sheet leaves room above it). `site-footer/`: on the dark footer the logo with `info@nfnn.cz` under it and a `nav` "Odkazy v patičce": on wide screens O nás, Aktuality, Pomáháme and Přispět (each with `aria-current="page"` and `.is-current` on its own tab), at every width "Kontakt" (`/o-nas#kontakt`), then the bottom bar with the copyright, the registry line and the privacy link; while any part of it is on the screen it marks the root `footer-in-view` (an `IntersectionObserver`, dropped with the footer), which gives `body` the footer's colour (`styles.scss`, `html.footer-in-view body`, beside the landing and 404 canvas rules), because Safari paints the strip under its floating toolbar at the end of a page with the body's background, and the shell's `main` keeps the white surface itself on a page with the footer, so only that strip turns dark. `public-shell/` renders the footer on wide screens on every page but the landing page, and on phones only on the About page, where the tabs end (`phoneFooter`). `public-shell/` asks `SetupRedirect.checkOnce()` (`features/operations/setup/setup-redirect.ts`) once per page load: while `GET /api/operations/setup` says `required`, it navigates to `/prvni-nastaveni`; a failure is ignored, so a running site never depends on it. Both shells start with the skip link "Přeskočit na obsah" (global `.skip-link`, fixed top-left, shown on keyboard focus) to `main#obsah`: in `public-shell/` the `main` around the pages, in `donor-shell/` (`/darce`, `/sprava`) the content column: in the donor area that of `side-nav` at every width (one `side-nav` whose `panel` input drops the panel on phones, so the routed page stays the same component when the window crosses `nav`), in the administration likewise (the panel only above `nav`, 2.24). `public-shell/` also follows navigation for keyboard and screen-reader users: after a route change (not the first load, not back/forward, not while a dialog is open) it focuses the page `h1` unless the page already moved the focus into `main`, and after a link to a section (`/o-nas#kontakt`, also when clicked again) it focuses that section; both get `tabindex="-1"` and no ring.

Switching tabs (Jakub 2026-11-05: switching between the tabs blinked, as if each page loaded only after the tap): a tab opens at once, with its last content, like a tab of a native app. Three pieces do it. `core/navigation/tab-preloading.ts` (`TabPagesPreloading`, `withPreloading` in `app.config.ts`) loads ahead, once the first page has opened, exactly the routes whose `data` carries `LOADED_AHEAD`: the public shell, the content routes and the pages of the four tabs (a failure is swallowed, and the page then loads when it is opened, through the chunk-load recovery as before); every other page stays lazy, and the route names alone decide nothing, since the administration has an `aktuality` of its own. `core/navigation/kept-public-answers.ts` (`KeptPublicAnswers`, `keptPublicAnswersInterceptor`, the first interceptor of `appHttpClient`) keeps in memory the last answer of each GET of the public content (`/api/content/home`, the news and project lists by their query, a news or project page), at most 50 (`KEPT_ANSWERS_LIMIT`, the oldest forgotten first): a page that asks for one it already had within 10 minutes (`KEPT_ANSWER_MAX_AGE_MS`, by `performance.now()`) gets a copy at once (`structuredClone`, so a page that changes its answer changes no other's) and the server is asked again in the background, once per address at a time, so the next visit shows what the server answers now; an older answer, an address never asked for and a failed answer go to the server as before (the first visit of a tab still shows its loading line once), a background answer that fails keeps the kept one and a 404 forgets it, and any write under `/api/admin/content/` forgets them all, so an administrator sees a change at once. A copy served from memory is recorded in `ShownAnswers` (`core/connectivity/`, slice 2.7) with the `Date` of the answer it keeps, since it never reaches `connectivityInterceptor`, so the offline pill tells the true age of what a returning tab shows, and the background answer, once it arrives, records its own `Date` over it. It keeps only paths of the service worker's same-for-everyone list (`service-worker-bypass.ts`, pinned by its spec), nothing a donor or an administrator alone receives, and nothing outlives the tab. `StatsCountUp` (`home/sections/stats-bar/`) lets the figures on O nás count up only the first time they come into view in a tab; afterwards they stand at their values, so a return to O nás does not replay the animation. `e2e/tab-switching.spec.ts` checks that a tab seen before shows its content while its new request is still held, and that the first switch to each tab after O nás loads no script.

List rows of plan v3 2.25: `CompactLayout.listsAsRows()` (`core/layout/compact-layout.ts`) is true below `nav` and also wherever the window is narrower than `nav` measured in the root text size (`innerWidth <= 899 × root font size / 16`, so an iPad on its side at 200 % text, 1194 px wide with a 32 px root, counts as narrow), following the media query, `resize` and a `ResizeObserver` on a hidden `1rem` probe, so enlarging the text switches at once; at the default text size on a wide screen it is false and every table stays as it was, pixel for pixel. Where it is true the lists render as `ui-list` rows instead of tables (one layout chosen by `@if`). A row (`src/styles/ui/_list.scss`) stands its lead icon, value and chevron in the middle of its height, also when its title wraps or the text is enlarged: only a row with a `__state` and no `__meta` lets its title span both grid rows, so a row with neither has one grid row and no hidden second one that held the icon and the arrow above the middle (Jakub 2026-11-06). The lists: the administration's payments (`/sprava/platby`) as rows grouped by month under a `h3.ui-list__group-title` ("Říjen 2026") that sticks under the header's back button (`--list-group-title-top`, the header's height in the administration), each row a `button.payment-row` (`aria-haspopup="dialog"`) with the sender (or "Bez jména odesílatele"), the date, "VS …" or "bez VS" and, when paired, "dárce …" (or "dárce bez záznamu"), the amount and at most one state badge (vráceno, jen ručně, Ověření účtu); a waiting payment's row opens the pairing dialog, a paired one's opens the sheet "Přiřazená platba" (`presentation="sheet"`) with the payment, the sender, the symbol and message, the donor with a link to the card, the method and tags, and "Zrušit přiřazení" with its inline confirmation (or the line that an issued receipt blocks it); the sheet closes after an unpairing, a refused one keeps it open with the reason inside it, its donor link hands the sheet's history entry over to the card (`DialogHistory.handOver`), so one Back returns to the payments, and a live change waits while it is open; the filter stacks wherever the list is rows; the donors (`/sprava/darci`) as link rows to the card with the name, "VS …" · e-mail (or "vymazán") · IČO and only the tags that say something (no "běžný účet"); the history (`/sprava/historie`) as rows with the action, the time, the administrator (or server, or "správce bez účtu") and the application, then the server's sentence, the donor card link and the collapsed technical details (with the administrator's e-mail); the donor card (`/sprava/darci/:id`) as the summary over an inset list of its parts (Údaje, Přihlášení, Bankovní účty "1 účet / n účty / n účtů", Dary "n darů za … Kč", Potvrzení "1 vydané potvrzení / n vydaná / n vydaných potvrzení", Výmaz údajů; a part whose data failed says "Nepodařilo se načíst, otevřete a zkuste znovu", one still loading names no count), each part a page of its own at `/sprava/darci/:id/<part>` (`udaje`, `prihlaseni`, `bankovni-ucty`, `dary`, `potvrzeni`, `vymaz`; an unknown part opens the card) with only that card under the name, the header's back button leading to the card (the route's `parent` is `/sprava/darci/:id`, and `RouteChrome` fills in a parent's `:name` segments from the route's parameters) and, where the header shows no back button, the link "← <name>", its tables stacked as cards (`.ui-table--stacked`), and while its data loads or after it failed the part shows the wait or the failure with a retry under its own title; the Dary part shows the gifts as rows under a heading per year ("2026 · 2 dary · 800 Kč", refunds left out of the sum) with the date, the purpose and pairing, the sender account and symbol, the amount, the refund pill and the receipt number (or "Potvrzení zatím žádné"); on a wide screen the same address shows the whole card and focuses that part; and the donor's gifts (Moje dary and the overview) as rows per year with the date, the purpose and pairing tag, the amount, the refund pill and the receipt download under them. The `ui-list` row is a grid of named areas (`lead title value chevron` over `lead meta value chevron`, one line when a row has no meta), `ui-list__state` sits under the value, `ui-list__extra` spans the row under the title, and rows inside a `ui-card` take the card's padding.

## content

- The pages of the tabs (2.23) are built from `home/sections/`: `landing/` (`/`, `Landing`) shows only `hero` (full-bleed photo under the header, darkened at its top by `--hero-top-scrim` over the 160 px shade, so the white logo stays legible over the light wall, at least `100svh` tall and taller only when its text needs it, so the page fits the visible screen without scrolling in Safari, whose `100vh` is its larger viewport with the toolbar retracted, and in the installed app; copy, the CTA "Přispět" to `/prispet` and "Kde pomáháme" to `/projekty` only while `CompactLayout.isCompact()` and `ShortViewport.isShort()` are both false, because on phones and tablets, upright or on their side, the tab bar right under them offers both (Jakub 2026-11-05); the copy keeps the header's room above it, the safe-area insets beside it and room for the tab bar under it; up to the height `hero-lead` (680 px, an iPhone SE) the lead is only visually hidden and the screen reader still reads it, so the copy stays under the child's face (Jakub on #371), and up to `short` (500 px, a phone on its side) the headline takes `--text-hero-short`, capped by the width and the height, so it stays in the left part of the photo beside the face; no footer; the root and the body behind it are `--color-image-shade` (`styles.scss`, `html:has(app-landing), html:has(app-landing) body`, the same rule as the 404 page's dark canvas on phones), because Safari paints the strip under its floating toolbar with the body's background, so that strip continues the photo's dark bottom instead of white; the `html` rule also gives the root `overscroll-behavior: none`, so pulling past the top or bottom no longer drags the photo away from the page, as the rubber band of Safari and the installed app on an iPhone did), `about-page/` (`/o-nas`, "O nás | …", `AboutPage`) asks `GET /api/content/home` once (`HomeApi`) and shows `about` (with its `h1` "Vznik a poslání") with the photo and the people from that answer, `stats-bar` and the contact form, then the footer on every width, `donate-page/` (`/prispet`, "Přispět | …", `DonatePage`) shows `donate-cta` (its `h1` "Přispějte na pomůcky pro děti") with the signed-in donor's name and symbol from `AuthStore`; news are the Aktuality tab (`news-page/`) and projects the list of the Pomáháme tab (`list/`). The sections: `stats-bar` (years counted from the registry entry of 28 October 2005 on the visitor's day; the figures wait hidden at zero until 40 % of the bar is in view (`IntersectionObserver`), then fade in one after another and count up to their values in 1.6 s, the year 2005 only fades in; a screen reader reads the final figures all along from a visually hidden copy, and with `prefers-reduced-motion` or without `IntersectionObserver` they stand at their values at once), `about` (what the foundation does first, then the work with FOD, then its founding; beside the text the photo an administrator set at `/sprava/o-nas` (input `photo`, `about.photo` of the home content, Jakub 2026-10-30) as a `figure` with the whole photo (`?w=480` with a 480/960/1 440 px `srcset` never wider than the original, `sizes` the column, `loading="lazy"`, `width`/`height` from the API, `alt` from the API or empty; the figure narrows to keep an upright photo at most 640 px tall, never cropping it, and its shape is the `--about-photo-ratio` the template binds) with the FOD badge over its lower corner and the caption under it in a `figcaption`, where the badge never reaches; up to 768 px the photo goes under the text and the badge is hidden; without a photo, or when the home content fails, the stock photo with the badge as before; under both columns, after a hairline, the block "Kdo fond vede" (Jakub 30. 10. 2026, after a sponsor in the user tests could not find who runs the foundation today): the eyebrow `h3` "Kdo fond vede", then the two founders as a list (`.board-people`, two columns where each gets at least 28 rem, otherwise one under the other): each a round portrait (`app-avatar` with `/images/pavel-znamenacek-` and `/images/andrea-znamenackova-160.webp` and `-320.webp`, 160 px, 96 px up to 480 px, lazy) beside the name and the role ("Zakladatel a předseda správní rady", "Zakladatelka a členka správní rady"; Andrea added on Jakub's word of 30. 10. 2026, both approved their portraits), and under them the board as in the registry (not the auditor, Jakub 2026-10-30), the names held together by no-break spaces; that built-in block stays while the home content carries no `people` (input `people`, `PublicPeopleResponse` in `features/content/people-api.ts`, Jakub 2026-10-30). With people from `/sprava/lide-fondu` the same classes show the featured people as cards (`app-avatar`: the portrait `?w=480` at 160 px in a circle, or the initials without one, beside the name and the role), then the founders' note (`.founders-note`, at most 44 rem wide, one `p` per paragraph) with the web signatures of the featured people under it (`.signatures`, a list left-aligned and aligned to the bottom so the names under them line up: each an `img` of the uploaded image as it is, in its own ink colour, `?w=480`, about 44 px tall and 34 px up to 480 px, `alt=""` because the name is printed under it in small text), then the board line "Správní rada: …" built by `boardLine()` with no-break spaces inside each name and the note in parentheses, and the line under the board (`people.footnote`, `.board-footnote`, the same muted text close under it)), `donate-cta` (the eyebrow, title and lead, then `app-payment-panel` with the line before paying and the `h3` "Zaplaťte" projected between the amount and the bank details, then "Co bude dál"), in one order for every width (Jakub 2026-10-24, the audit's option B1; the decorative photo with its quote only above 768 px and taller than `short` (500 px), so a phone on its side keeps the phone's one column, the dark quote card at the photo's lower right, over the dress and clear of the child's face, at the top of its column and sticky under the header): (1) eyebrow "Přispět", heading "Přispějte na pomůcky pro děti" and one sentence why; (2) "Zvolte částku": preset or custom amount (500 Kč preselected; "Jiná částka" opens an empty field, so the QR says "Nejprve zadejte částku" until an amount is typed; whole crowns with spaces, hint after leaving the field); (3) `.before-pay`, the line before paying: signed out "Chcete potvrzení o daru pro daňové přiznání? Přihlaste se nebo si založte účet ještě před platbou: …", then "Potvrzení vystavíme i na firmu: při registraci zvolte Firma a vyplňte IČO." (`.company-hint`), with "Založit účet" and "Přihlásit se" (they open the dialog; after a sign-in from here the page stays and the focus moves to the "Bankovní spojení" label, now with the donor's symbol), signed in "Platíte jako <name>, variabilní symbol <VS>." (input `donorName` from `AuthStore.donor().name`, shown alone); (4) `h3` "Zaplaťte": the bank details (account, amount, the signed-in donor's variable symbol, IBAN, each with a copy button), for a signed-out visitor right under them an amber callout "Potvrzení o daru k platbě bez přihlášení vystavíme, až si založíte účet a ověříte v něm bankovní účet, ze kterého jste platili." whose "založíte účet" opens the registration like "Založit účet" (testers of 2026-10-30 paid by QR without reading the line before paying), then the QR that `spayd()` and `encodeText` (level H) draw in the browser from `PaymentDetailsStore`, by `app-qr-code` at 200 px (nothing is asked after the details: a preset redraws at once; typed digits and "Jiná částka" dim the QR at once (`.qr-image-stale`, `aria-busy`, "Uložit QR kód" disabled) and redraw it 400 ms after the last key, plan D30, so the morph runs once for the finished amount and a new one interrupts a running one; a visually hidden hint on the field says so; an amount that is not valid shows "Nejprve zadejte částku" only after those 400 ms; when the details fail or `spayd()` refuses, the QR's alert with "Zkusit znovu" loads the details again; after the visitor changes the amount a visually hidden `role="status"` line says "QR platba na 1 000 Kč je připravená." once, after 1.2 s without a change, and only when it names another amount than the last one) with its text and "Uložit QR kód" (kept in place and disabled while the QR waits to be redrawn; saves a 720 px PNG made in the browser, `qr-platba-nova-nadeje.png`, "Připravujeme obrázek…" while it runs, then the always rendered `role="status"` line `.qr-saved` names the file and says it is in the downloaded files, until the amount changes); up to 480 px the QR sits above its text; the DOM order is the visual order, so Tab reaches the copy buttons before the QR; (5) `h3` "Co bude dál": signed in, the gift shows in the overview within an hour of the bank booking it (another bank's transfer usually arrives by the next working day) and the yearly receipt is downloaded in the donor account after the year ends; signed out, how an earlier gift paid without signing in is paired once the donor sends one with the symbol from the same account, and that the yearly receipt comes with a donor account; both end with the sentence that the gift can be deducted from the tax base under the income tax act.
- `detail/` (`content-detail`, both `/aktuality/:slug` and `/projekty/:slug`): loads the article or project by its slug (`NewsApi.publishedBySlug`, `ProjectsApi.publishedBySlug`; when it answers with another slug, a former one, the page navigates to the current address with `replaceUrl`, so the canonical link names it); "Načítáme stránku…" (`role="status"`), a failure as `role="alert"` "Stránku se nepodařilo načíst." with "Zkusit znovu", a 404 (`content/news-not-found`, `content/project-not-found`) renders `not-found` at the same address with `[missing]` set to the kind ("Tato aktualita už na webu není." / "Tento příběh už na webu není.", "Všechny aktuality" / "Kde všude pomáháme" and "Zpět na úvodní stránku") and the tab title "Stránka nenalezena | …"; when loaded, the dark intro with "← Všechny aktuality" / "← Kde pomáháme" (`/aktuality`, `/projekty`), the eyebrow "<tag> · <date>" or "<tag> · <year>", the focused `h1`, the perex as lead, then the photo (`?w=960`, `srcset` 480/960/1440, `alt=""`) and the body rendered by `<app-markdown>` (plain text becomes paragraphs at empty lines, with single line breaks kept), the photo's `alt` from `coverImage.alt` (empty when absent), under a signed news article its signer (`.signer`: `<app-avatar>` of 64 px from the portrait's `?w=480` copy or the initials, the name and the role), then `<app-share-button>` with the title and the canonical address (`canonicalAddress()` of `core/seo/canonical-link.ts`, so `https://nfnn.cz/aktuality/<slug>` or `/projekty/<slug>` whichever host serves the page; not in the preview), and the line "Chcete pomoct dalším dětem? Přispějte na jejich pomůcky." over "Přispět" (`/prispet`) with "Zpět na aktuality" / "Kde všude pomáháme"; the header's "Zpět" leads to the list (`parent`). The loaded page is `detail/content-article` (`app-content-article [page] [kind] [preview]`, the `ContentPage` built by `newsPage()` / `projectPage()` in `detail/content-page.ts`, which also holds `ContentKind`), so the administration's preview shows the same page: with `preview` the title is an `h3` instead of the focused `h1` and the dark intro drops the room for the fixed header; `content-detail` focuses the heading through `focusHeading()`. Texts under `content.detail.*`; specs `content-detail.spec.ts` (Markdown and the alt included) and `content-article.spec.ts`.
- `news-page/` (`NewsPage`, `/aktuality`, v3 2.26 "Novinky", after Jakub's mockups of 2026-10-31): the light intro (`.ui-intro--light`) with the eyebrow "Aktuality" (hidden up to 480 px), the focused `h1` "Co je u nás nového" and a lead, then a page of `PAGE_SIZE` = 10 from `NewsApi.published` (`?strana=`, 1-based, anything else is page 1; a newer page change cancels the answer still on its way). Page 1 waits for the news and the stories together, so nothing moves under the visitor once it shows: the projects (`ProjectsApi.published`, pages of 50 one after another, at most `MAX_PROJECT_PAGES` = 20, each project once by id) load once per visit to the page and are kept for a return to page 1 and for a retry; their failure, or no answer within `STORIES_WAIT_MS` = 4 s, only leaves the stories out. On page 1 the newest news leads as `featured-news/` (`app-featured-news [item]`): its photo across the card (`?w=960` with the 480/960/1 440 px `srcset` the server keeps, `focalShape` `newsFeatured`, `fetchpriority="high"`) under a shade, the tag and date, the title as an `h2`, a perex of three lines (two up to 480 px), the signer (`app-avatar` of 40 px from `?w=480`, the name and, from 481 px, the role) and the actions, which wrap to the right under the signer when the text is large: `<app-share-button tone="dark" [subject]>` and the link "Číst celé" (its accessible name adds the title; it covers the whole card and shows only a round arrow up to 480 px). Then `year-stories/` (`app-year-stories [stories]`, `storiesOfNewestYear()`): "Příběhy z roku N" (each strip names its section by a heading id of its own) with the projects of the newest year in the site's order as a strip that scrolls sideways (each tile a 72 px photo `?w=480`, `focalShape` `storyTile`, or a heart, the tag and the title linking to the project; columns at least 16 rem wide, so at 200 % text a tile keeps its words, four in a row on a computer and 78 % tiles up to 768 px) and the link "Všechny N v Pomáháme" (ICU plural, "Zobrazit v Pomáháme" for one) to `/projekty?rok=N`, which the Pomáháme page of 2.23a reads. Then "Starší zprávy" (on a later page only the visually hidden "Zprávy na straně N", with no leading news and no strip) as `app-news-card`s in an auto-filling grid and the `nav` "Stránky aktualit" with "Novější", "Strana N z M" and "Starší" as the shared pager (`_pager`). The always rendered `role="status"` line says "Načítáme aktuality…", "Zatím tu žádná aktualita není." with "Zpět na úvodní stránku" or "Tato strana neexistuje." with "Na první stranu"; a failure is the `role="alert"` "Aktuality se nepodařilo načíst." or, while `Connectivity.isOffline()`, "Bez připojení se aktuality nenačetly. Až budete online, zkuste to znovu.", in the danger colour, each with "Zkusit znovu", after which the focus goes to the `h1` or, when it fails again, back to the button. The news card (`news-card/`) shows under its perex the signer (`app-avatar` of 40 px from `?w=480`) and, when linked, Sdílet with the article as its `subject`; from a card 480 px wide (the container `news-card`) its photo stands beside the text (`newsCardSide`). The closing Přispět band of the mockups comes with 2.23a's `<app-donate-band>`. Texts under `content.news.*`; spec `news-page.spec.ts`; Playwright `e2e/content-pages.spec.ts` (the leading news, the stories, paging by ten, sharing and copying from the feed, the offline failure and the retry), the axe cases (with a signer and the failure) and the device step `news-feed`.
- `list/` (`content-list`, `/projekty`): the dark intro with the focused `h1` "Kde pomáháme" and a lead, then the page of `PAGE_SIZE` = 10 from `ProjectsApi.published`; the page is `?strana=` (1-based, anything else is page 1) and changes reload the list in place. A `role="status"` line that is always rendered says "Načítáme příběhy…" and, when nothing is published, "Zatím tu žádný příběh není." with "Zpět na úvodní stránku", and a page past the last one says "Tato strana neexistuje." with "Na první stranu"; a failure is the `role="alert"` "Seznam se nepodařilo načíst." with "Zkusit znovu".
