The cards are `app-project-card` in an auto-filling grid (it and `news-card/`, a published article also in the administration's preview, take `[linked]`, true by default; false shows the title without its link, for the administration's preview; specs `news-card.spec.ts`, `project-card.spec.ts`), and when there is more than one page the `nav` "Stránky příběhů" offers "Novější", "Strana N z M" and "Starší" as the shared pager (`_pager`). Texts under `content.list.*`; spec `content-list.spec.ts`; Playwright `e2e/content-pages.spec.ts` (paging by ten with the address, and the pager of both lists in one row with its position in the middle from 320 to 1280 px and at 200 % text), the device step `projects-page` and the axe case for a middle page and the empty projects list.
- `privacy-policy/` ("Zásady ochrany osobních údajů"; "Jaké e‑maily posíláme" lists the e-mails a donor gets (the templates in `backend/src/main/resources/templates/email/donors/`: confirmation and one reminder, password reset, e-mail change, security notices, an address used by another registration or account, erasure), says receipts, newsletters and per-gift e-mails are never sent and that these e-mails cannot be turned off; "Jak dlouho údaje uchováváme" says an unconfirmed registration is deleted with all its data 30 days after it was made (#455); "Vaše práva" says what an erasure deletes and what stays: the issued receipts unchanged on the server with the name and address (and a company's identification number) on them, the payment records with the bank statement's account number and payer name, and the record of the administrators' actions (A6; nothing about devices until Phase 2 brings copies onto them); under the intro a `nav` "Obsah" links every part, whose `section`s carry the ids `spravce`, `udaje`, `emaily`, `uchovavani`, `prijemci`, `cookies`, `prava`, `kontakt`, `ucinnost`; "Evidence darů a potvrzení o daru" also says that `/overit` checks the signature in the browser and sends only the SHA-256 digest of the signed data for the status, and that sharing a verification link shares the receipt data, S-L7; "Hlášení chyb z prohlížeče" says what an error report holds and never holds, that it carries no account or IP address but could be matched by its time to other server records, which happens only when looking for a fault, that it is kept at most 14 days in the application log and in the container log until that is deleted by volume, and the limits name the 30 reports per 15 minutes and IPv6 counted by its /64), `not-found/` ("Tuhle stránku jsme nenašli", links home and to the contact form; up to the height `hero-lead` (680 px, an iPhone SE in Safari or as an app) its band starts closer under the header and its buttons sit closer, so both show above the tab bar at load (#358); with the input `missing` (`news` / `project`) it says the article or project is gone and leads to its list first; `NOT_FOUND_TITLE` is the tab title, `<meta name="robots" content="noindex">` while shown, because nginx answers an unknown slug of a detail route with 200 and cannot know it is missing; below the `nav` breakpoint, where the page shows no footer, `styles.scss` gives the canvas and `body` the hero's `--color-surface-inverse` while `app-not-found` is shown, so the dark page continues under the tab bar and Safari's toolbar instead of ending in a white band).

## contact

`contact-api.ts` (`POST /api/contact/messages`); `contact-form/` with honeypot, 429 and generic errors (both end with a `mailto:` link to info@nfnn.cz), a request that reached no server (status 0) "Bez připojení se zprávu nepodařilo odeslat. Zpráva vám zůstala; až se připojíte, odešlete ji znovu." (2.7; the send button carries `appNeedsConnection`), "Odesíláme…" while sending, no example placeholders in the name and e-mail fields, the e-mail hint "Odpovíme vám na zadaný e-mail." (the site names no reply time, and the sender gets no confirmation e-mail on purpose), a client check of the backend's address shape (`pattern`, a dot in the domain); a signed-in donor finds the e-mail, and for a person also the name, filled in from `AuthStore.donor()` (only empty fields, also when the session arrives later and again after "Odeslat další zprávu"), editable; a 400 with `errors[]` puts each message at its field through `placeServerErrors` (the first refused field takes the focus) (the e-mail field shows its own "Zadejte e-mail ve tvaru jan@example.com." for any server refusal) instead of the general failure; the thank-you ("Zpráva odeslána!", "Odpovíme vám e-mailem na <address>.") fills an always rendered `role="status"` region and its heading takes the focus, "Odeslat další zprávu" brings back an empty form (prefilled again for a signed-in donor) with the focus in the name field; and a line under the button that the data serves only to handle the message, linking the policy (legitimate interest, no consent wording). Beside the heading only the e-mail stays; the seat ("Sídlo", no longer "Adresa") and the IČO moved into `accounting-details/` (`app-accounting-details`, Jakub 30. 10. 2026), a `ui-card` placed under the e-mail in the left column on wide screens and after the form below 768 px (grid areas, so the reading and Tab order is heading, form, details everywhere): "Údaje pro účetní" as a `dl.ui-data-list` with Název, IČO, Sídlo, Zápis (the registry entry), Číslo účtu and IBAN (from `PaymentDetailsStore` once it holds the payment details, the IBAN through `formatIban`; until the account is known the account row says "Najdete ho v sekci Přispět" linking `/#prispet` and the IBAN row is left out), a `copy-button` for the IČO, the seat, the account and the IBAN, and the line "Potvrzení o daru na firmu získáte, když si při registraci zvolíte Firma a vyplníte IČO."; each row puts its label beside the value while the card is wide enough and above it otherwise (flex-wrap, no breakpoint), and a copy button sits right of its value or, in a narrow card, under it. Texts under `home.contact.accounting.*`, spec `accounting-details.spec.ts`.

## donations (services and helpers)

- `payment-details-api.ts`: `GET /api/donations/payment-details` without the access token (`PaymentDetails`: `account` `{ number, iban, recipientName }`, `currency`, `messages` `{ donation, accountVerification }`, `accountVerificationAmountMinor`; `recipientName` is the SPAYD text for `RN`, never shown). The answer passes a shape check and the account check (`paymentDetailsOf`: the IBAN must be the IBAN of the account number, `czech-account.ts`), so a malformed 200 or an account that disagrees with its IBAN becomes a failed load instead of breaking a panel or showing that account. `payment-details-store.ts` (`PaymentDetailsStore`, root): `load()` asks once per page load (again after a failure), `state` is `loading`, `ready` with the details or `failed`; every answer is kept with its `Date` in `localStorage` under `nfnn.payment-details` (`KEPT_PAYMENT_DETAILS`, `{ details, writtenAt }`), and a load that reached no server (`isUnreachable`: status 0, or a gateway status without a problem body) shows the kept details instead (read back through `paymentDetailsOf`, so a changed or broken entry is refused), while an answer of the server that fails the check or is a problem shows none, so a changed account never brings back the old one, with `age` (`{ writtenAt, kept }`) telling the panel how old they are (plan v3 2.7); the Přispět panel, the standing order guide and the add-account dialog share it, so a session asks once. Once the held details are five minutes old (the answer's `max-age`), the next `load()` or the page becoming visible again (`visibilitychange`) asks again with the request header `Cache-Control: no-cache` (the XHR backend ignores Angular's `cache` option, which is set too), keeping the details meanwhile and on a failure, and replaces them only when they changed (an unchanged answer is a 304 from the ETag); `reload()`, behind every "Zkusit znovu", shows the wait and asks the same way. `iban-format.ts` `formatIban` writes an IBAN in groups of four for the panel and the contact section (the panel shows it through `ibanHalves` as two halves of whole groups that do not break inside, so a narrow column breaks it only in its middle, "CZ26 2010 0000" over "0055 4433 2217", never leaving one group alone, while the copy button copies the IBAN as one value), whose "Údaje pro účetní" (`contact/accounting-details`) shows the foundation's account from `PaymentDetailsStore` once the details are held, without asking for them itself.
- `czech-banks.ts`: `bankNameOf(account)` → the short name of the bank of a Czech account (`Česká spořitelna` for `…/0800`) or `null` when the account is not Czech (`bankCodeOf` of `czech-account.ts`, the account's shape only, no modulo 11, since it only names) or its code is not listed; `CZECH_BANK_CODES` holds the `source` (the ČNB list "Kódy bank ČR", `kody_bank_CR.csv`), the day it was fetched (`fetchedOn`, 2026-11-06, the file last changed at the ČNB on 2026-09-25) and the names, shortened by hand without the legal form (`a.s.`, `pobočka`, `S.A.` …; the spec refuses one). These hand-made names are preferred and need no request; to refresh them: download the CSV again, add or drop codes, shorten new names the same way and set `fetchedOn`. `shortBankName(name)` shortens a name as the ČNB writes it (cut at the first comma or a branch marker such as `pobočka`, `organizační složka`, `Filiale`, `Prague Branch`, `v likvidaci`, then trailing legal forms such as `a.s.`, `s.r.o.`, `S.A.`, `SA`, `AG`, `GmbH`, `Co.`, `Ltd.`, `spořitelní družstvo`, case-sensitive; `testing/cnb-bank-names.ts` holds the 47 names of the list fetched 2026-11-06 and the spec checks that none keeps a legal form; the name as written when nothing would be left). `bank-names.ts` (`BankNames`, root): `nameOf(account)` gives the hand-made name, else the server's name of the code shortened, else `null`; `require(account)` asks `GET /api/donations/bank-codes` (`bank-codes-api.ts`) only for a Czech account whose code has no hand-made name and only while `Connectivity` does not know the app is offline, so the usual accounts cause no request; at most one request is in flight, a list once held is kept for the page load, and a failed answer or an empty list (a server that has not downloaded it yet) leaves only the hand-made names until the next `require` (a page or a list that shows such an account again) asks once more. The answer's shape is checked and each name shortened once when it arrives. Only the browser shortens and shows bank names, so there is no shared case (the server keeps the ČNB's names as written, contract "Bank codes"; the e-mails and the connector show the number alone). Logos (contract "Bank logos"): `require(account)` of a Czech account also asks once per page load for `GET /api/donations/bank-logos` (`bank-logos-api.ts`, `BankLogosApi`, public, without the access token, `WITHOUT_ACCESS_TOKEN`), never while `Connectivity` knows the app is offline (the next `require` online asks); the answer is kept whole, an empty list included; a failure without an answer (status 0, a lost connection) is asked again at the next `require`, which `app-bank-logo`'s effect repeats when `Connectivity` is online again, while a 429 or another refusal is not asked again until the next page load, so the accounts keep their monograms; `forgetLogos()`, which the administration's Loga bank calls after each saved, downloaded or removed logo, drops the list (an answer still on its way is ignored) so the next `require` asks again; only items with a four-digit code and a UUID image id count. `logoOf(account)` gives `/api/donations/bank-logos/images/<imageId>` (`bankLogoImageUrl`) or `null`. `bank-monogram.ts` (`bankMonogram(shortName)`): an abbreviation of two to four capitals (digits and `&` allowed) leading the name stays whole (`ČSOB`, `PPF`, `J&T`), else the first letters of the first two words (`ČS`, `KB`, `FB` for Fio banka, `MM` for MONETA Money Bank), else the first two letters of a single word (`RA`, `MB`), all in capitals. `bank-logo/`: `<app-bank-mark [src] [name] [size]>` (sizes 24 and 40, `aria-hidden`) draws a round mark: the image (`loading="lazy"`, `width`/`height`, `alt=""`, on `--color-paper`, which is the same in both appearances, since a site icon may be transparent), on its `error` the monogram until another `src` comes, the monogram when there is no image (drawn by CSS from `data-monogram`, so it is not part of the text or the accessible name; sized by the theme's `--text-bank-monogram` and `--text-bank-monogram-long` in container units of the mark, so it never leaves the circle) and a plain bank sign (inline SVG) without a name and at 24 px, where letters would be too small to read and the bank's name follows the number; `<app-bank-logo [account] [size]>` feeds it from `BankNames` (its host is not hidden, only the mark inside it). Specs get `testing/bank-logo-fixtures.ts` (`FakeBankLogosApi`, an empty list unless a spec sets `items`) from `src/test-providers.ts`, so no spec asks for the list unless it provides the real `BankLogosApi`. `bank-account/` (`<app-bank-account [account]>`, `BankNames` through an effect on the input): a Czech account starts with its 24 px `app-bank-logo`, then the account in `.ui-figures` and, when its bank is named, `· <name>` after it in `.bank` (muted, regular weight, may wrap onto the next line while the number never breaks); a parent styles the element by its own class. It stands in the Odesílatel column and both sheets of `/sprava/platby`, the Bankovní účty table of the donor card and the Účet column of `/sprava/starsi-symboly`; the donor's `bank-accounts-card` starts the row of each Czech account with a 40 px `app-bank-logo` (a foreign account has none; the logo centres on the number line and the lines under it (bank, ověřeno, gifts without a symbol), as an iOS cell does, while the hint, the confirmed symbols and the offer below them start beside it under that block, in `.account-body`; the gap is the logo's own margin, so a foreign account starts at the edge; each row is the size container `account`, and below `tabs` of it, as with enlarged text on a phone, the logo stands on a line of its own above the number, which may break anywhere when even its own line is too narrow, `e2e/largest-text.spec.ts`) and names the bank as the first item of the line under the number instead (`.account-bank`, allowed to wrap, through `BankNames` for every listed account), so the status label keeps its place beside the number. Account numbers inside sentences (the overview's question, the add-account dialog's titles, the status lines after an action), the gift lines of the donor card's Dary (the card's Bankovní účty names the bank) and the shortened `…5399/0800` of a gift's tag stay without the bank.
- `spayd.ts`: `spayd(details, { amountMinor, variableSymbol?, purpose? })` → `{ spayd }` or `{ refused: { fields } }` (`amountMinor`, `variableSymbol`, `details`, in that order): the SPAYD text of a payment (`SPD*1.0*ACC*AM*CC*PT:IP[*X-VS][*RN]*MSG`, the message of the purpose; `PT:IP` asks the payer's bank for an instant payment, Jakub 2026-11-06), refused for an amount outside 1 heller to 9 999 999,99 Kč or not whole hellers, a symbol that is not 1 to 10 digits (or none for `ACCOUNT_VERIFICATION`) and details that are not SPAYD-ready (contract, "`GET /api/donations/payment-details`": among them an IBAN that is not the IBAN of the shown account number, checked by `czech-account.ts`, `ibanOfAccountNumber` and `accountMatchesIban`, on the cases of `shared-cases/payment/account.json`); the cases are `shared-cases/payment/spayd.json` (`spayd.spec.ts` runs each through `spayd()` and then `encodeText` at level H against its namesake in `qr/payment.json`).
- `payment-panel/` (`app-payment-panel`, inputs `variableSymbol` and `idPrefix`, `donate` by default, so the home page keeps `#donate-custom-amount`; a size container `payment-panel` that stacks its bank rows and sets the QR above its caption as on a phone also when the panel itself is at most 480 px of the root text size wide, as in the two-column home section on a tablet upright or with enlarged text): the amount choice, then its projected content, then the bank details, then the content projected with the attribute `afterBankDetails` (the signed-out receipt note of the home page, the standing order guide of the card "Poslat dar"), and the QR with "Uložit QR kód" of the home donate section (2) and (4); on phones and tablets (`CompactLayout.isCompact()`, slice 2.23c, Q13) the QR's text block gives way, after the projected content, to the primary "Otevřít v aplikaci banky", which on a device whose `navigator.canShare` takes both the payment file and an image (iOS, `sharesPaymentFiles` and `sharesImages` in `bank-handoff.ts`) shares the PNG of the drawn QR (drawn ahead as below and waited for with the button disabled; shared as "QR platba Nová naděje 500 Kč.png" with the drawn amount, because iOS heads its share sheet with the file's name and ignores the share's `title` and `text`, #387, while a saved or downloaded image keeps `qr-platba-nova-nadeje.png`), because no iOS bank app takes the payment file from the share sheet (Jakub's iPhone test of 5 November 2026; Alza's `pay-via-bank-app` shares the image on iOS too), and when that share is dismissed or refused the notice says to choose "Uložit obrázek" and read the payment from Photos in the bank app; there the second button is "Poslat jako soubor platby", which shares `platba.spayd` (the SPAYD text of the drawn amount, `application/x-shortpaymentdescriptor`) for a bank app that may take it, with its own notice pointing back to the image; a drawing that failed ahead makes the primary share `platba.spayd` instead; elsewhere the primary hands `platba.spayd` to the share sheet where `navigator.canShare` takes the file and otherwise, when the service worker controls the page and reserves the payment (`local-payment-route.ts`, `LocalPaymentRoute.reserve`: a message `nfnn-payment-file` over a `MessageChannel`, answered within 2 s with a random id), opens `/platba.spayd?id=<id>`, which the worker answers on the device (Android, follow-up of 2.23c: `frontend/service-worker/payment-route.js`; the notice then says what to do if no bank opened), and otherwise downloads it (a browser without the worker, a worker of an older release or the safety worker, which do not answer the message); when another amount was picked while the worker was asked, neither the earlier payment opens nor its file downloads, then (where the primary does not share the image) "Poslat QR jako obrázek", which shares the PNG of the drawn QR (`QrCode.pngBlob`, 720 px, drawn ahead after each redraw and waited for with the button disabled, so `navigator.share` is called within the tap before anything is awaited and keeps its user activation on iOS; a drawing that failed ahead leaves the button to save the PNG instead) or, where images cannot be shared or the share is refused, downloads it and is then labelled "Uložit QR kód"; while a share is open both buttons stay focusable with `aria-disabled` and a second tap does nothing, and the answer to a share that comes after another amount was picked says nothing; one always-rendered `role="status"` line after the first button says, while a hand-over runs, what happens ("Otevíráme nabídku sdílení…" while the share sheet is open, so the dimmed buttons in the second or so iOS takes to answer a closed sheet are explained, "Otevíráme platbu v aplikaci banky…" while the worker reserves the payment, "Připravujeme obrázek…" while the image is drawn to be saved; #387), and then what to do when the bank did not open (a dismissed or refused share, or the downloaded file), is cleared by another amount, and says nothing after a share that went through, since the page cannot know whether a bank app took it; the QR itself stays rendered (so its image can be made) in a `role="region"` folded with `hidden` under the disclosure button "Platíte z jiného zařízení?" (`aria-expanded`, `aria-controls`; the region is named by the button's constant first line), 260 px wide when unfolded (`max-width: 100%`, never a percentage the frame would size itself by: such a width sizes the frame by the SVG's default 300 px and leaves the QR at its left side) with a caption naming the amount (read out too, since the image's label names no amount), and its failure callout stands outside the fold before the buttons, which wait disabled while no payment is drawn; there "Zkusit znovu" moves the focus to the block of the buttons (`tabindex="-1"`, `aria-busy`) while it waits and to "Otevřít v aplikaci banky" once the payment is drawn; on computers the panel is as before, shared since Jakub's decision of 29 October 2026 by the home page and the card "Poslat dar" of the donor overview; the chosen amount (a preset, or a valid "Jiná částka" with its typed value) is kept in memory by the root `ChosenAmountStore` (`chosen-amount.ts`, nothing stored in the browser, 500 Kč after a reload) and shared by both places, so moving from the home page to the overview or back keeps it (testers of 2026-10-30); it exposes `accountState` (`loading`, `ready` with the account, `failed`) and `amount` (the chosen whole crowns, `null` while none is typed) so the standing order guide in the card shows the account of the same answer and the chosen amount, `retry()` and `focusBankDetails()`. Under the bank rows a `role="note"` says "Údaje účtu z 3. 11. 2026 · nemusí být aktuální" ("Údaje účtu nemusí být aktuální" without a known day) while the details are the kept ones or the app is offline (2.7). The QR's failure is a callout of two kinds, inside an always-rendered `role="status"` region when it comes with the page and a `role="alert"` once the visitor's own retry fails again: `unavailable` (the details did not load: the error text) and `refused` (the details loaded but `spayd()` or `encodeText` refused them: "QR kód teď nejde vytvořit. Zaplaťte prosím podle bankovního spojení."); "Zkusit znovu" calls `PaymentDetailsStore.reload()`, moves the focus on computers to the QR box (`tabindex="-1"`) while it waits (on phones and tablets to the block of the buttons, above) and back to the button when it fails again. "Jiná částka" clears the ready line; an amount is announced once, not again when it is picked again, but again after the placeholder or an invalid amount came between. "Jiná částka" renders its field and focuses it within the tap itself (`ChangeDetectorRef.detectChanges()` before `focus()`), also when it is pressed again, because iOS opens the keyboard only for a focus inside the tap's handler (#381); the field is `inputmode="numeric"` with `enterkeyhint="done"`, and Enter lets go of it.
- `gift-arrival/` (Jakub 2026-11-06): a signed-in donor who pays from the payment panel (Přispět or Poslat dar) learns there that the gift arrived. `GiftArrivalWatch`, provided by `PaymentPanel` and enabled only while the panel has a variable symbol and a donor is signed in, starts watching on an intent to pay, never on display alone, because Poslat dar stands on every visit to Můj přehled: a copy of a bank detail (`CopyButton`'s `copied`), a hand-off (Otevřít v aplikaci banky, Poslat jako soubor platby or QR jako obrázek, Uložit QR kód), unfolding Platíte z jiného zařízení?, the QR code at least half in view for 5 s with the tab visible (`IntersectionObserver`; a QR scanned with a phone leaves no other trace), or the tab hidden while any part of the panel is in view and no thanks stand (the donor left for the bank app). The baseline, the gifts there before, is a fresh read at the start of the watch (`GiftFeed.requestGifts()`, read again at the next intent or check when it failed), never the device copy as it was last synced, which may lack a gift imported meanwhile; until it lands nothing counts and no check asks the bank (a check waiting for it asks once it lands). While it watches it follows the `donations` feed (and the resync after a reconnect) and asks the bank through the on-demand check 30, 60, 120, 240 and 480 s after each intent and at once when the tab becomes visible again, never sooner than 30 s after its own previous ask (the server's cooldown would make it a no-op), never while the tab is hidden or `Connectivity` is offline (once when it is back), one check at a time and at most 12 per watch; it ends 10 minutes after the last intent or return (paused while hidden, so a donor who stays long in the bank app is still thanked), on the first arrival, when the panel goes, the symbol goes or another donor signs in. A gift counts as arrived when it belongs to the donor the watch started for, its id is new, it is `RECEIVED` and it is neither an account verification nor an answer about an older symbol (`CONFIRMED_SYMBOL`); a gift paired by the sender account counts, and the client clock plays no part. The gifts come from a `GiftFeed` (`GIFT_FEED`): outside the donor area `RepositoryGiftFeed` (`DonorDonationsRepository.fresh('sync')` for the baseline and on each hint, `checkBankUntilDone()` for the check and, without a live stream, one read after it), in the donor area `DonorDonations` itself (`donor-area` provides it as `GIFT_FEED`), so Můj přehled asks the bank through its own check (a check of the watch leaves the outcome line of the donor's own check alone unless it finds a new gift or the line said the check failed or was unfinished) and follows the gifts through its own reading, with its Kontrolujeme nové platby v bance… and Zobrazit 1 nový dar; only the baseline is a read of its own. The panel shows the thanks right after the content projected with `afterBankDetails`, so above Otevřít v aplikaci banky on a phone and above the QR on a computer, where the donor returns or looks, without scrolling or moving focus: `.ui-notice--status` with an always rendered `role="status"` paragraph, "Váš dar 500 Kč dorazil. Děkujeme!" ("Dorazily 2 dary, celkem 1 500 Kč. Děkujeme!", "Dorazilo 5 darů, …"), the link Zobrazit v přehledu outside Můj přehled, and Zavřít, which returns the focus it held to Otevřít v aplikaci banky or the QR. The thanks stay until closed, the panel goes, the panel loses its variable symbol or another donor (or nobody) is signed in (no timeout, WCAG 2.2.1). Anonymous visitors are never watched.
- `donations-api.ts`: `GET /api/donations/me` and `/me/summary` (the API's `year` filter has no caller); `requestBankCheck()` (POST) and `bankCheck()` (GET) on `/me/bank-check`; `checkBankUntilDone()` posts and, while `checking`, reads the check again as soon as `LiveUpdates` hints `bank-import`, else after 2 s without a stream and 15 s with one, for at most 90 s, emitting every answer; `downloadedFile(response, fallbackFileName)` turns a blob answer into `DownloadedFile` (`blob`, `fileName` from `Content-Disposition`: `filename*=UTF-8''` first, then `filename=`, else the fallback) for the administration's downloads; the donor's receipts are drawn in the browser (`receipt-pdf/`, plan 1.9), so the donor area no longer calls the PDF endpoints.
- `bank-accounts-api.ts`: `GET /api/donations/me/bank-accounts` (`ACTIVE`, `AWAITING_PAYMENT`, `DISCONNECTED`, `shared`, `pairingExcluded`, `verificationPaymentOn`, `symbolOffer` with `variableSymbol`, `paymentCount`, `firstMonth`/`lastMonth` as `YYYY-MM`, and `confirmedSymbols`), `POST …/{id}/disconnect|reconnect` (204), `confirmSymbol` / `declineSymbol` on `POST …/{id}/symbol-claim/confirm` (`{ pairedCount, changedYearlyReceipts }`) and `…/decline` (204) with `{ variableSymbol }`; `DonationItem.pairedBy` (`VARIABLE_SYMBOL` | `SENDER_ACCOUNT` | `CONFIRMED_SYMBOL` | null), `senderAccount`, `confirmedSymbol` and `receiptAvailable`.
- `receipt-format.ts`: what a receipt shows, one place for the verification page and the receipt drawn in the browser: `formatReceiptAmount(minor)` (`formatMinorUnits` + `\u00a0Kč`), `formatReceiptDate` (`5. 3. 2026`, as everywhere on the site, Jakub 2026-11-06), the English values of a bilingual receipt `formatReceiptAmountEn` (`CZK 1,000.50`) and `formatReceiptDateEn` (`15 March 2026`), `fillReceiptText(text, values)` (the frozen texts' `{name}` placeholders with MessageFormat's apostrophe quoting; a missing value throws), `formatReceiptDonor` (name, IČO for a company, `street, postal code city`), `receiptDisplay(receipt)` for a `SignedReceipt`; the donor and amount cases of `shared-cases/receipt/` run in its spec.
- `receipt-pdf/` (plan 1.9): the donor's receipt drawn in the browser from its frozen data, so the server renders no PDF (since 1.10 it has no drawing at all). `receipts-api.ts`, the feature service of both sides: `issue(subject)` → `POST /api/donations/me/receipts` (`{donationId}` or `{year}`, `IssuedReceipt` of `frozen-receipt.ts`), `image(sha256)` → `GET /api/donations/me/receipt-images/{sha256}` as bytes with their media type, and the two `ReceiptSource`s a drawing reads from: `donorSource(subject)` (that issue and those images) and `administrationSource(receiptId)` (1.19: `GET /api/admin/donations/receipts/{id}`, which issues nothing, and `GET /api/admin/donations/receipt-images/{sha256}`). `receipt-document.ts`: `receiptDocument(receipt, issuedAt, captions)` puts every line together from the frozen texts and values (the English line beside the Czech one on a bilingual receipt, English table values in brackets, the footer from `receipt.footer`); the caption beside the verification QR and the images' alternative texts also come from the frozen texts (`receipt.verification.*`, `receipt.signature.alt`, `receipt.portrait.alt`, frozen since 1.10, the alternative texts in Czech), and `captions` (`receipt-captions.ts`, the same words) only fills in for a receipt frozen without them, which only the shared cases still hold and names the file `potvrzeni-o-daru-<number>.pdf` / `potvrzeni-o-darech-<year>.pdf`, on an English receipt `donation-receipt-<number>.pdf` / `donation-summary-<year>.pdf`. `verification-qr.ts`: `verificationSymbol(qrText)` encodes the proof address as a byte segment and the proof digits as a numeric one, level H at the smallest version that holds it, so the foundation's star fits in its middle, else L at the smallest version, without the star (above about 220 gifts; `smallestVersion` of `shared/ui/qr-code/qr-encoder.ts`); `qrPlacement(symbol)` puts a symbol up to version 15 beside the signature as a 46.2 mm square (modules of at least 0.6 mm, Jakub 2026-10-31) and a larger one in an area of its own with modules of 0.6–0.8 mm (100 mm at most). `receipt-art.ts`: the signature and portrait images, PNG, JPEG or the vector JSON of `vector-art.ts` (strict: exact keys, a positive view box, 1–2 000 paths of SVG path commands starting with `M`, fills `#rrggbb`, opacity 0–1, 256 KB at most), each refused unless its SHA-256 is the digest in the frozen data. `receipt-style.ts`: `resolveReceiptStyle(element)` reads the theme's `--receipt-*` tokens as text, with no canvas: colours only as literal `#rrggbb`, sizes in pt, margins in mm, leading as a number and the three font files by their content-named file name (`body.<16 hex>.ttf`, `body-bold.…`, `heading.…`); anything else is refused with `ReceiptStyleRefused`, and so is a text colour (`text`, `muted`, `english`) or the QR colour (`code`) below 4.5:1 on the white page (`checkedReceiptStyle`, which `drawReceipt` runs again in the worker). `receipt-layout.ts`, the only module that imports pdfkit (`eslint-confinement.js`), draws an A4 tagged PDF (PDF/UA subset, `lang`, Document, H1, H2, P, Table with TR/TH/TD, Figure with alt text, Link): the vector logo, the replaced-receipt notice, both parties, the gift line or a table whose header repeats as an artifact on every page, the totals, the closing kept on one page (declaration, then the signature block in the layout "3B dopis" of plan 1.16: place and date on the left, on the right a card with the round portrait, the signature, a rule, the name and role), the verification QR in the look of the site's payment QR (Jakub 2026-10-31: dots in `--receipt-color-code`, rounded finder and alignment marks in `--receipt-color-code-mark`, at level H a badge in the middle with the foundation's star in `--receipt-color-code-star` on `--receipt-color-code-star-fill`, 12 % of the symbol's width; the geometry is `brandedQrLayout` of `shared/ui/qr-code/qr-branded-layout.ts`, which the site's QR component draws from too) inside a link to its address with a caption (`receipt-captions.ts`, "Ověření potvrzení" and "Načtěte kód telefonem a uvidíte, co fond podepsal." (Jakub 2026-10-31), on a bilingual receipt with their English ids `…English`; beside the signature the caption stands under the card, and the signature row counts the code in the left column and the card with the caption in the right, so a long caption moves the closing to the next page instead of running into the footer, and a QR in its own area keeps its four-module quiet zone above the bottom margin), and the footer with the page numbers on every page; vector art is clipped to its box (a signature to 60 × 18 mm), and Title, Author and Subject are XML-escaped for the XMP packet only (pdfkit 0.20.2 writes them there raw; the document information dictionary keeps them as written); it is deterministic (the creation date is the issue time, the font subsets are named from the font id). Its specs run in the Node environment (`// @vitest-environment node`, so pdfkit's XMP is the bytes a worker writes) and draw every frozen case in both themes with each theme's own `--receipt-*` values and the captions the site prints: exact page counts (v2's, apart from the listed differences: the gift rows are 1.2 mm lower than v2's so a year of 12 or 13 gifts stays on one page with the larger code (Jakub 2026-10-31), which also brings the 366-gift year from 12 pages to 10, and a bilingual year of 2 or 3 gifts takes a second page for its closing in the default theme), the progress bar's estimate (`estimatedReceiptPages(gifts, bilingual)`, fitted to the default theme) equal to the drawn count, each caption once, no text below the bottom margin, the quiet zone clear, the XMP well-formed with `pdfuaid:part` 1, `dc:title` and `xmp:CreateDate`. `receipt-pdf.worker.ts` is a module worker (its own chunk `worker-<hash>.js`, compiled with `tsconfig.worker.json`) that fetches the fonts the style names from `/receipt-fonts/` (copied from `src/themes/<theme>/receipt/` by `angular.json`; the hash in the name makes them immutable, as the worker chunk is), says `ready`, draws the request it gets, posts `page` per page and `done` with the transferred bytes, or `failed` with a reason and the closed `name` of what it caught (`errorNameOf`, `core/errors/error-vocabulary.ts`): `tools-missing` when a font answers 404 (`receipt-fonts.ts`, `loadReceiptFonts`: a tab older than a deploy that changed the receipt fonts, which then takes the same one reload as a lost worker chunk), `fonts` for any other font failure, `drawing` for a throw in the layout. `receipt-pdf.ts`: `ReceiptPdf.draw(source)` emits the phases `data` (the receipt from its source and the images), `tools` (the worker loading) and `drawing` (with the page), then `done` with the `DownloadedFile`; it fails with `ReceiptPdfFailure` `tools-missing` (the worker failed before it said ready, as when a deploy removed its chunk, or could not be started), `fonts`, `image`, `unprintable` (the frozen data holds a character outside the printable set, found before any worker starts), `style` (the theme's receipt tokens were refused) or `drawing` (any other throw, on the main thread too), or after 120 s without an event (`drawing`, named `TimeoutError`); each failure carries the closed `errorName` of its cause, and `ReceiptPdf` reports it through `ErrorReporter` (place `global`): `pdf-load` for `tools-missing` and `fonts`, `pdf-render` for `drawing`, `image` and `style`, nothing for `unprintable` (the data, not the browser) or a refusal of the server; unsubscribing terminates the worker; specs replace the worker and the style through `RECEIPT_PDF_ENVIRONMENT` (`testing/fake-receipt-worker.ts`). `pdf-delivery.ts`: `PdfDelivery.needsSecondTap(file)` is true on an iPhone or iPad (iPadOS's desktop agent with touch too) whose `navigator.canShare` takes the file, because Safari drops the tap's user activation while the PDF is drawn and would refuse both the download and the share sheet; `save(file)` goes through `saveBlob`, `share(file)` answers `shared`, `dismissed` (the sheet closed) or `failed`. `receipt-restart.ts`: `ReceiptRestart.tryRestart(download)` reloads the page once per tab (`sessionStorage` `nfnn.receipt-restart`) with `?stahnout=rok-<year>` or `dar-<id>` and answers `reloading`, `reloaded-already` (this tab already reloaded for that download; the marker is cleared) or `unavailable` (no session storage, so no reload); `finished()` clears the marker, `abandoned(download)` only when it names that download.
  Bulk (slice 2.29): `receipt-layout.ts` splits into `ReceiptPdfFile` (the document, its fonts, the `Document` structure root and an image cache that opens each raster once with `doc.openImage`) and `ReceiptLayout` (one receipt in its own structure parent, footer pages numbered within that receipt), so `drawReceipt` draws exactly as before (the 14 frozen cases with a portrait give the same bytes) and `drawReceiptBundle(items, {info, duplex}, …)` puts each receipt in its own `Sect` on a new page, with a blank page before it when `duplex` needs it to start on an odd page, and flushes the pages after each receipt to keep the worker's memory flat. Its types sit in `receipt-bundle-options.ts`, outside the pdfkit confinement. The worker takes a `bundle` request beside the single one (`receipt-worker-messages.ts`) and reports `{kind: 'receipt', index}` after each receipt; `ReceiptPdf.drawBundle` fetches each image digest once (`ReceiptsApi.administrationImage`), sets the PDF's creation date to the latest `issuedAt` and emits `images`, `tools`, `drawing {receipt, receipts}` and `done`. `receipt-bundle.spec.ts` proves that each receipt's pages in a bundle print exactly what its single PDF prints, footer numbers included.
- `verification/` (the page `/overit`, public shell, plan 1.8, D32): `receipt-proof.ts` reads the proof of `shared-cases/verification/payload.json` strictly (digits back to bytes with `BigInt`, the body as `ProofBytesReader`/`ReceiptProofBody` read it on the backend, every rejection reason in the backend's order, only a key whose status is `active` verifies, `statusDigest` the SHA-256 of the signed message and the signature); `ed25519.ts` verifies with WebCrypto `Ed25519` and, when the browser lacks it, with `noble-ed25519.ts` (`@noble/curves` 2.4.0, `zip215: false`), the only file `eslint-confinement.js` lets import it and only by `import()`, so the library is a lazy chunk the WebCrypto path never loads; `receipt-keys.ts` (`trustedKeys(list, hostname)`) checks each entry of a key list and drops a bad one, counts a key id listed twice as revoked, and trusts the RFC 8032 test keys only on `localhost`, `127.0.0.1` and `[::1]`; `receipt-keys-api.ts` (`GET /api/donations/receipt-keys` with the request header `Cache-Control: no-cache`, the app's HTTP backend being XHR) and `receipt-status-api.ts` (`POST /api/donations/receipts/status` with `{ digest }`) both send `WITHOUT_ACCESS_TOKEN`; `receipt-key-store.ts` keeps the last trusted list in `localStorage` `nfnn.receipt-keys` (every access in try/catch, replaced whole); `receipt-verifier.ts` (`ReceiptVerifier.verify(fragment, progress)`) reads with the kept keys, fetches the list when the browser says it is online (5 s), keeps it, reads again, reports `asking-status` and asks the status (5 s) only for a valid signature; a key list failure is offline only when no response came at all (status 0) or the browser is offline, anything else (429, 5xx, timeout) is `no-answer`, and a list that is not a list is `keys-refused`; once the list came, any status failure or unknown answer is `unanswered`, never offline; outcomes `empty`, `broken`, `newer` (also a reserved flag), `no-connection`, `no-answer` (no keys), `keys-refused`, `failed` (an exception while verifying, reported as `verify-signature`/`global`; a code that is broken, invalid or revoked is no browser failure and reports nothing), `invalid`, `revoked`, `unknown-key` (`reach` `online`, `offline` or `no-answer`) and `signed` with the status `active`, `replaced`, `withdrawn`, `outdated`, `unknown`, `offline`, `no-answer` (key list failed, kept keys) or `unanswered` (status asked, no answer). `verification-page` drops the fragment with `Location.replaceState('/overit')`, sets `robots` `noindex` while shown (nginx also sends `X-Robots-Tag: noindex` for `/overit`, mirrored by `scripts/serve-dist.mjs`), says "Ověřujeme potvrzení…" with a `role="status"` line that changes to "Podpis sedí, ptáme se fondu na stav potvrzení…" once the signature has passed, and focuses the `h1` of the result, and on "Zkusit znovu" that of the checking state; `verification-result` shows the result: green (✓) only for a valid signature the foundation keeps as `active` or one checked offline (with the sentence that a replacement cannot be seen), a caution (!) for `replaced`, `outdated` (1.26, Jakub 2026-11-05: "Potvrzení platilo ke dni vystavení" and that the donor's gifts changed since, so only a newer receipt counts; without one, write to info@nfnn.cz with the receipt's number), `unknown` (Jakub 2026-10-30: never green), no usable answer from the foundation ("Podpis sedí, ale fond teď neodpověděl, zda potvrzení vede", with "Zkusit znovu"), an unknown key offline or without an answer, no keys, a refused key list ("Neověřeno: seznam klíčů fondu teď nejde použít", no retry, write to info@nfnn.cz), a broken or newer code (a broken one says to scan it again only when it was read by the camera or from a photo, `ProofInput`'s `found` carrying `{ fragment, scanned }`; from the address, a pasted link or a PDF it says to open the link from the PDF again or read its QR code) and a failure, red (×) for `withdrawn`, `invalid`, `revoked` (write to info@nfnn.cz with the receipt number; no new receipt is promised, since the web cannot issue one under a new key yet) and a key the foundation does not publish; the signed data as a `ui-data-list` (number, issue day, the receipt it replaces, donor, IČO, address, gift day and amount or year and total) and, for a yearly receipt, the table "Jednotlivé dary" with each day as a row header and amounts on one line; under every result that checked a signature a line saying what left the device (the digest after the foundation answered, the digest without an answer after an unanswered status call, nothing otherwise), and always the hint that the foundation verifies only at nfnn.cz/overit. Without a code the page offers `proof-input` (Jakub 2026-10-31, from the plan's admin scanner 2.30 brought to the public page): under three `h2`s "Načíst fotoaparátem" (only when `navigator.mediaDevices.getUserMedia` exists), "Nahrát soubor" and "Vložit odkaz". "Naskenovat QR kód" asks for the rear camera, shows a muted inline `<video>` named "Náhled z fotoaparátu" with "Zastavit skenování" (which takes the focus when the scan button it replaces had it; stopping turns off every track and focuses the scan button again), and reads a frame 250 ms after the previous read ended, until a timer of its own ends the scan after two minutes with what to try; a preview that cannot play, a frame that cannot be read or a camera that ends by itself stops the scan with "Fotoaparát se nepodařilo spustit…", a hidden tab turns the camera off (the scan button takes the focus if it was lost), and a code handed over by any way turns it off too; when the scan ends by itself the scan button takes the focus only if it was lost; "Nahrát PDF nebo fotku" (`app-file-button`, `application/pdf,image/*`, at most 20 MB) reads a PDF with `pdf-proof-link.ts` (the first `/URI` string of the file, literal or hex, a hex string in UTF-16 included, that is a proof link; the PDF is not rendered, so it finds the link the browser-drawn receipt carries on its QR code and nothing in a scanned or re-saved PDF without link annotations) and an image through `scan-device.ts` (the size from its PNG, JPEG or WebP header by `picture-size.ts`, more than 50 megapixels refused as "Obrázek je příliš velký…" and any other format, GIF included, as unreadable, then `createImageBitmap`, upright by its EXIF orientation, scaled to at most 2 400 px into a canvas); `pdf-proof-link.ts` looks at no more than 64 `/URI` strings and reads each over at most 64 KiB, so a crafted PDF cannot hold the page; "Odkaz z QR kódu" is a template-driven field with `appProofLink` (`proof-link-validator.ts`). Every way accepts only the exact prefix `https://nfnn.cz/overit#` followed by digits (`proof-link.ts`, at most 7 089 digits; a code of another sender says "Tento QR kód nepatří k potvrzení fondu Nová naděje…" and scanning goes on), never navigates anywhere and hands the digits to the page, which verifies them in place without touching the address or the history, so the code stays out of the URL; after any result "Ověřit další potvrzení" returns to the empty page and focuses its `h1`. `qr-reader.ts` (`QrReader`) asks the browser's `BarcodeDetector` for `qr_code` where it has one and otherwise, or when it finds nothing, the module worker `qr-decoder.worker.ts` (its own `worker-*` chunk, one per page, answers matched to their read by id, ended when the page is left or after a 10 s timeout, which gives every waiting read nothing; a worker that fails before its first answer, or cannot be started, is reported as `qr-encode`/`global` (the closest kind the closed list has for the QR code worker), and after three such starts in a row the reader starts no more and rejects every read with `QrReaderOutdated`, which `proof-input` shows as "Web se mezitím aktualizoval. Obnovte prosím stránku a načtěte kód znovu."), which alone loads `qr-decoding.ts`, the only importer of `jsqr` 1.4.0 (`eslint-confinement.js`), so jsQR is never in the page's bundles. Two `role="status"` lines, always rendered (an empty one is taken out of the layout with `position: absolute`, never `display: none`, so it stays in the accessibility tree), say under the camera "Spouštíme fotoaparát…" or "Namiřte fotoaparát na QR kód z potvrzení." and under the file "Čteme kód ze souboru…", and a `role="alert"` callout under the way that caused it names a refused or missing camera, a failed camera or a scan that gave up, a PDF without a verification link, an image without a code, an unreadable or too large file or picture. The gift table stays a two-column table on phones, against the rule that tables become cards: cards would triple its height and it fits at 320 px with WCAG text spacing even for 120 gifts. The `.ui-status__icon` modifiers `--success` (green), `--caution` (amber) and `--danger` (red) in `styles/ui/_panel.scss` colour its icon; the older `--warning` is red as well, so a new warning takes `--caution`.
- `donation-month.ts`: `formatMonth('2025-01')` → `leden 2025` (names `@@donor.month.january` … `december`), `formatMonthRange(first, last)` → `říjen 2026`, `březen–říjen 2026` or `leden 2025 – říjen 2026`, with no-break spaces and no locale data.
- `donation-date.ts`: `YYYY-MM-DD` → `15. 3. 2026` without locale data (no leading zeros in day and month, ČSN 01 6910, Jakub 2026-11-06); `formatBankTime` → Prague `5. 1. 2026 8:05` (hour without a leading zero, minutes two digits, as the backend's e-mails). `donation-amount.ts`: whole crowns without decimals, hellers always two (`1 000,50`). `testing/` fixtures.

## operations

- `setup/setup-api.ts` (`SetupApi`, `providedIn: 'root'`): `status()` → `GET /api/operations/setup` (`SetupStatusResponse`, sent with `WITHOUT_ACCESS_TOKEN`: the endpoint is public, and a session token the restarted backend no longer accepts would make every poll fail), `unlock(code)`, `sendTestEmail(code, request)` → `POST /api/operations/setup/test-email` (`SetupTestEmailRequest`: `MailServerRequest` plus `sender` and `recipient`; `TestEmailResponse` with `outcome: TestEmailOutcome`, `sent`, `recipient`), `complete(request)` (`SetupRequest`, `SettingValueRequest`, `SettingsSavedResponse`), and `watchRestart()`, which polls the status every `RESTART_POLL_MS` (3 s) and answers `'restarted'` after a failed poll followed by an answer (or after ten answers in a row, when the restart was faster than a poll) and `'slow'` after 100 polls; before answering `'restarted'` it refreshes an open session once (`AuthStore.refresh()`, a refusal ignored), because the access token's audience is `PUBLIC_URL` + `/api` and a changed site address makes the restarted backend refuse the old token with `auth/unauthenticated`, which the interceptor does not refresh; `SettingName` lists the setting names of the contract. `setting-labels.ts`: `SETTING_LABELS` (Czech label per setting, `@@operations.setting.<NAME>`), `settingLabel(name)` and `settingErrors(failure)`, which turns the `errors[]` of a 400 `operations/invalid-settings` into "Label: message" lines; shared with the administration's settings page. `test-email.ts`: `testEmailNotice(result, { host, sender })` turns a `TestEmailResponse` into the `Notice` both pages show (`@@operations.testEmail.<outcome>`): `SENT` "Zkušební e-mail odešel na {recipient}. Podívejte se prosím do schránky, i do nevyžádané pošty. Když do pár minut nedorazí, zkontrolujte adresu odesílatele a ověření její domény v Brevo." as a status, and as alerts: cannot connect to {host} (check the server name and port), {host} did not answer within 10 seconds (check the port, Brevo uses 587), no STARTTLS so the password was not sent, the sign-in refused (check the name and the SMTP key), the sender {sender} refused (verify the sender and its domain in Brevo), the recipient refused, or a general failure.
- `setup/first-setup` (`/prvni-nastaveni`, public shell): asks the status ("Zjišťujeme, jestli je web nastavený…"; a failure says to wait a minute with "Zkusit znovu"). Already set up: "Web už je nastavený" with a link to `/sprava/nastaveni`. With `codeRequired`: `h1` "První nastavení webu", a lead saying where the code is (ZimaOS → the app → terminal and logs → container `nfnn-backend`) and that the home network needs none, the field "Kód z logu" ("Opište prosím kód z logu." when empty) and "Pokračovat" ("Ověřujeme kód…"); a 403 says the code changes with every start. Otherwise, or once unlocked, the form in five numbered sections: 1. Web ("Adresa webu", filled with the page's origin when it is `https://`, else `https://nfnn.cz`; the field checks `https?://` without `?`/`#`), 2. Správce ("E-mail prvního správce", required; before the e-mails since the test e-mail goes there), 3. E-maily (server `smtp-relay.brevo.com`, port 587, name and password in `<app-password-reveal>`, both required while "Server vyžaduje přihlášení" is ticked, the checkboxes for sign-in and STARTTLS folded in `<details>` "Pokročilé nastavení serveru", sender and contact recipient `info@nfnn.cz`; at its end the hint "Zkušební e-mail přijde na e-mail prvního správce z kroku 2. …" and "Poslat zkušební e-mail" ("Posíláme zkušební e-mail…"; Jakub 2026-10-24), which first marks the involved fields that are invalid, says "Než zkušební e-mail pošleme, doplňte prosím označená pole." and focuses the first of them, and otherwise sends the typed server, sender and first administrator and shows `testEmailNotice` beside the button), 4. Banka ("Token Fio API (jen pro čtení, nepovinné)"), 5. Klíč k zálohám (Jakub 2026-10-28: the backups are encrypted and the setup does not go on until the key is saved): a lead saying the backups are encrypted with it and cannot be restored without it, the key from `SetupApi.backupKey(code)` (`GET /api/operations/setup/backup-key`, asked whenever the form opens and the key is not loaded; "Načítáme klíč k zálohám…", a failure with "Načíst znovu", 409 `backup-key-unavailable` a warning that the key appears later in Zálohy, and then the setup goes on without it) shown by `features/operations/backup-key/backup-key-panel` (`app-backup-key-panel`: "Klíč k zálohám je těchto 24 slov", an ordered list in a monospace grid (two columns on a phone) computed in the browser by `backup-key-words.ts` (with its own `bech32.ts` and synchronous `sha256.ts`, and the list `czech-wordlist.ts`); the `AGE-SECRET-KEY-1…` text itself is not shown (Jakub 2026-10-29: one form only); "Kopírovat", which copies the 24 words separated by spaces; "Stáhnout jako soubor", which saves `nfnn-klic-k-zaloham.txt`, an age identity file whose `#` lines explain how to restore and carry the 24 numbered words, so `age -d -i` takes it as it is and a printout holds both forms; a hint to keep the words in a password manager or on paper, four letters a word being enough, never only on the server), then "Mám uloženo, ověřit": the words disappear and three required fields "N. slovo klíče" ask for the positions the API drew, so they are typed from the saved copy (`backup-key-word-check.ts`: `appBackupKeyWord` compares the typed word read as the backend reads it, "Slovo nesedí s klíčem …" otherwise; autocomplete, autocorrect, autocapitalization and spellcheck off; focus on the first field), with "Ukázat slova znovu" to go back; "Uložit a spustit" before that step says at the button to save and check the words first and focuses it; the words are sent as `backupKeyWords`; a note that the web restarts, and "Uložit a spustit" ("Ukládáme…"); under the lead a "Budete potřebovat:" list (Brevo SMTP name and key, a verified sender domain, the administrator's e-mail, optionally the Fio token). Unless a test e-mail was sent with exactly the server and sender about to be saved, "Uložit a spustit" first shows the `role="alert"` warning "Zkušební e-mail s těmito údaji zatím neodešel. Bez funkčních e-mailů se správce nepřihlásí." with "Poslat zkušební e-mail" (focus to the test button, then the test) and "Uložit i tak". A 400 puts each refused value at its field (`placeServerErrors`, focus to the first) and lists the rest under the form by label; success shows "Web se restartuje" with a spinner while `watchRestart()` runs, then "Web je nastavený" with the four next steps as a numbered list (register with the administrator's address, open the link, turn on the second factor in Údaje a zabezpečení, sign in again with the code) and "Zaregistrovat se" (opens the sign-in dialog in registration mode with that address filled in), or after 100 polls an alert pointing at the container log with "Zkusit znovu". Above the form a box offers "Obnovit ze zálohy" for a site moved or recovered from another server: the panel (a form) "Obnovit web ze zálohy" asks for the `.dump.age` file ("Soubor zálohy", `accept=".age,.dump"`; an older `.dump` is accepted too, another ending is named at once under the field, "Tohle není soubor zálohy …") and, for a `.dump.age`, the field "Klíč k zálohám" in `<app-password-reveal>` (required, the key or its 24 words; `appBackupKey` checks it with `parseBackupKey` and the canonical `AGE-SECRET-KEY-1…` form is sent); "Nahrát a obnovit" (disabled until a backup file is chosen) streams it with `SetupApi.uploadBackup(code, file, key)` (`PUT /api/operations/setup/restore`, the code in `X-Nfnn-Setup-Code`, the key in `X-Nfnn-Backup-Key`) showing "Nahráváme zálohu… N %", "Zpět k nastavení" returns. Then "Obnovujeme web ze zálohy" waits with `watchRestart(RESTORE_MAX_POLLS)` and asks the status: setup no longer required → "Web je obnovený ze zálohy" (sign in with the old administrator account; the backup brought the SMTP password and the Fio token with the backup key, and only a backup from an earlier version needs them entered again in Nastavení) with "Přihlásit se" (sign-in dialog returning to `/sprava/nastaveni`); still required and `lastRestore` of this request OK → back to the form with "Záloha je obnovená. Pochází z doby před prvním nastavením …"; failed → the restore panel with "Obnova se nepovedla: {error}. Web zůstal prázdný …"; not taken → "Kontejner záloh si obnovu do pěti minut nevzal …"; a code that changed with the restart returns to the code step first. `setup-redirect.ts` (`SetupRedirect.checkOnce()`, called by the public shell): asks the status once per load; while the setup is required it sends every other page back to `/prvni-nastaveni` and asks again after each navigation away from it, and stops once the status says it is done. Specs `first-setup.spec.ts`, `setup-api.spec.ts` and `setup-redirect.spec.ts`; Playwright `e2e/first-setup.spec.ts` (home network: the home page leads to the setup, saves everything and waits for the restart, the registration gets the administrator's address; the backup key shown, downloaded and three of its words sent; saving without a test e-mail warns first; the test e-mail goes with the typed values to the first administrator; leaving for the home page comes back to the setup; from elsewhere: the code unlocks the form; a backup uploaded and restored ending at the sign-in; the form at 390 px) and the axe case for the code step, the form with its errors and the backup key, and the restore panel with a refused file and an encrypted file without its key.

## donors

- `login-dialog/`: sign-in and registration through `AuthStore`; while `Connectivity` says offline, the two starting tabs give way to `.offline-entry` (`h3` "Jste offline" focused, "Přihlásit se ani zaregistrovat se bez připojení nejde. Jakmile budete připojeni, přihlášení se tu ukáže samo.", an always rendered `role="status"` line and "Zkusit znovu", which asks `Connectivity.tryAgain()` and says "Pořád se nedaří připojit." when it reaches nothing), and the form comes back by itself when the app is online again (Jakub 2026-11-12, offline sign-in, scenario E); a later step (the code, the recovery) stays as it is; under the password a row `.session-choice` (plan v3 2.10, Jakub 2026-11-05 Q7, left out in the installed app): the checkbox "Zapamatovat si mě" (unticked, `REMEMBERED` when ticked, else `BROWSER`) and the small button "Cizí počítač?", which swaps the row for the ticked checkbox "Cizí počítač, nic neukládat" (`PUBLIC`) with the hint "Po zavření prohlížeče tu nic nezůstane." (`aria-describedby`) and "Zpět" (unticking it or "Zpět" brings the first row back); each swap moves the focus to the row's checkbox, and the chosen kind goes with the password, the passkey and the second-factor step (without a choice, such as a confirmation link's sign-in, `BROWSER`); `core/auth/public-session-idle.ts` (`PublicSessionIdle`, started at app start) signs a `PUBLIC` session out after `PUBLIC_IDLE_MS` (1 hour) without a pointer, key, touch or wheel input in any of its tabs (each tab tells the others over the session channel at most once a minute, `public-activity` in `session-tabs.ts`, and keeps the last input in `sessionStorage` `nfnn.public-activity`, so a reload continues the hour instead of starting it again), rechecked on every input and when the tab is shown again, then goes home with the signed-out notice, because the page's own refreshes keep the server's idle limit from ever running out; the problem's title and detail on failure, "Příliš mnoho pokusů z této adresy. Zkuste to prosím za chvíli znovu." on 429; a sign-in answered `auth/second-factor-required` swaps the form for the code step. Every step after the two starting tabs has the same shape: "← Zpět na …" on top, a visible `h3`, then the content, and no tab switch; every change of step moves the focus to the step's `[autofocus]` element (the first field, or the heading of a sent panel). The code step: "← Zpět na heslo", `h3` "Ověření kódem z aplikace", "Přihlášení dokončíte kódem z ověřovací aplikace.", field "Kód z aplikace" with `inputmode="numeric"`, `autocomplete="one-time-code"` and the six-digit pattern, which posts the challenge and the code through `AuthStore.completeSecondFactor`; a wrong code keeps the step with the problem in the alert line, `auth/invalid-second-factor-challenge` returns to the password form with the problem's sentence, 429 the same sentence as the sign-in; under the code form "Nemáte telefon s aplikací? Obnovit přístup platbou" posts the challenge through `AuthApi.startSecondFactorRecovery` and replaces the step with the instructions ("← Zpět na kód" back to the focused code field, `h3` "Obnova přístupu platbou" focused, a lead asking for 1 Kč "z bankovního účtu, ze kterého jste nám už poslali dar (obvykle ten, ze kterého nám platíte)", never pointing to the overview a signed-out donor cannot open, a warning "Platbu pošlete nejdříve …" before the details while `usableFrom` is set, with a second line in the same box "Nemůžete tak dlouho čekat? Napište nám na info@nfnn.cz a kód z aplikace vám vypneme." (mailto link), a warning "Tuto obnovu už jste zahájili … Pošlete platbu s tímto kódem." when the answer is `resumed` (Jakub 2026-10-24), account number and "OBNOVA …" message with copy buttons, "1 Kč", then `<app-qr-code>` with the answer's `spayd` encoded in the browser at level H (an alert "QR kód se nepodařilo vykreslit. Zaplaťte prosím podle uvedených údajů." if it cannot be) in a framed box below the details (so the details come first on a phone too), "Zpráva pro příjemce platí do …" in Prague time, and a note that the dialog may be closed because starting again while the message is valid shows the same details); 409 `donors/second-factor-recovery-unavailable` stays on the code step with the problem in the alert line; a registration answered 202 replaces the form with "Zkontrolujte si e-mail" (focused; the address in a `role="status"` line, a hint about the spam folder, "Poslat odkaz znovu" posting `AuthApi.requestEmailVerification` with its own `role="status"` line (the registration, this request and the sign-in tab's "Poslat ověřovací e-mail znovu" send `returnTo: '/#prispet'` when the dialog was opened from the "Přispět" section, `LoginDialogOptions.confirmationReturnTo`, kept also after switching tabs), "Opravit e-mail" back to the kept form with the e-mail focused, "Zpět na přihlášení"); a 400 with field errors puts them at their fields (`placeServerErrors`, focus to the first) and keeps only the title and detail in the alert line; a sign-in answered `auth/email-not-verified` keeps the problem in the alert line and adds "Poslat ověřovací e-mail znovu", which posts `AuthApi.requestEmailVerification` and confirms in a `role="status"` line (its own sentence on 429); "Zapomněli jste heslo?" right under the password field opens the reset step ("← Zpět na přihlášení", `h3` "Obnovení hesla"), which posts the trimmed address through `AuthApi.requestPasswordReset` ("Zkontrolujte si e-mail" focused on 202 with the address, the hour of validity, the spam hint and "Poslat znovu"; its own sentence on 429, `describeProblem` otherwise). An address typed on one tab follows to the other and to the reset. When the browser supports passkeys, the login tab adds under the form a "nebo" divider (with `--space-6` above and `--space-5` below, so the passkey button is not hit instead of "Přihlásit se") and "Přihlásit se přístupovým klíčem" (`AuthStore.signInWithPasskey`, both buttons disabled while it runs, the form's fields made untouched so an empty e-mail shows no error; a dismissed device prompt says "Přihlášení přístupovým klíčem neproběhlo. Zkuste to prosím znovu, nebo se přihlaste heslem." and a refusal the problem, both in a `role="alert"` line under the passkey button). The registration opens with a lead saying the name (or company name, IČO and seat) goes on the receipt, no field carries an example placeholder (they read as filled-in values; labels and hints stay, as in the code field of the code step), the visible label "Registruji se jako" over "Fyzická osoba" / "Firma", PSČ before Město, name, street and city checked with `appPrintableOnReceipt` as in the profile card, IČO checked by its mod-11 checksum (`company-identifier.ts`, `appCompanyIdentifier`: "Toto IČO neexistuje, zkontrolujte prosím číslice."); a company types the IČO first (it takes the `[autofocus]`), and once it holds 8 digits with a valid check digit the dialog asks `CompanyLookupApi.lookUp` (`features/donors/company-lookup-api.ts`, `GET /api/donors/company-lookup`, 204 read as `null`; Jakub 2026-10-24) and fills the name, street, PSČ and city that are empty or still hold an earlier lookup's value, never what the donor typed, then says "Údaje jsme doplnili z obchodního rejstříku (ARES). Zkontrolujte je." in an always rendered `role="status"` region (`.company-lookup`); a 204, any error or a lookup still running when the registration is sent, the type switches to a person or the dialog closes changes nothing and shows nothing (no spinner, no alert, the registration never waits for it), the consent above "Vytvořit účet" ("Vytvářím účet…"). When the browser supports passkeys, it asks "Jak se budete přihlašovat" with an always visible hint explaining a passkey (radio group "Heslem" / "Přístupovým klíčem"); with the key the password field gives way to a hint and the request sends `password: null`. `donor-field-messages.ts` holds the name, street, city and postal code messages shared with the profile card.
- `email-verification/` (`/potvrzeni-emailu#token=…`): reads the token once and removes it with `Location.replaceState`; posts `AuthStore.confirmEmail` at once ("Potvrzujeme váš e-mail" with a spinner and a `role="status"` line); on 200 the donor is signed in, "E-mail je potvrzený" takes focus and "Přejít do přehledu" navigates to `/darce/prehled`; a link with `returnTo=%2F%23prispet` (checked by `core/auth/confirmation-return.ts`, anything else ignored) says the QR code now carries the symbol and offers "Pokračovat k příspěvku" to `/#prispet` instead, with a second, soft link "Přejít na můj přehled" (`a.overview-link` to `/darce/prehled`) under it, and its new-link form and "Přihlásit se" keep that destination; `donors/invalid-email-verification-token` and a missing token show a form asking for the address that posts `AuthApi.requestEmailVerification` ("E-mail odeslán" on 202 with the spam hint and "Na úvodní stránku", the reset's 429 sentence, `describeProblem` otherwise); the invalid link adds "Už jste e-mail potvrdili? Pak se stačí přihlásit." with "Přihlásit se" (the dialog returning to `/darce/prehled`); another failure shows "E-mail se nepodařilo potvrdit" with the reason in `role="alert"` and "Zkusit znovu"; each panel's `<h1 id="email-verification-title" tabindex="-1">` takes focus after a switch. For an account without a password the confirmed panel asks "Teď si vytvořte přístupový klíč…" with "Vytvořit přístupový klíč" (options with `{}`, the device, `addPasskey` named by `deviceName`; then "Přístupový klíč je uložený…" and "Přejít do přehledu"), a refusal in `role="alert"` saying it can be done later, and "Přejít do přehledu bez klíče". The link of an e-mail change carries `&change=email` (`changesEmail`): the page posts it with `AuthApi.confirmEmailChange` to `/api/donors/auth/email-change/confirm` (204, no session), signs out a session the browser still holds (the change ended it on the server), and its confirmed panel says "E-mail je změněný", "Nová adresa je potvrzená. Přihlaste se jí, heslem nebo přístupovým klíčem jako dosud. Kvůli bezpečnosti jsme vás odhlásili na všech zařízeních." and offers only "Přihlásit se" (the dialog returning to `/darce/prehled`), never the passkey step.
- `connect-app/` (`/pripojeni?client_id=…&redirect_uri=…&code_challenge=…&scope=…&resource=…&state=…`, the consent page of the MCP connector, public shell): reads the OAuth request from the query (`oauth-scopes.ts` names the known scopes "Čtení: …" and "Změny: …" for the connected applications of the account card and lists, in `SCOPE_ABILITIES`, what each lets the application do in plain words for the consent); without `client_id`, `redirect_uri` or `code_challenge` it says the request is incomplete; a visitor sees "Aplikace žádá o přístup ke správě" with "Přihlásit se", which opens the dialog (staying on the page) and the page reacts to `AuthStore.status()`; a signed-in donor without `ADMIN` sees "Připojit aplikaci může jen správce" with "Přihlásit se jiným účtem" (signs out, opens the dialog, and the page goes on to the consent after the sign-in) and "Odmítnout a vrátit se do aplikace"; an administrator sees "Ověřujeme aplikaci" while `AuthApi.describeClient` runs, then the consent ("Povolit přístup ke správě aplikaci claude.ai?", who the application says it is, the administrator's name, what reading allows as a list, the changes (`admin:write`, when asked for) as the checkbox "Povolit i změny" unticked by default with their list and the hint that the extent changes only by connecting again, a paragraph "Nikdy nebude smět …" naming the web-only actions, where to disconnect (Údaje a zabezpečení → Připojené aplikace); for a loopback redirect a note that the application runs on this computer) with a fresh confirmation: for an administrator with the code from the app the field "Kód z aplikace" (`#connect-app-code`, six digits, "Povolení potvrďte kódem z ověřovací aplikace.", the hint asks to wait for the next code) and "Povolit", plus under the field "Nebo bez kódu: Povolit přístupovým klíčem" (a link button) when the browser supports passkeys; an administrator with the code who also has a passkey (`AuthStore.hasPasskeys()`, asked before the consent shows) gets the passkey first instead, with "Povolit přístupovým klíčem" as the main button and "Nebo bez přístupového klíče: Zadat kód z aplikace" (Jakub 2026-10-29); for one without the code only "Povolit přístupovým klíčem" (disabled with an explanation in a browser without passkeys), which runs `AuthStore.confirmWith(null)`; and "Odmítnout", all posting `AuthApi.authorize` (an approval carries only the granted scopes and `secondFactorCode` or `passkey`; a refused code or key, or a cancelled device, shows a `role="alert"` line in the consent and focuses the code with its text selected, so the next code replaces it) and leaving through `ExternalNavigation.assign(redirectTo)` ("Vracíme vás do aplikace…"); a refused client or a failed request shows the problem with "Zkusit znovu"; each panel's `<h1 id="connect-app-title" tabindex="-1">` takes focus after a switch.
- `password-reset/` (`/obnova-hesla#token=…`): reads the token once and removes it with `Location.replaceState`; asks for the new password once, in `<app-password-reveal>`; posts `AuthApi.confirmPasswordReset`; on 204 calls `AuthStore.logout()` if a session is held and offers the sign-in dialog; `donors/invalid-password-reset-token` and a missing token offer the dialog's reset tab (`LoginDialogService.open('reset')`); other failures show `describeProblem` in the form's alert line; each panel's `<h1 id="password-reset-title" tabindex="-1">` takes focus after a switch. With passkey support the page is "Obnovte si přístup" with a radio group "Nové heslo" / "Nový přístupový klíč"; the key form has "Název klíče" (from `deviceName`) and, after `donors/second-factor-code-required`, "Kód z aplikace" (focused, the server's sentence in the alert line); "Vytvořit klíč" posts `AuthApi.passkeyRecoveryOptions`, lets the device create the key and posts `recoverWithPasskey`, then "Přístupový klíč je uložený" offers the sign-in dialog; an administrator's refusal shows the backend's title and detail.
- `dashboard/`: `symbol-card/` (variable symbol with a copy button, "Přispět" to `/#prispet`, where the home page puts the symbol into the QR; "Přispět znovu" once the input `hasDonated` is true). `profile-card/` ("Údaje na potvrzení o daru": name, IČO for a company, address, receipt language; "Upravit údaje" turns it into a form (street, then PSČ and Město in the order of the registration) saved with "Uložit údaje" sending name, address and `preferredLocale` through `AuthStore.updateProfile` (`PATCH /api/donors/me`); e-mail and IČO read-only; focus to the first field on edit and back to the button after save or cancel; the hint says a new language reaches only receipts downloaded for the first time; name, street and city carry `appPrintableOnReceipt` (`features/donors/printable-on-receipt.ts`, `printedText` of `receipt-characters.ts`, whose spec runs the shared cases of `receipts/printable.json`), so a character no receipt prints is refused at its field before anything is sent ("Obsahuje znak, který na potvrzení o daru nevytiskneme, například emoji nebo čínské písmo. Napište prosím tento údaj latinkou, cyrilicí nebo řeckým písmem."), and a 400 with field errors puts them at their fields (`placeServerErrors`, focus to the first) and keeps only the title and detail, with any error no field owns, in the form's alert line; a `role="status"` line confirms the save; `sessionEnded` is answered like a failed load). `account-card/` ("Přihlášení a zabezpečení", Jakub 2026-10-24): one summary list (`dl.account-data`, one `.account-row` per item: label, state, one action; up to 768 px the state goes under the label and the action) with the rows E-mail (the address; while `pendingEmailChange` is set "Čeká na potvrzení: <adresa>" in amber, "Potvrzovací odkaz platí do …" and "Poslat znovu" (`AuthStore.resendEmailChange`, "Posíláme…") and the quiet "Zrušit" (`AuthStore.cancelEmailChange`, "Rušíme…", focus back to "Změnit e-mail"), their outcome in the card's status line and a refusal as an alert under them; action "Změnit e-mail"), Heslo ("Nastavené", "Bez hesla" or "Bez hesla, jen přístupové klíče"; "Změnit heslo" or "Nastavit heslo", plus the quiet "Odebrat heslo" for a donor with a password and a key), Dvoufaktorové ověření ("Zapnuté, kód z ověřovací aplikace" / "Vypnuté", with a passkey and the code off the note "Přihlášení přístupovým klíčem je chráněné i bez kódu.", for an administrator without it the amber hint "Správa se vám otevře, až se přihlásíte přístupovým klíčem nebo zapnete kód z aplikace a přihlásíte se s ním."; "Zapnout/Vypnout dvoufaktorové ověření") and Přístupové klíče ("Žádný", "1 klíč", "2 klíče", "5 klíčů"; "Spravovat přístupové klíče"). Each action is a disclosure (`aria-expanded`, `aria-controls` to the row's `dd.account-panel`, which spans the row): opening one closes any other, the focus moves to the form's first field (to the passkeys panel for keys), and cancel or success returns it to the row's button; every visible action word carries the rest of its name as hidden text. The forms are the earlier ones: "Zapnout kód z aplikace" asks the current password (`AuthStore.setupSecondFactor`) after saying what a lost phone means, since there are no backup codes (Jakub 2026-10-29): "Když telefon s aplikací ztratíte, přístup obnovíte platbou 1 Kč z bankovního účtu, který u nás máte ověřený aspoň týden. Dřív to nejde; do té doby nám napište a kód z aplikace vám vypneme." (the 7-day rule of `SecondFactorRecoveryPolicy`, the administrator's reset otherwise), then shows the QR code (`app-qr-code`, plain, 200 px, encoded in the browser from the enrolment's `otpauthUri` at level M; a URI the encoder refuses, such as one with a character outside ISO-8859-1, shows instead the alert "QR kód se nepodařilo vykreslit. Opište prosím klíč níže do ověřovací aplikace." and the key and the code field stay), the link "Přidat do ověřovací aplikace v tomto telefonu nebo tabletu" to the enrolment's `otpauthUri` (under the QR, above it up to 480 px), the key in groups of four with a copy button and the code field posting `AuthStore.confirmSecondFactor`, which adopts the new session, and the status line says the factor is on and the other sign-ins ended; "Vypnout kód z aplikace" asks the password and a code (`AuthStore.disableSecondFactor`); the e-mail form sends the new address and the current password through `AuthStore.requestEmailChange`, after 202 the status line says the link went to the new address and the current one stays until it is opened, and the store reads the profile again so the pending change shows (a failed reload changes nothing); the password form posts `AuthStore.changePassword`, which adopts the new session; refusals are form-level alerts and `sessionEnded` is answered like the profile card. For an account without a password and without a passkey (`passkeyCount` 0) the rows offer no action but "Spravovat přístupové klíče", and the amber callout "Účet zatím nemá heslo ani přístupový klíč. Nejdřív si vytvořte přístupový klíč, …" with the primary "Vytvořit přístupový klíč" opens the passkeys row with its form (no button where the browser cannot create passkeys). With a key the password form replaces every password field with "Po odeslání vás zařízení požádá o potvrzení jedním z vašich přístupových klíčů." (`AuthStore.confirmWith`). `passkeys-panel/` (`app-passkeys-panel`, the Přístupové klíče row's panel, a region named by the row label through `labelledBy`, `focusTarget()` and `collapse()` for the card; it reports the count through `countChanged`): an intro opening with the benefit ("Přihlaste se otiskem prstu, obličejem nebo zámkem obrazovky místo hesla."), naming Apple, Google and Windows Hello and saying a passkey sign-in is protected without the code from the app; the keys from `AuthApi.passkeys` with "přidáno … · naposledy použito …" or "zatím nepoužito", "Přejmenovat" opening an inline "Nový název klíče" field (prefilled, focused) with "Uložit název" / "Zrušit" posting `AuthApi.renamePasskey`; "Odebrat" with the inline "Odebrat klíč <název>? Přihlásit se jím pak nepůjde." and "Ano, odebrat" / "Ponechat"; "Přidat přístupový klíč" opens a form with "Název klíče" (prefilled from the user agent) and the current password, "Vytvořit klíč" ("Čekáme na zařízení…") asks for the options, lets the device create the key and posts it; outcomes in a `role="status"` line, refusals and the device's own answers as a form alert; without browser support a sentence replaces the button; for an account without a password the form asks no password, the only key shows "Jediný způsob přihlášení…" instead of "Odebrat", one key brings the advice to add a second device and none an amber warning; the first key of a donor with a password also says the password can now be removed at the Heslo row. `connected-apps-card/` ("Připojené aplikace", its own card after the account card, only for an administrator): `AuthApi.connections`, one row per connection with the host, "připojeno … · naposledy použito …", the permissions as a list and "Odpojit" (`AuthApi.disconnect`, "Odpojujeme…", then a `role="status"` line that the application is disconnected and cannot act any more), "Žádná aplikace není připojená. …" without one, a failed load in `role="alert"` with "Načíst znovu"; `sessionEnded` like the other cards. the card "Když web nefunguje" (`.diagnostics-card`, after "Data v tomto zařízení", for administrators too) holds `<app-diagnostics-download source="account-page">`, and the alerts of "Načíst data znovu" for `mismatch` and `no-copy` ask to attach the file from it;
