every receipt PDF failure the drawing reports (not `unprintable`, and `tools-missing` only once the reload did not help) sets `ReceiptError.offersDiagnostics`, and each place that shows the donor's receipt error (Přehled, Moje dary above the years, at a year and at a gift, both Potvrzení o daru cards) shows `<app-diagnostics-download source="receipt-pdf">` under it. `device-data-card/` ("Data v tomto zařízení", after the account card, plan v3 2.14): one state line ("Uloženo v zařízení · aktualizováno <datum> <čas>" in Prague time, the date and the time each kept whole but free to wrap apart, "Data se ukládají do zařízení…" during the first sync, "Kopie v tomto zařízení je zamčená. …" when `DeviceVault.locked()`, which also drops "Načíst data znovu", "V tomto zařízení zatím nic uloženo není.", or in memory-only mode "V tomto prohlížeči se nic neukládá. …", which also drops the sentence about the encrypted copy and the delete button), the sentence that the copy of gifts, receipts and bank accounts is encrypted with a key only this device knows, that the server does not change, and when sign-out keeps it (password: always; passkey: only when the key gives PRF), "Načíst data znovu" (`DonorCopySync.reload`, every chunk again, "Načítáme data…", disabled while running; `appNeedsConnection`, so offline it is held with the hint "Potřebuje připojení" and no request; the outcome in `app-live-notice`, `failed` blaming the connection, `mismatch` saying the server's data could not be verified ("Data ze serveru se nepodařilo ověřit. …") and `no-copy` naming info@nfnn.cz) and "Smazat data z tohoto zařízení" (danger outline), which opens `delete-device-data-dialog` ("Smazat data z tohoto zařízení?", the lead that the copy goes from this device, the donor is signed out here, the server keeps everything and the copy comes back at the next sign-in, "Smazat data a odhlásit" / "Mažeme data…" and "Zrušit", which takes the focus; `app-dialog [dismissible]="false"` while deleting, so Esc, × and the backdrop do nothing): it calls `DeviceVault.forget`, then `AuthStore.logout`, goes home and shows the signed-out notice with the reason `device-data-deleted` ("Data z tohoto zařízení jsme smazali a odhlásili jsme vás.", Q6); a failed deletion keeps the dialog open with an alert and signs nobody out. Every busy label speaks as "we" ("Ukládáme…", "Odebíráme…", "Ověřujeme kód…").
- `bank-accounts-card/` ("Moje bankovní účty", on the section Bankovní účty): a text button "Jak přiřazujeme platby?" with a round "?" icon (`aria-expanded`/`aria-controls`) expands a `role="region"` panel (`pairing-help/`, inputs `variableSymbol` and `open`, `closeRequested` on Escape), collapsed by default, answering short questions, each in at most two sentences (how a gift arrives, a bank without a symbol field (write the symbol into the message for the recipient), a payment without the symbol, a standing order with an old symbol, the answer to the question, the shared account, how to stop pairing, the exceptions: another donor's symbol, symbol 0, collection accounts; then the e-mail) in true-to-backend Czech with the donor's symbol; Esc collapses and refocuses; the text is capped at 520 px. One row per account (its number in `--text-account-number`, the large size capped at 7vw, so it stays on one line at twice the text size on a phone and breaks only when it cannot fit at all) with status label, hint, the bank's short name first in the line under the number (`.account-bank`, `BankNames`, wrapping), "ověřeno …", "vypnuto od …" (the day after `disconnectedOn`) and the gifts without a symbol (`donationCount` counts only donations paired by account), plus a line per `confirmedSymbols` entry. A row with `symbolOffer` shows `symbol-claim/` (`app-symbol-claim`: inputs `accountId`, `offer`, `donorName`, `busy`; output `answer`): a `role="group"` named by the question "Z tohoto účtu k nám chodí i platby s variabilním symbolem … (14 plateb, leden 2025 – říjen 2026). Jsou to dary od vás (Jana Nováková)?" (never an amount) with "Ano, jsou moje" (described by the note that the answer cannot be changed) and "Ne, nejsou moje"; while saving both are `aria-disabled` and the pressed one `aria-busy`; the answered offer is dropped at once. "Ano" posts `confirmSymbol`, emits `donationsChanged` when `pairedCount` is above 0 (the area refetches summary and list) and announces "Přiřadili jsme vám … plateb se symbolem … z účtu …" plus "Potvrzení za rok … se změnilo, stáhněte si nové." for `changedYearlyReceipts`; "Ne" posts `declineSymbol`. While no account is verified (`ACTIVE`) and none carries a question, a short nudge under the list says a 1 Kč from the standing order's account brings the older gifts too; an empty card says the account appears by itself once a gift with the symbol comes from it, and that a gift sent without the symbol, with another one or before the account existed needs the account added through "Přidat účet" (earlier gifts from it usually follow), then, in two sentences, that a standing order is best switched to the donor's symbol, else a 1 Kč through "Přidat účet". The public `focusTitle()` focuses the card heading; the public `openAddDialog()` (used by the section when the address carries `?pridat=ucet`, in the `read` phase of `afterNextRender` so it runs after the page title took the focus) focuses "Přidat účet" first, so closing returns there, and opens the dialog unless the donor has no symbol. A shared account's hint is an amber warning box (`account-hint--warning`, `--color-warning*`, hidden "Upozornění:") that never names the other donor, in the list and in the add dialog alike. The buttons and chips avoid the word "párování": a `pairingExcluded` account keeps its chip ("Ověřený" / "Nepřiřazujeme"); its live row says only payments with the symbol pair now; an active one offers no "Nepřiřazovat platby z tohoto účtu", an `AWAITING_PAYMENT` one reads "Jen s variabilním symbolem" and keeps that button, a disconnected one offers no "Znovu přiřazovat platby z účtu" and says why on its newest row. "Nepřiřazovat platby z tohoto účtu" (set apart by `--space-5` above it up to 768 px while the row asks a question) asks an inline confirmation "Ano, nepřiřazovat" / "Ponechat" (focus on "Ponechat"; with confirmed symbols it says those stop pairing too); "Znovu přiřazovat platby z účtu" shows only on the newest row of its account number; both reload the list, announce in `role="status"` and focus the card heading; problems go through `describeProblem` in `role="alert"`, 404/409 reload. "Přidat účet" opens an `app-dialog` with one short paragraph (send 1 Kč by the details below; the account then appears and pays without the symbol), right under it the watch box, then while nothing new is verified one line naming the accounts already verified (`ACTIVE`, not shared, not symbol-only) that need no 1 Kč ("Účet … už máte ověřený, 1 Kč z něj posílat nemusíte." / "Účty … a … už máte ověřené, …"; the dialog cannot know which account the donor means, testers of 2026-10-30), then the branded QR (`app-qr-code`, 200 px) that `spayd()` writes from `PaymentDetailsStore` with `accountVerificationAmountMinor`, the donor's symbol and `ACCOUNT_VERIFICATION` and `encodeText` draws at level H (when the details did not load the error with "Zkusit znovu", when they loaded but are refused "QR kód teď nejde vytvořit. Zkuste to prosím znovu za chvíli.", both retrying through `PaymentDetailsStore.reload()`; when the donor's own variable symbol is what `spayd()` refuses, "QR kód teď nejde vytvořit. Napište nám prosím na info@nfnn.cz." with no retry, which could not help), and account, amount, message "Ověření účtu Nadační fond Nová naděje" and symbol, each but the amount with a copy button (up to 480 px the details come before the QR), under them the line that a bank without a field for the variable symbol takes it in the message ("Ověření účtu Nadační fond Nová naděje <symbol>", pairing rule 9), and a closed `<details>` "Kdy to funguje a co se stane s dřívějšími dary" (Czech accounts only, no collection account, earlier gifts for the first verifier, a standing order with an older symbol needs exactly 1 Kč with that message, the timing); it watches in the `role="status"` box ("Čekáme na vaši platbu…" with a spinner): 30 s after opening and 30 s after each check ended (the backend's global pause) it runs `checkBankUntilDone()` and reloads the list, emitting `bankChecked`; an account `ACTIVE` now that was not when the dialog opened turns the box into a drawn tick "Účet … je ověřený" (plus "Přiřadili jsme vám také N dřívější dary z tohoto účtu, celkem … Kč." from the row's `donationCount` and `totalAmountMinor` when it brought any) and ends the watch, and so does a changed `verificationPaymentOn` ("Platba z účtu … dorazila"); the result then replaces the intro, the QR, the payment details and the `<details>`, takes the focus (`tabindex="-1"`) and the close button reads "Hotovo"; with a `symbolOffer` the dialog asks the question itself (`app-symbol-claim` under the box, hidden from that account's row meanwhile, so it stands once) and announces the answer in its own `role="status"` line, which takes the focus (a refused answer shows its `role="alert"` in the dialog); a hidden tab pauses and checks at once when visible; while the dialog is open a `bank-accounts` hint reads the list at once, so a verification the hourly import or another check brought shows without waiting for the next 30 s, and a tick ends the watch; after 20 checks "Platba zatím nedorazila" with "Kontrolovat dál"; animations stop under `prefers-reduced-motion`. A payment paired by `SENDER_ACCOUNT` carries the tag "bez symbolu · účet …3333/2010" in its purpose cell and one paired by `CONFIRMED_SYMBOL` "symbol 6123456789 · účet …5399/0800" (the full sentence as hidden text, no `title`).
- Donor area (`donor-area`, 2.23): the app header and the tab bar of the public pages (Layout), the header's top padding as room above the content and `--tab-bar-clearance` below it; on wide screens the side panel "Můj účet" with the sections Můj přehled, Moje dary, Potvrzení o daru, Bankovní účty and Údaje a zabezpečení, and for an administrator the group "Pro správce" with Správa, around the routed section; on phones (`CompactLayout.isCompact()`, the `side-nav` with `[panel]="false"`, so one `router-outlet` serves both widths and a page keeps its state when an iPad turns) no panel, the account sheet leads between the sections and the header's "Zpět" leads home; on phones (`CompactLayout.isCompact()`) a section chosen in the account sheet opens inside it as the next step of a stack, after the Music app (Jakub 2026-11-11, option a): `AccountSection` renders the section's page from `DONOR_SECTION_PAGES` of `donors.routes.ts` with its own `DonorDonations` and `PAGE_IN_SHEET`, so `PageTitle` leaves out its `h1`, the sheet's header shows a round Zpět on the left and the section's title in the middle, which takes the focus, and no ×; Zpět, the edge swipe and the browser's Back return to the list with the focus on the row the donor came from, and the next Back closes the sheet; the address of the page under the sheet does not change and the browser gains no entry; a link inside the section to another section opens that one in the sheet, any other link closes the sheet and opens its page; a step back while a form of the section is dirty (`form.ng-dirty`) keeps the section and asks "Zahodit rozepsané údaje?" (Pokračovat v psaní, Zahodit); dragging the sheet down or Esc closes it whole; Správa, wide screens and a `/darce/…` address opened directly stay pages; the push animation of the stack waits for the shared back transition; the routed section and the help line "Máte dotaz k platbě? Napište nám na info@nfnn.cz" (Jakub 2026-10-30 kept it without a second link to the contact form, because two colour-only links in one line would need underlining). It provides `donor-donations.ts` (`DonorDonations`, one per visit of the area, so switching sections loads nothing again and a new sign-in starts empty; only opening Přehled or Moje dary more than 30 s after the last bank check ended (the backend's pause, measured from the device's clock) starts the same silent check as opening the area, through `checkBankIfStale()`, and nothing while one runs): the bank accounts from `BankAccountsApi.list()` once on open (a failure leaves none; the bank accounts card replaces them with every list it shows or answer it records, through its output `accountsChanged`), whose accounts with a `symbolOffer` are the overview's question callouts and the badge of "Bankovní účty" in the side panel and, through `AccountBadges`, on the account button and the sheet's row (Jakub 2026-10-24: the count, read as "1 otázka čeká na odpověď", "2 otázky čekají na odpověď", "5 otázek čeká na odpověď" through `pluralCategory`); summary and list from `DonationsApi` on open (`state` loading/ready/error with retry, shown by `donations-pending/`; a 401 `auth/unauthenticated` or a session lost by a failed refresh sends the donor home with the sign-in dialog, returning to the same section), the bank check (on open and on "Zkontrolovat nové platby" it posts `requestBankCheck`, reads it again while `checking` as `checkBankUntilDone()` does, refetches summary and list whenever a check ends, keeps `checkingBank` until they arrived, ignores an answer overtaken by a later fetch, and counts `bankDataArrived` when `bankDataAsOf` changed or the donor asked, so the bank accounts section reloads its list; after "Zkontrolovat nové platby" `bankCheckOutcome` says how many `RECEIVED` gifts are new, none, that the bank is still busy after the last poll, or that the check failed). `bank-check/` (`app-bank-check`, on the overview's bank line and in Moje dary) shows "Kontrolujeme nové platby v bance…" or the outcome ("Přibyl 1 nový dar." / "Přibyly 3 nové dary." / "Přibylo 5 nových darů.", "Žádný nový dar zatím nepřišel. Banky se ptáme nejvýš jednou za 30 sekund, zkuste to proto za chvíli znovu." (the backend's `on-demand-cooldown`), "Banka zatím neodpověděla. Zkuste to prosím za chvíli znovu.") before "Údaje z banky k …" in one always rendered `role="status"` line, the button "Zkontrolovat nové platby" (disabled while checking) and, for a failed check, the `role="alert"` "Nové platby se nepodařilo zkontrolovat. Zkuste to prosím za chvíli znovu."; leaving the section clears the outcome. From the device copy (2.15): `DonorDonations` reads summary and gifts only through `DonorDonationsRepository` (`features/donations/device-copy/donor-donations-repository.ts`, root): `held()` is the open copy's view while it agrees with its summary (a copy that disagrees counts as absent, so the page reads the API instead of a list that contradicts its totals), and `fresh('catch-up' | 'sync')` answers from the copy after that catch-up or sync stored it and from the API (`/api/donations/me/summary` and `/api/donations/me`) when there is no open copy, when it is still empty (its catch-up then runs beside) or when the sync did not store it, so a sync that fails, times out or finds the session gone never leaves the page without data. A copy that holds the gifts is shown at once and caught up, and what the catch-up brings waits behind the "Zobrazit N nový dar" button like a live hint; the reload that ends an automatic bank check (on opening the area) offers its gifts the same way while the page shows the copy, so a catch-up's button is not replaced by a list that moves; every other reload (the end of a bank check the donor asked for, a receipt whose row is gone) syncs and shows its answer directly; a copy that opens after the page asked the API is shown as soon as it opens (also after the API failed), unless the API's answer on the page is newer than the copy; a copy that opens for another donor than the one the page showed (a session that changed donor in place; a read from the API names the donor signed in when it was asked) replaces the list at once instead of offering its gifts as new ones; a failed reload keeps a list shown from the copy instead of the error. A receipt is drawn through `copyBackedReceiptSource`, so the copy's active receipt and its kept images are drawn again when the server cannot be reached (status 0, or a gateway status 502–504 or 520–524 without a problem body). The bank accounts still come from the API (2.15 kept the Bankovní účty page on it). `first-sync-notice/` (`app-first-sync-notice`, in the donor shell, an always rendered `aria-live="polite"` region without `role="status"`, as `update-notice`): when the first sync of an empty copy takes longer than a second, a bar fixed at the bottom of the screen (`ui-notice--floating` in a neutral look, the page kept clear of it through `keepRoomForFloatingNotice`, so nothing on the page moves) says "Ukládáme přehled vašich darů do tohoto zařízení…", and when it stored the copy, in the status look, "Přehled vašich darů je uložený v tomto zařízení." for 4 s; nothing for a quick sync, a failed one, a copy that already held the gifts or a device that keeps nothing (memory only, "Cizí počítač"). It promises nothing offline: until the copy opens without a connection (after 2.7), the offline-unlock PR adds that sentence under a new id. Live hints (2.16): on `donations` `DonorDonations` reads summary and list (with a copy: syncs it) in the background and shows them at once unless they bring gifts the page does not show yet, which wait behind a button at the end of the same status line, "Zobrazit 1 nový dar" / "Zobrazit 3 nové dary" / "Zobrazit 5 nových darů" (`incomingCount`, `showIncoming()`, the focus then on "Zkontrolovat nové platby"), so the list never moves under the donor's finger; while a bank check runs the button is hidden (its end reloads everything anyway, and the focus would land on the disabled check button); a check the donor starts, or any full reload, shows everything at once and drops the button. On `bank-accounts` it reads the bank accounts again (the badge and the questions), on `bank-import` the time of the bank data (`GET` of the bank check, unless a check runs), and after a reopened stream all three. `missing-gift/` (`app-missing-gift`, in the empty states of Přehled (at the top of "Jak poslat první dar", above its steps, so a donor who already paid reads it first), Moje dary and Potvrzení o daru): the `h3` "Poslali jste dar a nevidíte ho?", two sentences (a payment without the symbol, with another one or from before the account is not paired yet; verifying the account it came from with 1 Kč usually brings the earlier gifts) and "Přidat účet, ze kterého jste platili" to `/darce/ucty?pridat=ucet`, which opens the add dialog and drops the query (`replaceUrl`). `standing-order/` (`app-standing-order`, input `variableSymbol`): the text button "Jak nastavit trvalý příkaz" (`aria-expanded`/`aria-controls`) opens a `role="region"` named by it, scrolls the button with the opened guide into view (`scrollIntoView({ block: 'nearest' })` after render, so the opening never happens below the screen), with the account number (input `account` from the card's `app-payment-panel`, otherwise, as in Moje dary, from `PaymentDetailsStore`; "Načítáme číslo účtu…", on failure a `role="alert"` "Číslo účtu se nepodařilo načíst. Zkuste to prosím znovu." with "Zkusit znovu", which loads again or, with `account`, emits `retryRequested` for the card), the donor's symbol, both with copy buttons, "<amount> Kč, nebo jiná, kterou chcete posílat" (input `amount` from the card's panel) or, without an amount, "Jakákoli, kterou chcete posílat", "Každý měsíc, nebo jak často chcete", that every payment then appears by itself and counts in the yearly receipt, that the amount and the day are changed only in the donor's bank and the site needs no setting, and for an older symbol the advice to switch it or verify the account under "Bankovní účty" (link); Esc closes and refocuses the button., the receipt is drawn in the browser by `ReceiptPdf` (plan 1.9; a running one marks its button `aria-disabled="true"` and `aria-busy` (the button keeps the keyboard focus, so a drawing of several seconds never drops it to the page, and a press meanwhile starts nothing); `yearlyProgress(year)` / `singleProgress(item)` hand its state to `<app-download-progress class="receipt-saved">` under its button in the table row, the year heading of Moje dary, the year row of Potvrzení o daru and the card of Přehled: "Připravujeme údaje potvrzení…", "Načítáme nástroje pro PDF…", "Vytváříme PDF…" (the status line changes only per phase, so a screen reader hears three announcements, not one per page; the bar moves by page against `estimatedReceiptPages(gifts)` of `receipt-document.ts`, which the layout spec holds to the real page count of every case, with "Strana N" beside it as its `aria-valuetext`), then `PdfDelivery.save` and "Potvrzení <file> je stažené. Najdete ho ve stažených souborech.", or on an iPhone or iPad that shares files "Potvrzení <file> je připravené. Otevřete ho tlačítkem Otevřít potvrzení." (under a payment's row "… tlačítkem Otevřít PDF.") while the same button now reads "Otevřít potvrzení za rok <year> (PDF)" (in the payments table "Otevřít PDF") and takes the focus unless the donor moved it elsewhere while the PDF was drawn (after a save, a failure, a cancel and on ready the button gets the focus back only when it fell to the page or still stands where it stood when the download started); a ready file is dropped, with the finished lines, as soon as the signed-in donor's name or address changes (`AuthStore.donor`), because the next issue replaces that receipt, while a change of the language keeps it; tapping it hands the file to the share sheet and issues nothing again ("Potvrzení <file> jste předali z nabídky sdílení."; "… je stažené …" when the browser saved it instead or sharing failed; a closed sheet keeps it ready); "Zrušit" from 8 s on ends it ("Přípravu potvrzení jste zrušili.", the `cancelled` state in the neutral colour) and returns the focus to the download button; every download keeps its own line (`receiptTasks`, one per year or gift), and a new download clears only the finished lines of others; `tools-missing` reloads the page once through `ReceiptRestart`, and after its first load the section resumes the download named by `?stahnout=` only when this tab's `sessionStorage` marker names that same download (`ReceiptRestart.resumes`), so a link with the parameter issues nothing; it removes the parameter either way; every other end of that download (saved, failed for another reason, cancelled) clears the marker (`ReceiptRestart.finished`, `abandoned(download)`), so a later link cannot issue it without a click; a miss after that one reload (`tryRestart` answers `reloaded-already`) says "Nástroje pro PDF se nepodařilo načíst ani po obnovení stránky. Zkontrolujte prosím připojení nebo blokování skriptů, zkuste jiný prohlížeč, nebo nám napište na info@nfnn.cz.", and a browser without session storage (`unavailable`, no reload) "Web se mezitím aktualizoval. Obnovte prosím stránku a stáhněte potvrzení znovu.", an image that fails its digest "Podpis na potvrzení se nepodařilo ověřit, a tak jsme PDF nevytvořili…", a drawing failure "PDF se v prohlížeči nepodařilo vytvořit…") and their failures (`receiptError` with its scope: the problem title for `donations/year-not-yet-completed`, `donations/yearly-receipt-not-available` and `donations/receipt-before-first-year`, the title of a 404 or `donations/receipt-before-first-year` on a single receipt after which summary and list are fetched again, the title and detail of `donations/receipt-unprintable`, of `donations/receipt-signing-paused` (slice 1.13), of `donations/receipt-signing-key-unpublished` (slice 1.22) and of `donations/receipt-signatory-missing`, `donations/receipt-issue-limit` and `donations/receipt-too-long` (slice 1.21: each says why a retry cannot help now and what to do instead), else "Potvrzení se nepodařilo stáhnout. Zkuste to prosím znovu."). Every section starts with `page-title/` (`<h1 tabindex="-1">`, focused on open). `overview/` (Přehled), the same order on phones and desktops (Jakub 2026-10-24): first the notices, a card "Potvrzení o darech za rok … je připravené" with "Stáhnout potvrzení za rok … (PDF)" when the donor gave in `yearlyReceiptsAvailableThrough` and that year is not before `receiptsAvailableFrom`, and an amber callout per bank account with a `symbolOffer` ("Máme na vás otázku k platbám z účtu …" with "Odpovědět" to `/darce/ucty`, from the area's bank accounts); then for a donor with gifts (an account verification alone is none, Jakub 2026-10-29) the card "Poslední dar" (the amount, date and state of the newest item that is not an account verification, then `app-bank-check` with the note on the hourly search and bank delays), the card "Poslat dar" (`send-gift/`, Jakub 2026-10-29: the `h2`, a lead that the symbol is already in the details and the QR, `app-payment-panel` with the donor's symbol and the standing order guide fed by it, projected right after the bank details and before the QR (testers of 2026-10-30 missed it under the QR); one instance outside the state branches, so loading the donations never asks for the QR again; it replaced the symbol card and every link to `/#prispet`), one row of stats as a `dl` without icons (Celkem darováno, Počet darů (the summary's count without the `RECEIVED` account verifications, which a smaller "a ověření účtu" names under it), "Darováno v roce …" for the year after `yearlyReceiptsAvailableThrough`, 0 Kč without a gift that year; three columns, up to 640 px three lines of label and value) and "Poslední dary" (the newest five through `donations-table/` without the receipt column, "Všechny dary" to `/darce/dary`); for a donor without a gift "Jak poslat první dar" takes the place of the last gift and the stats (three steps pointing to the card "Poslat dar" below and naming the donor's symbol, then the bank line), followed by "Poslat dar"; while loading or after a failure the loading line or its alert stands above "Poslat dar". Last comes `install-card/` (`app-install-card`, plan v3 2.4, only while `AppInstall.offered()`): the `region` "Nová naděje jako aplikace" with a lead, for `ios-share` and `mac-dock` the steps in Safari and that the app asks to sign in once more (an app added to the Home Screen keeps its own storage), for `prompt` "Nainstalovat aplikaci", which opens the browser's install question, and "Teď ne", which hides the offer for 90 days; it never takes the focus as it appears (it can come at any moment, when the browser fires `beforeinstallprompt`), and after either button the card is replaced by its closing line ("Aplikace se instaluje…" or "Dobře, nabídku aplikace teď skryjeme…"), which takes the focus, so it is not lost with the pressed button. It is offered only to a signed-in donor (plan 2.1), never on the public site. `gifts/` (Moje dary): "Všechny dary" with `app-bank-check` and, with gifts, the sentence that single receipts come at once, the yearly one from 1 January of the next year and only one kind goes into the tax return (the yearly one holds every gift of the year), then the gifts grouped by year, newest first (Jakub 2026-10-24): each year a `section` named by its `h3` (the year) with "3 dary · 1 500 Kč" from the summary's `byYear` (through `gift-count/` (`app-gift-count`, also in the year rows of Potvrzení o daru; `receivedVerifications(items, year)` counts the `RECEIVED` account verifications, which are named apart: "1 dar a ověření účtu · 2 001 Kč", "Ověření účtu · 1 Kč" alone, their amount staying in the sum) and, by `yearReceipt(year, summary)` (`dashboard/yearly-receipt.ts`, also used by Potvrzení o daru, from `yearlyReceiptsAvailableThrough` and `receiptsAvailableFrom`, never the client clock), "Stáhnout potvrzení za rok … (PDF)" for a year with its receipt, "Roční potvrzení za rok … stáhnete po skončení roku, od 1. ledna …." for a later year and nothing before the first receipt year; the year's gifts through `donations-table/` named by the year heading, with "Stáhnout PDF" where `receiptAvailable` (a gift on the active yearly receipt of its year says "Je na ročním potvrzení za rok …" instead, #467: `DonorDonations.onYearlyReceipt`, from the yearly receipts the device copy holds, matched to the gifts by day and amount in `giftsOnActiveYearlyReceipts` (`dashboard/yearly-receipt.ts`), or after the server refused that gift with 409 `donations/donation-on-yearly-receipt`, whose title and detail the row's alert shows while the payments load again; a `REFUNDED` row says "Vrácený dar potvrzení nemá", an account verification "Započítá se do ročního potvrzení", one before the first receipt year "Potvrzení vystavujeme od roku …", and a line under the list says from when receipts are issued, verifications aside), and in the footer the note on the hourly search and bank delays and the standing order guide; without gifts "Zatím tu není žádná platba." and the missing gift block; a failed download shows its alert where it was started, a yearly one under that year's heading and a single one in the gift's receipt cell (`app-donations-table` input `errorsAtRows`, through `DonorDonations.yearlyError` and `singleError`, the error carrying the download key; Jakub 2026-10-31), so the page does not move; only when that year or gift is gone after the payments load again does the alert stand above the years and scroll into view. `receipts/` (Potvrzení o daru): an intro under the heading (single receipts at once, the yearly one from 1 January of the next year, and for the tax return either the single ones or the yearly one, not both, since the yearly one holds every gift of the year; after a change of name or address a newly downloaded receipt gets a new number and replaces the earlier one), then "Roční potvrzení o darech" as one row per summary year (year, count through `app-gift-count`, total) with "Stáhnout potvrzení za rok … (PDF)" (download icon `aria-hidden`) up to `yearlyReceiptsAvailableThrough`, "Ke stažení od 1. ledna …" (the next year) after it and "Za tento rok potvrzení na webu nevystavujeme. Potřebujete ho? Napište nám na info@nfnn.cz." before `receiptsAvailableFrom` (plus the line from which year receipts are issued; without such a year the line "Potvrzení tu vystavujeme za dary od roku …. Za dřívější dary nám napište na info@nfnn.cz." instead, `.yearly-earlier`), no calendar in the frontend, and without gifts a line that a gift with the symbol appears here plus the missing gift block; the card "Potvrzení k jednotlivému daru" lists the gifts with `receiptAvailable` of every year whose yearly receipt is not out yet (`yearReceipt` is `after-year-end`, so the running year) through `donations-table/` named by its heading, with "Stáhnout PDF" and its saved line per row and a failed single download as its alert (Jakub 2026-10-30: testers looked for these receipts on this page first; Moje dary keeps them too), and says older gifts' receipts are under Moje dary; without such a gift it only says single receipts are under Moje dary. Last, always shown (also while the donations load), the card "Ověřit potvrzení" (Jakub 2026-10-31: one entry in the receipts section, not a link per receipt) says the donor can verify their own receipt or one someone showed them and links to `/overit` with a `ui-button`. `bank-accounts/` (Bankovní účty): an intro and the bank accounts card wired to the area's state. `details/` (Údaje a zabezpečení): the profile card, the account card (with the passkeys panel inside), the devices card (`devices-card/`, "Přihlášená zařízení", 2.9: `AuthApi.devices()` once on open, each row a `ui-list` row with the platform's outline (phone, tablet, computer) as its lead, named by `deviceName()` of `device-labels.ts` from the closed platform and browser lists ("iPhone · Safari", "Neznámé zařízení" when both are other), or "iPhone · aplikace" for a device whose `session` is `APP`, the installed app (Jakub 2026-11-11), with the last use and, below it, the sign-in date and the kind of the other sessions ("zapamatované přihlášení", "do zavření prohlížeče", "cizí počítač"); `groupDevices()` of `device-groups.ts` puts this device first and the others by their last use, newest first, and folds the ones last used more than 30 days (`OLDER_AFTER_DAYS`) before the newest use in the list under a row "Starší (N)" with `aria-expanded`, collapsed at open (the newest use is the server's clock, never the device's), and a narrow card (`bp.container-down(devices, pager)`, large text) puts the row's action under its text; "Toto zařízení" as a badge on the current one and "Odhlásit" (aria-label "Odhlásit {name}") on every other, "Odhlásit všechna ostatní zařízení" while there is another, "Jinde přihlášení nejste." when there is none; each sign-out's status line advises changing the password when the donor did not sign in there, and for an administrator the line after "Odhlásit všechna ostatní zařízení" says the connected applications stay connected (Q12); a device the server no longer finds (`donors/device-not-found`, signed out elsewhere) leaves the list like a signed-out one; focus moves to the next row's "Odhlásit", else to "Odhlásit všechna ostatní zařízení", else to the card's heading (`tabindex="-1"`); a failure is an alert with "Načíst znovu" for the list, and a 401 `auth/session-ended` ends the session through its output), for an administrator the connected-apps card, and "Smazání účtu" (a donor cannot delete the account: an e-mail to info@nfnn.cz with a prefilled subject, what is erased and what stays for accounting, and to download the receipts first). `donations-table/`: input `labelledBy` names the table by its card heading; every payment cell carries `data-label`, so up to 640 px each row is a card (date and amount without their labels); amounts right-aligned with `--numeric-figures`, bold in the text colour (Jakub 2026-10-29: amounts in the donor overview, Moje dary, Potvrzení o daru, the administration's payments and the donor card are bold `--color-text`, the accent stays for links and buttons); a row with `accountVerification` (Jakub 2026-10-29) is secondary: muted text and a smaller, lighter amount, its purpose "Ověření účtu" as the API names it; each "Stáhnout PDF" carries the gift's date and amount as hidden text.

## admin

- `core/auth/admin-guard.ts` on `/sprava/**`: restores the session first; an administrator passes, a signed-in donor without the role lands on `/darce/prehled`, and the guard tells `core/auth/admin-access-refusal.ts` (`AdminAccessRefusal`, a root signal) why, so the overview opens with a warning callout in an always-rendered `role="status"`: "Tento účet nemá přístup do správy. Pokud ho potřebujete, požádejte jiného správce, aby vám ho přidělil.", or, for an administrator signed in without the second factor (`secondFactor.neededForAdministration`), "Správa se vám otevře, až se přihlásíte přístupovým klíčem nebo kódem z aplikace. Kód z aplikace si zapnete v části Údaje a zabezpečení." with a link to `/darce/udaje`; leaving the overview clears it; a visitor goes to `/` with the sign-in dialog open and returns to the asked page after signing in (like `donor-guard.ts`); the guard opens it with `forAdministration: true`, so the login tab's lead says "Pro vstup do správy se přihlaste." instead of "Přístup do vašeho přehledu příspěvků".
- `shell/admin-shell` is the parent route of every `/sprava` page (`admin.routes.ts`, plan v3 2.24, the approved mockup `ucet-a-sprava-mobil-po-revizi-2026-10-31`): the app header and the site's tab bar of the public pages and the donor area (one app, Jakub 2026-10-31; the route `sprava` has `head: 'light'`, `parent: '/'` and `backOnWide: false`), `DIALOG_PRESENTATION` `sheet`, so every `presentation="auto"` dialog of the administration is a sheet below `nav`, and between them `<app-side-nav navId="admin-nav" label="Správa" [panel]="!compact()">` around the `router-outlet`, the panel only above `nav`. `shell/admin-sections.ts` holds `ADMIN_SECTIONS`, the panel: "Přehled" (`/sprava`, exact), then groups by how often an administrator needs them (Jakub 2026-10-24): `ADMIN_DAILY` Každý den ("Platby z banky", "Dárci", "Aktuality", "Pomáháme"), `ADMIN_OCCASIONAL` Občas ("Hromadná potvrzení", once a year and so first there, slice 2.29, "Fotka v sekci O nás", "Lidé fondu", "Správci", "Měsíční e-mail", "Zálohy", "Upozornění", "Historie změn"), `ADMIN_TECHNICAL` Pro technika ("Nastavení", "Logy a diagnostika", "Odpovědi o starších symbolech", "Loga bank"); a new section adds its entry there (texts `admin.nav.*`) and its route a `parent`. Every child route names its `parent` for the header's back button: the daily sections `/sprava`, a donor `/sprava/darci`, an editor its list, the occasional and technical pages `/sprava/vice`, so every section is two taps from the overview (from a daily section to an occasional one it is three: Back, the row, the section) and Back retraces them. `shell/administration-lock.ts`: the token `ADMINISTRATION_LOCK` (`{ locked, panel, keepsPage, firstDownload }`, unlocked with no panel by default; `admin.routes.ts` provides it on the shell route from `lock/administration-lock-service.ts`, slice 2.19): while `locked()` the shell renders `panel()` (a signal, `NgComponentOutlet`) instead of the page, with the header and the tabs kept; locking removes the `router-outlet`, except when `keepsPage()` (a form was half-typed, `DirtyForms.any()`, at the moment it locked): then the page stays rendered, `hidden` and `inert`, under the panel and is there again, text and all, after the unlock. The kept page sits in `[appSuspendsDialogs]` (`shared/ui/dialog/dialog-suspension.ts`, the token `DIALOG_SUSPENSION`, `false` by default): a dialog of the page closes its native `<dialog>` while locked, without being dismissed, and opens again at the unlock, so a hidden modal never blocks the panel; a navigation while locked asks `unsavedChangesGuard`, which lets a page that is not rendered go (`null`) and lets a kept editor's question wait behind the panel. The panel's heading takes the focus when it appears; at the unlock the shell gives the focus back to the field of the kept page that had it, else to the page's `h1`. While unlocked an always rendered `aria-live` region `.admin-first-download` shows "Ukládáme správu do zařízení" with a progress bar and "n z m" during the first download of the administration's copy (`firstDownload()`). `lock/`: `administration-lock-service.ts` (`locked` = an administrator and `AdministrationAccess` not unlocked, `passkeyUnlocks()`, `unlockWithPasskey()`, `accountPasskeyUnlocks()` (the account holds a passkey, `GET /api/donors/me/passkeys`), `unlockWithAccountPasskey()` (a passkey ceremony with PRF over a sign-in challenge, `POST …/unlock` with `{passkey}`, then the access unlocks and the vault's `unlockAdministrationWithPrf` replaces a copy no passkey opens with a fresh one under that passkey, each only while the same administrator is still signed in; a cancelled ceremony sends nothing and the PRF output is zeroed whatever happens), `unlockWithPassword(password, code)`: the server checks both, then the access unlocks and the vault opens the copy with the password, each only while the same administrator is still signed in; `signOut()` keeps `locked` through `logout()` and the navigation home, so a kept page never shows on the way out) and `administration-lock-panel` ("Správa je zamčená": "Odemknout přístupovým klíčem" first when the copy has a PRF wrap, never asked for before the press, and otherwise, when the account has a passkey, the same button as the main way through the server (`appNeedsConnection`, disabled offline, Jakub 2026-11-06), "Použít heslo" beside it or as the main button, disabled offline with "Jste offline…", the password view "Odemknout heslem" with `#zamek-heslo`, the hidden user name and `#zamek-kod`, "Odhlásit se z tohoto zařízení", and only signing out when neither way can unlock). `device-copy/`: `administration-sync-api.ts` (`/api/admin/donations/sync`, `/api/admin/donors/sync` with their `/chunks`, `POST …/devices/current/administration-copy` and `/unlock`) and `administration-copy-download.ts` (`AdministrationCopyDownload`, loaded by `device-copies.ts` for an administrator: when the vault opens the administration copy it records a first copy stored on the device before downloading it, downloads both manifests and their chunks as `donations/<id>` and `donors/<id>`, checks every digest twice at most, reports `device-copy-mismatch`, clears the copy on `administrator: false`, keeps it on a 403, and shows `firstDownload` only for a copy that opened empty) and `administration-copy.ts` (`AdministrationCopy`, root: what the pages read from the open administration copy; `rows(part, kind)` gives one signal per part and kind, the same signal at every call, with the rows of every chunk `<part>/<kind>` or `<part>/<kind>:…` in the order of the chunk ids and each chunk's rows as the server listed them, `null` while no copy is open and loaded (locked, downloading for the first time, empty or damaged) and `[]` for a loaded copy without that kind, so a page can choose between the copy and the API; `summary` the donations summary `{ today, byYear }` and `storedAt` the time of the last download, both `null` while nothing is loaded). `shell/section-rows/` (`app-admin-section-rows`, inputs `label` and `groups` of `SectionRowGroup { id, label, rows: SectionRow { path, label, icon?, badge? } }`): a `nav` of titled groups (`section` named by its `h2`), each a `ui-list ui-list--inset` of row links with an optional inline icon (`payments`, `donors`, `news`, `projects`, `more`), the title, an amber count pill `.ui-list__count--warning` (`aria-hidden`, its description visually hidden, only above 0) and the chevron. `more/` (`/sprava/vice`, "Občas a pro technika", `h1` focused on open): the same rows, `nav` "Další sekce správy", for `ADMIN_OCCASIONAL` and `ADMIN_TECHNICAL`. The pages render no "← Správa" link; the crumbs of a donor and of the news and project editors show only above `nav`, where no back button stands; their `.container` is left-aligned beside the panel.
- `admin-api.ts`: `importState()` → `GET /api/admin/donations/import-state` (`ImportStateResponse`, `ImportFailure`, `ImportFailureKind` as in the contract); `payments(pairing, year)` → `GET /api/admin/donations/payments` (`AdminPaymentListResponse`, `AdminPaymentResponse`, `PairedDonationResponse`, `PairingMethod`), `searchDonors(query)` → `GET /api/admin/donations/donors` (`AdminDonorSearchResponse`, `AdminDonorResponse`), `pairPayment(paymentId, donorId)` and `unpairPayment(paymentId)` → the two `POST`s (`PairPaymentResponse`, `UnpairPaymentResponse`); `administrators()` → `GET /api/admin/donors/administrators` (`AdministratorListResponse`, `AdministratorResponse`), `grantAdministration(email)` → `POST` there, `revokeAdministration(donorId)` → `DELETE .../administrators/{id}`, `resetSecondFactor(donorId)` → `POST /api/admin/donors/{id}/second-factor/reset`; `symbolClaims()` → `GET /api/admin/donations/symbol-claims` (`SymbolClaimListResponse`, `SymbolClaimResponse`, `SymbolClaimAnswer`), `reverseSymbolClaim(id)` → `POST .../symbol-claims/{id}/reverse`, `deleteSymbolClaim(id)` → `DELETE .../symbol-claims/{id}`; `adminActions(before?, limit = 50)` → `GET /api/admin/donors/actions` (`AdminActionListResponse`, `AdminActionResponse`); `donors(query)` → `GET /api/admin/donors` (`DonorAccountListResponse`, `DonorAccountResponse`, `AddressResponse`), `donor(donorId)` → `GET /api/admin/donors/{id}`, `donorDonations(donorId)` → `GET /api/admin/donations/donors/{id}` (`AdminDonorDetailResponse`, `AdminDonorRecordResponse`, `AdminReceiptResponse`, `ReceiptKind`; the accounts and donations reuse `BankAccountItem` and `DonationListResponse` of the donor features), `disconnectDonorAccount`, `reconnectDonorAccount` and `removeDonorAccount(donorId, linkId)` → the two `POST`s and the `DELETE` under `.../bank-accounts/{linkId}`; `report()` → `GET /api/admin/donations/report` (`AdminReportResponse`, `ReportSettingsResponse`, `ReportSectionsResponse`, `SentReportResponse`, `NextReportResponse`), `changeReportSettings(request)` → `PUT .../report/settings` (`ReportSettingsRequest`), `sendReport(month)` → `POST .../report/send` (`SendReportResponse`), `overview(year)` → `GET /api/admin/donations/overview` (`AdminOverviewResponse`, `MonthOverviewResponse`, `TallyResponse`), `backups()` → `GET /api/admin/operations/backups` (`AdminBackupsResponse`, `BackupFileResponse`, `BackupRunResponse`, `RestoreFileResponse`, `BackupKind`, `RunOutcome`, `RestoreFileState`, `BackupProblem`), `requestBackup(kind)` → `POST .../backups/requests` (`BackupRequestResponse`), `restoreBackup(fileName)` → `POST .../backups/{fileName}/restore` (`RestoreRequestResponse`), `downloadBackup(fileName)` → `GET .../backups/{fileName}` as a blob turned into a `DownloadedFile` by `downloadedFile` from `donations-api.ts`; `logFiles()` → `GET /api/admin/operations/logs` (`LogFilesResponse`, `LogFileResponse`), `logWindow(name, from, bytes)` → `GET .../logs/{name}` (`LogWindowResponse`), `searchLog(name, pattern, before, limit)` → `GET .../logs/{name}/search` (`LogSearchResponse`, `LogMatchResponse`), `logLevels()` → `GET /api/admin/operations/loggers` (`LogLevelsResponse`, `LoggerResponse`, `LogOverrideResponse`, `LogLevel`), `setLogLevel(logger, level, minutes)` → `PUT .../loggers/{logger}`, `resetLogLevel(logger)` → `DELETE` there, `diagnostics()` → `GET /api/admin/operations/diagnostics` (`DiagnosticsResponse`, `JvmDiagnosticsResponse`, `DatabaseDiagnosticsResponse`, `DiskSpaceResponse`), `threadDump()` → `GET .../diagnostics/threads` as text, `push()` → `GET /api/admin/operations/push` (`AdminPushResponse`, `PushSubscriptionResponse`, `OpenAlertResponse`, `PushAlertSwitches`, `PushAlertArea`, `PushService`), `subscribePush(request)` → `POST .../push/subscriptions` (`PushSubscriptionRequest`), `changePushAlerts(id, alerts)` → `PUT .../push/subscriptions/{id}/alerts`, `removePushSubscription(id)` → `DELETE .../push/subscriptions/{id}`, `testPush()` → `POST .../push/test` (`PushTestResponse`), `settings()` → `GET /api/admin/operations/settings` (`AdminSettingsResponse`, `AdminSettingResponse`, `SettingGroup`), `changeSettings(changes)` → `PUT` there (`SettingsSavedResponse`), `sendTestEmail(request)` → `POST .../settings/test-email` (`AdminTestEmailRequest`: `MailServerRequest` plus `sender`; `TestEmailResponse`), `receiptSignatory()` and `chooseReceiptSignatory({personId, receiptRole})` → `GET` and `PUT /api/admin/donations/receipt-signatory` (`ReceiptSignatoryResponse` with `ChosenReceiptSignatoryResponse` and `ReceiptSignatureSummaryResponse`s; `MAX_RECEIPT_ROLE_LENGTH` 120), `receiptSignature(personId)`, `uploadReceiptSignature(personId, file)` and `removeReceiptSignature(personId)` → `GET`, `POST` (multipart `file`) and `DELETE /api/admin/donations/receipt-signatures/{personId}` (`ReceiptSignatureResponse` with `kind` `ReceiptSignatureKind` and `vector` `SignatureVectorResponse` of `SignatureVectorPath`s); `receiptSigningKeys()` → `GET /api/admin/donations/receipt-signing-keys` (`ReceiptSigningKeysResponse`, `ReceiptSigningKeyResponse`, `ReceiptSigningKeyStatus`); fixtures in `testing/admin-fixtures.ts` (`importState`, `unpairedPayment`, `pairedDonation`, `paymentList`, `foundDonor`, `administrator`, `symbolClaim`, `adminAction`, `donorAccount`, `adminBankAccount`, `adminDonation`, `adminReceipt`, `donorDetail`, `reportView`, `monthOverview`, `overview`, `backupFile`, `backupRun`, `backupsView`, `logFile`, `logWindow`, `logOverride`, `loggerEntry`, `diagnostics`, `setting`, `settingsView`, `receiptSignature`, `noReceiptSignature`).
- `payments/` (`/sprava/platby`, "Platby z banky"): `h1` focused on open, an intro, and a toolbar with the two `aria-pressed` buttons "Čekají na přiřazení" (default; "Čekají na přiřazení (N)" once the unpaired list has answered, N its `total`, one more after each unpairing) and "Přiřazené" (the count only from an unfiltered unpaired list), then a `role="search"` bar with one text field "Hledat" (hint "Jméno odesílatele nebo dárce, účet, variabilní symbol, zpráva nebo částka v korunách.", asked 300 ms after the typing pauses) and the native date fields "Od" and "Do" (asked on change, each bounding the other with `min`/`max`). The state lives in the address (Jakub 2026-10-24): `stav=prirazene` for the paired tab, `q`, `od`, `do` and `darce` (a donor id, from the donor card's link "Platby tohoto dárce"; switching to the unpaired tab drops it), written with `replaceUrl` so a filter change adds no history entry, while Back from a donor card and a shared link open the same view; the page follows `queryParamMap`, so the panel link resets it. Each view asks for 50 (`limit=50`, `switchMap` drops a stale answer); while fewer are shown than `total`, the line "Zobrazeno 50 z 312." and "Načíst další" ("Načítáme další…") under the table ask for the next 50 with `offset` and append them, the focus moves to the first new row (`tabindex="-1"`), a failure is a `role="alert"` "Další platby se nepodařilo načíst. Zkuste to prosím znovu." and the button stays. With any filter the always-rendered `role="status"` line `.payments-count` says "Nalezena 1 platba." / "Nalezeny 3 platby." / "Nalezeno 12 plateb." / "Nenalezena žádná platba.", and when a live `payments` hint (2.16) brings payments the view does not show yet, the same line ends with the button "Zobrazit 1 novou platbu" / "Zobrazit 2 nové platby" / "Zobrazit 5 nových plateb", which shows the first page again and focuses the card's title, while the tab count follows at once (only payments that come before the last row shown count as new, so a payment that moves up from below when one leaves a longer list simply joins the rows; a hint that brings nothing new updates the shown rows in place; while the pairing dialog or an unpairing confirmation is open, or the next page loads, the page waits and reads after it closes or arrives), an empty result says "Žádná platba neodpovídá hledání." with "Zrušit filtr" (clears text, period and donor and focuses the search field), and a donor filter adds "Jen platby dárce <jméno>." (the name from the first listed donation; "Jen platby jednoho dárce." without one) with "Zobrazit platby všech dárců". The paired view lists every year newest first (no year choice any more). The card lists the payments as a table (cards with `data-label` up to 640 px): date (with the amber tag "jen ručně" and the visible note "Správce zrušil přiřazení, web ji proto sám nepřiřadí." when `unpairedByAdminAt` is set), amount (with the badge "Ověření účtu" when `accountVerification`), sender (name and account), symbol and message ("bez VS" without one), for paired payments the donor as a link to `/sprava/darci/{donorId}` ("dárce bez záznamu" when the record is gone) and the tag "vráceno" for a refunded donation, the method in words ("podle variabilního symbolu", "podle účtu", "podle potvrzeného staršího symbolu", "ručně správcem") and the tag "potvrzení vystaveno"; the last column holds "Přiřadit dárci" (unpaired) or "Zrušit přiřazení" (paired; replaced by "Nelze zrušit, k daru je vystavené potvrzení." when `receiptIssued`), which turns into the inline confirmation "Dar 500 Kč zmizí z přehledu dárce <jméno> a platba se vrátí mezi čekající na přiřazení; web ji sám znovu nepřiřadí. Zrušit přiřazení?" (a group named "Zrušit přiřazení platby <platba>?") with "Zrušit přiřazení" ("Rušíme…") / "Ponechat" before the `POST`; focus moves to "Ponechat" when it opens, back to the opener after "Ponechat" or a refusal, and to the page's notice after success. The card titles are "Platby čekající na přiřazení" and "Přiřazené platby". Empty lists say "Všechny platby z banky jsou přiřazené…" or "Zatím není přiřazena žádná platba."; loading is a `role="status"` line, a failed load a `role="alert"` line with "Zkusit znovu"; 401 ends the session and opens the sign-in dialog, 403 goes to `/darce/prehled`. "Přiřadit dárci" opens an `<app-dialog>` naming the payment (amount and day, sender, symbol and message; for an account verification the badge "Ověření účtu" and a warning callout that pairing it by hand counts 1 Kč for the donor but verifies no account, which a payment with the donor's symbol does) with a search field prefilled with the first word of the sender's name (two letters or more), searched at once and focused with its text selected; a typed query goes to `searchDonors` 300 ms after the typing pauses, `switchMap` drops stale answers and a failed search does not end the stream listing the donors as radio buttons ("Jana Nováková · VS 4821937560 · jana@example.cz · IČO …": the e-mail comes from `donors(query, 100)`, `GET /api/admin/donors` with the same query, asked beside the search and matched by `donorId`, so two donors of one name can be told apart; the results show at once and the e-mails follow, and a failed lookup only leaves them out; "Žádný dárce neodpovídá." as `role="status"`, a failed search as `role="alert"`); "Přiřadit" becomes "Přiřadit dárci <jméno>" once a donor is chosen and shows "Přiřazujeme…" while posting; a refusal stays in the dialog as a `role="alert"` line (`describeProblem`), success closes it, reloads the list and puts a `role="status"` line on the page ("Platba 500 Kč z 10. 10. 2026 je přiřazena dárci Jana Nováková."), extended by "Dárce si už stáhl roční potvrzení za 2026; při dalším stažení dostane nové. Dejte mu prosím vědět." when `changedYearlyReceipts` is not empty; unpairing reports the same way ("… už není přiřazena. Web ji sám znovu nepřiřadí; najdete ji mezi platbami čekajícími na přiřazení."), extended, when it withdrew the yearly receipt that listed the gift (1.26), by "Roční potvrzení za 2026, na kterém dar byl, je teď zrušené; ověření ukáže, že platilo ke dni vystavení. Zbyly-li dárci za ten rok další dary, může si stáhnout nové. Dejte mu prosím vědět."; after either, the link "Otevřít kartu dárce <jméno>" sits under the notice. Specs in `admin-payments.spec.ts`; Playwright `e2e/admin-payments.spec.ts` (pair through the dialog with the receipt note and the paired view, unpair with the confirmation and the held tag, a refused pairing staying in the dialog and Escape returning focus to the opener, a search with the next page and Back from a donor card restoring it) and the axe case for the page and the open dialog.
- `administrators/` (`/sprava/spravci`, "Správci"): `h1` focused on open (outline hidden, like the other script-focused headings), an intro saying who can become an administrator. The card "Kdo web spravuje" lists the administrators in the look of the people of the foundation (Jakub 2026-10-31: the shared block `.ui-person-list` / `.ui-person-row` of `styles/ui/_person-list.scss`): each row has a small round portrait (`app-avatar`, the initials without one), the name as a link to the donor card, the e-mail under it and the badges "To jste vy" (the signed-in one, found by `AuthStore.donor().id`) and "Dvoufaktorové ověření zapnuté" or "Dvoufaktorové ověření vypnuté" for `secondFactorEnabled` (the code only; the API does not say whether passkeys exist) with the line "Správa se mu otevře jen přístupovým klíčem." under the latter. The portrait is the one of the person of the foundation linked to the administrator: the page also loads `PeopleApi.load()` once and joins its members to the administrators by e-mail, taking the first member in the order of the site with `linkedToAccount` whose personal or fund e-mail equals the administrator's (the same person `GET /api/admin/content/people/me` gives that administrator for the account button; both e-mails come already normalised by the backend, so the join compares them as they are); when the people cannot be loaded the rows keep their initials and nothing else changes. The actions sit at the right of the row, and under it up to 640 px: for another administrator "Vypnout dvoufaktorové ověření" (only with the factor on; inline confirmation across the row "Ověřili jste, že žádá majitel účtu (například odpověděl z registrovaného e-mailu)? Správce se pak přihlásí jen heslem a všechna jeho přihlášení skončí." with "Ano, vypnout" ("Vypínáme…") / "Ponechat") and "Odebrat roli správce" (danger outline; "<jméno> přestane být správcem a všechna jeho přihlášení skončí. Jeho dárcovský účet zůstane. Odebrat roli správce?" with "Ano, odebrat" ("Odebíráme…") / "Ponechat"); focus moves to "Ponechat" on open, back to the opener after it or a refusal, and to the notice after success; the signed-in administrator has no buttons but the line "Svůj účet spravujete v Údajích a zabezpečení." (a link to `/darce/udaje`). Outcomes are a `role="status"` line on the page ("Petr Svoboda už není správce. Všechna jeho přihlášení jsme ukončili.", "Dvoufaktorové ověření u Petr Svoboda je vypnuté … Poslali jsme mu e-mail; …") or a `role="alert"` line with the backend's title and detail; the list reloads after each. The card "Přidat správce" is a form with the field "E-mail dárce" (`required`, `email`; "Vyplňte prosím e-mail registrovaného dárce." / "Zadejte prosím platnou e-mailovou adresu.") and "Přidat správce" ("Přidáváme…" while posting); success clears the field and says "<jméno> je teď správce. Správa se mu otevře, až se přihlásí s kódem z ověřovací aplikace nebo přístupovým klíčem." (or "… po dalším přihlášení." when the factor is already on); a refusal (404 not registered, 409 already) stays at the form as a `role="alert"` line and keeps the value. Loading, a failed load with "Zkusit znovu", 401 and 403 behave as on the payments page. Specs in `admin-administrators.spec.ts` (the portrait of the linked person, the first one when two share the e-mail, and the initials when the people cannot be loaded); Playwright `e2e/admin-administrators.spec.ts` (the portrait, add by e-mail, the refusal at the form, reset and removal with their confirmations) and the axe case for the page with an open confirmation and the empty-form error.
- `symbol-claims/` (`/sprava/starsi-symboly`, "Odpovědi o starších symbolech"): `h1` focused on open, an intro paragraph explaining the older-symbol question followed by a two-item list of what each correction does. The card "Odpovědi dárců" lists every answer as a table (cards up to 640 px): donor as a link to the donor card ("dárce bez záznamu" when the record is gone), account and symbol in the mono font, the answer as a tag ("Ano, jsou moje" green, "Ne, nejsou moje" grey) with the Prague time under it, the donations paired through it ("1 dar", "3 dary", "5 darů"), and the action: "Vrátit přiřazení darů" (danger outline) for a yes (an inline confirmation naming the donations that will be deleted, the payments going back to waiting and the answer turning to Ne, with "Vrátit přiřazení" / "Ponechat" before the `POST`) or "Zeptat se dárce znovu" for a no (with "Zeptat se znovu" / "Ponechat" before the `DELETE`); focus moves as on the administrators page. Outcomes are a `role="status"` line ("Přiřazení darů u Jana Nováková (VS 6123456789) jsme vrátili: 3 dary jsme smazali a platby s tímto symbolem zase čekají na přiřazení. Odpověď je teď Ne, dárce se už neptáme." / "Odpověď Ne u … jsme smazali. Pokud otázka pořád platí, dárce ji uvidí znovu ve svém přehledu.") or a `role="alert"` line with the backend's title and detail (the receipt refusal); the list reloads after each. The empty list says "Zatím nikdo neodpověděl na otázku o starším variabilním symbolu."; loading, a failed load with "Zkusit znovu", 401 and 403 behave as on the other admin pages. Specs in `admin-symbol-claims.spec.ts`; Playwright `e2e/admin-symbol-claims.spec.ts` (reverse and delete with their confirmations, the refusal by a receipt) and the axe case for the page with an open confirmation.
- `bank-logos/` (`/sprava/loga-bank`, "Loga bank", contract "Bank logos"): `h1` focused on open, two intro paragraphs (the server downloads the icon from the address, makes it a small square and serves it itself, so the donor's browser never contacts the bank or Apple; only App Store icons from is1-ssl.mzstatic.com to is5-ssl.mzstatic.com or a picture from the sites of the big banks, named in the text, https without a query, PNG or JPEG; a weekly download again that keeps the old logo on a failure). The card "Banky s logem" lists every bank as a `ui-person-row`: `app-bank-mark` (centred on the code and the state line, the failure and address notes under them) of the stored image (or the monogram of the ČNB name shortened), the code and the short name ("Banka, kterou seznam ČNB nezná" without one; `#bank-title-<code>`, which the row's three buttons name in `aria-describedby`, while the address field is named "Adresa loga <code> · <bank>"), "Staženo …" with "naposledy zkontrolováno …" when the last check is later, or "Zatím nestaženo.", the last failure in Czech words of a closed map (`FAILURE_WORDS`, an unknown word "důvod neznáme"), and for `sourceAllowed: false` "Adresa už není povolená…" without "Stáhnout znovu"; under it a form with the field "Adresa loga" (`type="url"`, `enterkeyhint="send"`, required, `https://` without spaces, at most 500 characters), "Uložit a stáhnout" (`PUT` with `If-Match`; the status says "z nové adresy" only when the address changed), "Stáhnout znovu" (`POST …/refresh`; the status says whether the picture changed) and "Odebrat", which opens `bank-logo-remove-dialog` ("Odebrat logo banky …?", `DELETE` with `If-Match`; a refusal stays in the dialog as an alert). The card "Logo další banky" adds a code ("Kód banky", four digits) with its address (`PUT` with `If-None-Match: *`); a code already listed is refused locally at the field "Kód banky". A refused address (`bank-logo-source-invalid`) is shown at its field, a failed download (502), a 429 and the rest under the row's form or the add form as an alert (`describeProblem`), a 404 (removed meanwhile, also for a removal, which closes its dialog) as the page's alert followed by a fresh read. A 409 opens `ChangedMeanwhileDialog` (subject `content`): with `version` it keeps the address in the form and offers to save it over the newer version; without one (the logo was removed and created again meanwhile, or a removal found another version) it only reads the list again. Success lines go to the page's `app-live-notice`; every control that calls the server carries `appNeedsConnection`; each read numbers the rows anew, so the forms start clean after it; only the first read shows the loading line, a read again after the sheet or a 404 keeps the rows until the answer and then focuses the row's address field, or the notice when the row is gone; each saved, downloaded or removed logo calls `BankNames.forgetLogos()`, so the other pages ask for the logos again.
- `yearly-receipts/` (`/sprava/hromadna-potvrzeni`, "Hromadná potvrzení", slice 2.29; contract "Bulk yearly receipts"): `h1` focused on open, an intro saying what is kept, issued and replaced, and that a single receipt to send by e-mail comes from the donor card. The card "Vystavit a vytisknout" asks `yearlyReceiptCandidates(null)` first (the last completed year) and offers the years from `receiptsAvailableFrom` through `yearlyReceiptsAvailableThrough` in a select, which keeps its place and focus while another year loads (an always rendered status line "Načítáme dárce za rok …" instead of the counts, the button and the table; "Zkusit znovu" after a failure asks for the year chosen); a 400 `donations/year-not-yet-completed` or `donations/receipt-before-first-year` there says that bulk receipts come after the first year the foundation issues them for. Four labelled counts (donors with gifts, receipts already issued, to be issued, erased donors skipped), the checkbox "Tisknu oboustranně" (on by default, because two donors' receipts on one sheet cost more than a blank page, UX review of 2.29; its label says it adds blank pages and to turn it off for one-sided printing; never disabled, so it keeps focus; changing it while drawing or after the PDF is ready draws it again without issuing), "Vystavit a připravit PDF k tisku" (`aria-disabled` while running, so focus stays on it; it says "Vystavujeme potvrzení…" and then "Připravujeme PDF…"; soft once a PDF is ready; after a failure it goes on as "Zkusit znovu" does) and "Nechte prosím stránku otevřenou…", which sends the year's donors in batches of 50 one after another (`issueYearlyReceipts`) with "Vystavujeme potvrzení N z M…", then draws every returned receipt into one PDF through `drawBundle` with "Kreslíme potvrzení…" (announced once; the counter "k z n" is the progress bar's visible detail and `aria-valuetext`). A failure shows a `role="alert"` line with "Zkusit znovu", which sends only the donors not yet answered, or after a drawing failure only draws again; the run can be cancelled, which moves focus to that line. While it runs `screen-wake-lock.ts` (`ScreenWakeLock` behind `SCREEN_WAKE_LOCKS`) keeps the screen on, takes the lock again when the tab comes back and lets it go on done, failure, cancel or leaving. When done: "PDF k tisku je připravené: obsahuje N potvrzení.", "Stáhnout PDF" ("Stáhnout znovu" from memory, offline too, for the life of the page) and "Otevřít PDF" through `PdfDelivery` (`potvrzeni-o-darech-<year>-hromadne.pdf`, titled "Roční potvrzení o darech za rok <year>"), "Tiskněte ve skutečné velikosti (100 %)…", the card "Výsledek" (in place of the counts from before the run) with the four outcomes and the replaced donors (with the sentence that the old receipts stop being valid, so the donors get the new ones) and the skipped donors, each linking to the donor card with a Czech reason per problem code, and the table "Dárci za rok <year>" (donor, gifts, total, receipt number or "zatím ne"; sorted with `Intl.Collator('cs')`; cards up to 640 px) updated to the new numbers. Tests: `admin-yearly-receipts.spec.ts`, `screen-wake-lock.spec.ts`, `receipt-pdf-bundle.spec.ts`, `e2e/admin-yearly-receipts.spec.ts` (a duplex run read back page by page and downloaded again with no request; a failed batch retried with 50, 10 and 10 donors), the axe, theme and device steps, and `e2e/bulk-receipts-load.spec.ts`, the nightly 300 receipts at 4× CPU (`BULK_RECEIPTS_LOAD=1`).
- `actions/` (`/sprava/historie`, "Historie změn"): `h1` focused on open, an intro. The card "Co správci udělali" lists the `admin_action` trail newest first as a table (cards up to 640 px): the Prague time, the administrator (name and e-mail; "správce bez účtu" when the account is gone, its id then inside the details; "přes aplikaci claude.ai" under them when the change came through a connected application, from the item's `client`), the action in Czech (`ACTION_NAMES` maps `PAIR_PAYMENT`, `UNPAIR_PAYMENT`, `GRANT_ADMINISTRATION`, `REVOKE_ADMINISTRATION`, `RESET_SECOND_FACTOR`, `REMOVE_PASSKEYS`, `REVERSE_SYMBOL_CLAIM`, `RESET_SYMBOL_CLAIM`, `DISCONNECT_SENDER_ACCOUNT`, `RECONNECT_SENDER_ACCOUNT`, `REMOVE_SENDER_ACCOUNT_LINK`, `ERASE_DONOR`, `CHANGE_REPORT_SETTINGS`, `SEND_MONTHLY_REPORT`, `CHANGE_RECEIPT_SIGNATURE`, `REMOVE_RECEIPT_SIGNATURE`, `ISSUE_RECEIPT` "Vystavení potvrzení o daru", `WITHDRAW_RECEIPT` "Zrušení potvrzení o daru" (1.26), `CREATE_NEWS`, `UPDATE_NEWS`, `PUBLISH_NEWS`, `UNPUBLISH_NEWS`, `DELETE_NEWS`, `CREATE_PROJECT`, `UPDATE_PROJECT`, `PUBLISH_PROJECT`, `UNPUBLISH_PROJECT`, `DELETE_PROJECT`, `CHANGE_PROJECT_ORDER`, `CHANGE_ABOUT_PHOTO`, `REMOVE_ABOUT_PHOTO`, `REQUEST_BACKUP`, `REQUEST_TEST_RESTORE`, `DOWNLOAD_BACKUP`, `RESTORE_BACKUP`, `BACKUP_RESTORED`, `BACKUP_RESTORE_FAILED`, `CHANGE_LOG_LEVEL`, `RESET_LOG_LEVEL`, `CHANGE_SETTINGS`, `SUBSCRIBE_PUSH` "Zapnutí upozornění přes push na zařízení", `CHANGE_PUSH_ALERTS` "Změna upozornění přes push na zařízení", `REMOVE_PUSH_SUBSCRIPTION` "Vypnutí upozornění přes push na zařízení" (2.27), `CHANGE_BACKUP_SCHEDULE`, `REGISTER_RECEIPT_SIGNING_KEY` "Registrace klíče k ověřovacím QR kódům", `ADOPT_RECEIPT_SIGNING_KEY` "Převzetí klíče k ověřovacím QR kódům ze zálohy"; an unknown code is shown as it is; a row by the server itself, `adminId` the nil UUID, names "server" as the administrator and no id in the details), the link "Otevřít kartu dárce" to `/sprava/darci/{donorId}` whenever the server names the donor the change concerned (`donorId`: donor actions, payment pairings, symbol answers, bank accounts), and in the details column the server's sentence `summary` (Jakub 2026-10-24: human Czech written by the backend, e.g. "Platba 500 Kč ze dne 10. 10. 2026 ručně přiřazena dárci Jana Nováková.") above the stored details inside a closed `<details>` "Technické podrobnosti" (with the ids; English for rows before 2026-10-24). The first page asks for 50; while `hasMore`, "Načíst starší" under the table asks for the next 50 after the last entry listed (`adminActions(lastListed)`: `before` its `performedAt` and `beforeId` its `id`, so entries recorded at one moment are not skipped) and appends them ("Načítáme starší…" while loading, a `role="alert"` "Starší záznamy se nepodařilo načíst. Zkuste to prosím znovu." on failure, the button stays). The empty trail says "Zatím žádný správce nic nezměnil."; loading, a failed load with "Zkusit znovu", 401 and 403 behave as on the other admin pages. Specs in `admin-actions.spec.ts`; Playwright `e2e/admin-actions.spec.ts` (the first page and the appended older page, the empty trail) and the axe case for the page with its paging button.
- `donors/` (`/sprava/darci`, "Dárci"): `h1` focused on open, an intro saying what the card offers. The card "Hledat dárce" has the field "Jméno, e-mail nebo variabilní symbol" (`type="search"`, hint "Hledáme část jména nebo e-mailu a začátek variabilního symbolu; nabídneme nejvýše 20 dárců."); typing is debounced 300 ms, trimmed and deduplicated, each query goes to `donors(query)` and lands in the address as `?q=` (`replaceUrl`), so the page reopens with the same search and the browser's back button returns to the result list. States as `role="status"` or `role="alert"` lines: "Začněte psát a dárci se objeví tady." (empty query), "Hledáme dárce…", "Hledání se nepodařilo. Zkuste to prosím znovu." with the button "Zkusit znovu" (asks the same query again), "Žádný dárce neodpovídá.", a visible "Zobrazujeme prvních N dárců. Upřesněte hledání…" when `hasMore`, and a visually hidden "Nalezení dárci: N" above the table (cards up to 640 px): the name as a link to `/sprava/darci/{id}` with "Osoba" or "Firma · IČO …" under it, e-mail, variable symbol, registration (the Prague date only) and the tags "Správce", "Dvoufaktorové ověření", "E-mail neověřen" (or "běžný účet"); an erased donor (`erasedAt`) shows "vymazán" instead of the placeholder e-mail and only the tag "Údaje vymazány". Every row leads with an `app-avatar` (40 px): the portrait of a person of the foundation linked to the account by e-mail (`people/portraits-by-email.ts`, the same match as `administrators/`, from `PeopleApi.load()`; none for an erased donor), else the initials; a failed load leaves the initials and shows nothing. While the query is empty, the page lists "Naposledy přispěli" (an `h3`) instead of the idle line, once the administration copy holds rows: `recent-donors.ts` takes the copy's `donations/donations` and `donors/accounts` rows (`AdministrationCopy.rows`), keeps `RECEIVED` gifts that are no account verification, takes each donor's latest (gift day, then `recordedAt`, then id), sorts newest first and keeps the first 10 whose account the copy holds (the two parts are of two moments); each row is the search row with the last gift (amount and day; in the table the column "Poslední dar" replaces "Registrace"). It is computed in the browser and asks the server nothing (local first); without a loaded copy the idle line stays. The first `/sprava` page that reads the copy. 401 and 403 as on the other admin pages. Specs in `admin-donors.spec.ts` and `recent-donors.spec.ts`.
- `donor-detail/` (`/sprava/darci/:id`, "Dárce"): the link "← Dárci", `h1` with the donor's name (focused on open; "Dárce" until it loads or when there is none). Once `donor(id)` answers, a summary strip (`section.donor-summary`, "Shrnutí dárce") shows the variable symbol with `app-copy-button` ("Kopírovat variabilní symbol"), the e-mail with "ověřen" / "neověřen", the received gifts ("2 dary za 800 Kč", once `donorDonations(id)` answers) and the badges "Správce" and "2FA". Under it the `nav` "Části karty dárce" lists in-page links "Údaje · Přihlášení · Bankovní účty · Dary · Potvrzení" (only to the cards shown; `href` `/sprava/darci/{id}#udaje` and so on): a click keeps the address, and focuses the card (`id` without diacritics, `tabindex="-1"`, no ring), which scrolls it into view below the top safe area thanks to the global `scroll-padding-top`. The cards, the first two from `donor(id)`, the rest from `donorDonations(id)`, each loading on its own: "Údaje" is a `dl` (type, e-mail with the tag, address, variable symbol, language ("česky" / "anglicky"), registration, role "správce" (a link to `/sprava/spravci`) / "dárce"). "Přihlášení" (once the account is loaded) is a `dl` (two-factor authentication "zapnuté" / "vypnuté", passkeys with their count or "žádné") with "Vypnout dvoufaktorové ověření" for a donor who has it on (inline confirmation "Ověřili jste, že žádá majitel účtu (například odpověděl z registrovaného e-mailu)? Dárce se pak přihlásí jen heslem a všechna jeho přihlášení skončí." with "Ano, vypnout" / "Ponechat"; success "Dvoufaktorové ověření u <jméno> je vypnuté a všechna přihlášení jsme ukončili. Dárce dostal e-mail; …") and "Odebrat přístupové klíče" with the hint "Pro dárce, který přišel o zařízení s klíčem." for a donor with keys (confirmation "Dárce se jimi pak nepřihlásí a všechna jeho přihlášení skončí. Odebrat?", `removePasskeys(id)` is a DELETE, success "Přístupové klíče u <jméno> jsme odebrali a všechna přihlášení ukončili. …"); after either the account is fetched again without the loading state, so the card and its result stay. A 404 leaves a single card "Účet dárce" saying "Takový dárce není. Vraťte se na seznam dárců a vyhledejte ho znovu." with the link "Zpět na hledání dárců" and hides everything else. Every inline confirmation answers "Ponechat"; focus moves to "Ponechat" when it opens, back to the opener (ids `donor-reset-open`, `donor-passkeys-open`, `account-<kind>-<linkId>`) after it or a refusal, and to the result line after success. The result lines sit in the card the action belongs to: one `app-live-notice` each in "Přihlášení", "Bankovní účty" and the erasure card (`cardNotice` names the card). The last card, "Výmaz osobních údajů (GDPR)", offers "Vymazat osobní údaje" (danger outline, id `donor-erase-open`) with the hint "Pro dárce, který požádal o výmaz podle GDPR."; for an administrator the hint "Správci nejde vymazat osobní údaje. Nejdřív mu odeberte roli správce na stránce Správci." replaces the button, and an erased donor reads "Osobní údaje tohoto dárce jsou vymazané.". The button opens an `<app-dialog>` "Vymazat osobní údaje: <jméno>" saying the step cannot be undone, listing what is deleted (name, address, e-mail and password replaced by placeholders, the bank accounts, the older-symbol answers) and what stays (donations and receipts for the accounting, the receipts unchanged on the server with the donor's name and address; the bank payments as the statement gives them, with the account number and payer name, and the entries in Historie změn; A6), that the donor gets an e-mail, and a checkbox "Dárce o výmaz požádal a ověřil(a) jsem, že jde o majitele účtu"; "Zrušit" has the focus when it opens, the danger button "Vymazat osobní údaje" stays disabled until the box is ticked and shows "Mažeme…" while `eraseDonor(id)` (a DELETE) runs; a refusal stays in the dialog as a `role="alert"` line, success closes it, puts "Osobní údaje dárce jsme vymazali a na jeho dosavadní e-mail poslali potvrzení. …" in the erasure card with the focus, and both halves reload. An erased donor (`erasedAt` on either half) gets a `role="note"` box under the `h1` "Osobní údaje tohoto dárce jsme vymazali <čas>. …", "vymazán" / "vymazána" instead of the placeholder e-mail and address, no actions, and "Bankovní účty" says "Bankovní účty jsme s osobními údaji dárce smazali; další platby se mu už nepřiřadí." "Bankovní účty" says that its column "Dary přiřazené podle účtu" (formerly "Dary přes účet", which Hana read as an error for a company giving with its symbol from a verified account, 30. 10. 2026) counts only payments without the symbol that were paired by the account, the gifts with the symbol being under Dary, explains when to switch pairing off and when to remove the account, and lists the links (account with the tag "sdílený", the status "Páruje platby" / "Čeká na platbu se symbolem" / "Párování vypnuté" with the verification, connection and disconnection dates, the donations paired by the account with their total and the last date) with "Vypnout párování" (live link), "Zapnout párování" (disconnected, not `pairingExcluded`) and "Odebrat účet" (danger outline; confirmation "Smažeme N darů přiřazené přes tento účet i odpovědi o starších symbolech; platby zůstanou nespárované. Odebrat?"), replaced by "Nejde odebrat: k darům z účtu je vystavené potvrzení." when the link is the only one of that account and one of its donations has a receipt number; the outcomes are `role="status"` lines naming the account, a refusal (409 by a receipt) a `role="alert"` line with the backend's title and detail plus, for `donations/bank-account-has-receipt`, the next step (switch pairing off), and the three cards reload after each. "Dary" says "Celkem N darů za X Kč." (counting only received donations, plus "Vrácené dary (n) se nezapočítávají." when some were refunded) and lists every donation (received, amount, purpose, how it was paired with the account or symbol, the receipt number or "zatím žádné"); "Vydaná potvrzení" lists every receipt (number, "Dar z <datum>" or "Rok YYYY", issued at with CZ/EN, "platné" / "nahrazeno novějším" / "zrušeno, dary se změnily" (1.26, `withdrawalReason` `DONATIONS_CHANGED`), and at each valid receipt, also of an erased donor, "Stáhnout PDF" (1.19, A7; its name ends with the hidden "potvrzení <číslo>", so it holds the visible text in every state): `admin-receipt-downloads.ts` (`AdminReceiptDownloads`, provided by the card) draws it with `ReceiptPdf.draw` from `ReceiptsApi.administrationSource(id)`, so it issues nothing, and refuses a receipt the read no longer answers as `ACTIVE` (replaced since the card loaded: the card says so in its alert and lists the receipts again), shows the donor portal's steps through `app-download-progress` at the row (the step and saved texts of `receipt-pdf/receipt-progress.ts`, which both use under their `donor.dashboard.receipt.*` ids, so rewording them changes both pages; "Zrušit" returns focus to the row's button; a new download clears the finished lines of the others), saves it through `PdfDelivery` or, on an iPhone that shares files, turns the button into "Otevřít PDF" for the share sheet, and puts any failure as a `role="alert"` line at the row: the problem of the read, or for a drawing failure an administrator's sentence (tools not loaded: reload the page; drawing; signature or portrait not verified; unprintable character; theme refused); a lost session goes to the sign-in as the card's other calls do; under the button of a receipt whose `keyId` the published key list names `revoked` an amber note "QR na tomto potvrzení podepsal zneplatněný klíč. Stránka /overit ho ukáže jako zneplatněné."
