(Jakub 2026-11-05, Phase 1 handover card 6: the PDF is still offered, warned), the card reading `GET /api/donations/receipt-keys` without the token (`ReceiptKeysApi`) once it lists a valid receipt, and only once per visit, through `trustedKeys`, as `/overit` does, so it warns exactly when `/overit` says "Neověřeno: fond tento klíč zneplatnil"; the note describes the button (`aria-describedby`); a list that cannot be read, and a key the list does not name, warn of nothing; empty: "Dárci zatím nikdo žádné potvrzení nevystavil.") and, unless the donor is erased, offers "Vystavit potvrzení" (1.7): a select "Potvrzení k vystavení" (its hint says the same data return the receipt that already holds) with "Roční potvrzení za rok YYYY" for each year from `receiptsAvailableFrom` to `yearlyReceiptsAvailableThrough` (both from the detail, so no client clock) in which a received donation lies, newest first, then "Dar z <datum>, X Kč" for each received donation with `receiptAvailable`; without any it says "Dárce nemá žádný dar, za který by šlo potvrzení vystavit.". The button posts `issueReceipt(id, {year} | {donationId})` (`POST /api/admin/donations/donors/{id}/receipts`), shows "Vystavujeme potvrzení…" with the button and the select disabled, then a `role="status"` line in the card's `app-live-notice` (focused) with the number, the Prague time and the status ("Vystavili jsme potvrzení 2027-000001 z <čas>. Je platné.", with ", které nahrazuje potvrzení …" for a replacement, or "Potvrzení … z <čas> dál platí: údaje se nezměnily, nové číslo jsme nevydali." when the answer is a receipt the card already listed) and reloads the donations part without hiding the cards; a refusal (the cap, an erased donor) is a `role="alert"` line with the backend's title and detail, or "Potvrzení se nepodařilo vystavit. Zkuste to prosím znovu.", and the focus goes back to the button. The PDF is drawn in 1.9; under the donations the link "Platby tohoto dárce" opens `/sprava/platby?stav=prirazene&darce={id}`, the paired payments filtered to this donor. Without a `donor_record` (404 from the donations side) the three cards say "Dárce nemá záznam v evidenci darů, takže nemá žádné účty, dary ani potvrzení." Specs in `admin-donor-detail.spec.ts`; Playwright `e2e/admin-donors.spec.ts` (the journey from `/sprava` through the search, the card, the reset, the disconnect and the removal, the refusal by a receipt, issuing a receipt of one donation, the unknown donor) and the axe case for the list and the card with an open confirmation; the journey (with the in-page link to Přihlášení) and the axe case cover the erasure dialog too.
- `home/` (`/sprava`, "Přehled" in the panel): `h1` "Správa" (`tabindex="-1"`, focused on open), then the card "Od posledního přihlášení" (Jakub 29. 10. 2026), then the group "Co potřebuje vaši pozornost" (on a phone, while `CompactLayout.isCompact()`, it comes right under the `h1`, above that card; Jakub 2026-11-10), then, only while `CompactLayout.isCompact()` (2.24, where no side panel stands), the section rows `nav` "Sekce správy": the group "Každý den" with Platby z banky (its badge the unpaired count the attention card loads, "N platba čeká na přiřazení"), Dárci, Aktuality and Pomáháme, and the group "Občas a pro technika" with the one row "Lidé fondu, správci, zálohy, nastavení…" to `/sprava/vice`, led by the foundation's star (Jakub 2026-11-06: the people of the foundation come first in it), below it two smaller status cards side by side (one column when narrow), "Stav stahování plateb z banky" and "Zálohy databáze", and at the bottom the card "Dary a dárci". The attention group (variant A of the iOS designer, Jakub 2026-11-10: rows of a grouped list like iOS Settings, never a card inside a card) is `section.attention` with `h2.ui-group-title` over one `ul.ui-list.ui-list--inset`; `attention-rows.ts` (`attentionRows`) turns six sources, each loading and failing on its own, into its rows: the import state, `payments('unpaired')` (every unpaired payment of every year: the count is `total`, the sum adds the listed `amountMinor`), `backups()` and `administrators()` (for each administrator without the code, `donor(id)` is asked and those with `passkeys === 0` count as without any second factor), `receiptSigningKeys()` (`GET /api/admin/donations/receipt-signing-keys`) and `receiptSignatory()` (`GET /api/admin/donations/receipt-signatory`, slice 1.16). Each row leads with a 28 px tile whose colour tells the kind (`--color-danger-fill` urgent, `--color-tile-warning` a warning, `--color-tile-accent` a task, `--color-tile-neutral` a source that could not be read, `--color-tile-success` all fine) and whose icon the topic, and says the kind in words too, visually hidden ("Naléhavé:", "Upozornění:"); then a short title, a line under it, a value at the right (the sum of the waiting payments; under the title in a container narrower than 13 rem, so at 200 % text) and a chevron. A row with a way forward is one link, the whole row the tap target; what acts on the spot is a pill at the right ("Zkusit znovu" with `appNeedsConnection`, "Kopírovat" the message for the technician). While any of the first five loads its first row says "Zjišťujeme, jestli na vás něco nečeká…" (`role="status"`), and the rows of the sources that answered already follow; with nothing pending one calm row "Vše je v pořádku" over "Platby z banky naposledy stažené {čas}, poslední záloha {čas}.". The rows, in this order: with nobody chosen to sign, first because it stops every receipt, the urgent "Nikdo nepodepisuje potvrzení o daru" / "Web je proto dárcům nevystaví." to `/sprava/lide-fondu`; the import outage (`reportedOutage`) as the warning "Platby z banky se nestahují" / "N× za sebou, fond o tom dostal e-mail. Důvod: <reason>." with "Co udělat:" and the next step per `ImportFailureKind` (`NEXT_STEPS`, adapted from the outage e-mail) under it, followed, where a page helps, by the action row "Otevřít Nastavení" or "Otevřít logy"; an import that never ran as the warning "Stahování plateb ještě neproběhlo" / "Pokud to trvá déle než hodinu, zkontrolujte v Nastavení token Fio API." to `/sprava/nastaveni`; the waiting payments "N platby čekají na přiřazení" (the badge's words of the section rows) with the sum "X Kč" to `/sprava/platby`; each backup problem as an urgent row with its plain summary as the title and what to do under it (`BACKUP_PROBLEM_SUMMARIES` and `BACKUP_PROBLEM_ACTIONS` in `backups/backup-texts.ts`, the sentences the backups page shows, which keeps the technical `BACKUP_PROBLEM_TEXTS` under its "Technické podrobnosti") to `/sprava/zalohy`, and, when the server administrator is needed, the row "Zpráva pro technika" with "Kopírovat" (the same message as the backups page); the warning "Klíč k zálohám si zatím nikdo neuložil" / "Bez něj žádnou zálohu neobnovíte. Uložit ho má někdo ze správní rady, ne technik." to `/sprava/zalohy#klic-k-zaloham` while `backups().key` is available and unconfirmed; the urgent "Klíč k ověřovacím QR kódům není zveřejněný" (plural "Klíče … nejsou zveřejněné") over "Klíč <id> není zveřejněný, proto se QR kódy jím podepsané neověří. …" (`settings/receipt-signing-key/unpublished-keys.ts`, whose `unpublishedKeyIds` adds the server's own key when `currentKeyPublished` is false, also when its row is missing) and the urgent "Klíč k ověřovacím QR kódům potřebuje kontrolu" over a withdrawn key of the server and every audit mismatch (`signingKeyWarnings`, `key-warnings.ts`), both to `/sprava/nastaveni#klic-k-overovacim-qr`; "Správce nemá druhý faktor, správa se mu neotevře" (plural "Správci nemají druhý faktor, správa se jim neotevře") over the names to `/sprava/spravci`; and last "Podepisující nemá nahraný podpis" / "Potvrzení o daru mají zatím jen čáru se jménem." to `/sprava/lide-fondu`. A source that fails is its own row with the tile of its topic in grey: "Platby čekající na přiřazení se nepodařilo zjistit.", "Klíč k ověřovacím QR kódům se nepodařilo zkontrolovat.", "Správce se nepodařilo zkontrolovat." and "Nepodařilo se zkontrolovat, kdo podepisuje potvrzení o daru." in a `role="alert"` with "Zkusit znovu", the import and the backups ("… se nepodařilo zjistit; zkuste to znovu v kartě níže.") retried in their cards. The import card: "Načítáme stav importu…" (`role="status"`) while loading; on failure a `role="alert"` line "Stav importu se nepodařilo načíst." with "Zkusit znovu"; when loaded and without a failure, the line "Stahování plateb je v pořádku, naposledy {čas}." in the success colour after a tick (no box inside the card, Jakub 2026-11-10), then a `dl`: "Stažené období" (`coveredFrom – coveredTo` as `formatDonationDate`, "zatím nic"), "Poslední úspěšné stažení" and "Poslední pokus" (`formatBankTime`, Prague; "zatím žádné" / "zatím žádný"), "Poslední chyba" (the Czech name of the `ImportFailureKind`, its time, the stored message in brackets, or "žádná"). The card "Dary a dárci" asks `overview()` on open and again when the "Rok" `select` (the `availableYears`) changes: "Načítáme dary po měsících…" while loading, a `role="alert"` "Dary po měsících se nepodařilo načíst." with "Zkusit znovu" on failure, then the line "Registrovaní dárci: N, z toho nových v roce Y: M." and the table (cards up to 640 px) Měsíc (`formatReportMonth`, "říjen 2026"), Dary (`received.count`, every payment that reached the account), Dárci (`givingDonors`, the distinct donors with a received donation in the month, so paired payments only; the footer counts each donor once a year), Noví dárci, Celkem (`received`), Přiřazené (`paired`) and Bez dárce (`unpaired`), amounts as `formatCrowns` + Kč, with a "Celkem Y" footer (on phones each month is a card with Dary, Dárci, Celkem and Bez dárce two by two; Noví dárci and Přiřazené are hidden there); above the table the hint "Dárci: kolik různých dárců v měsíci poslalo dar; platby bez dárce se nepočítají. Noví dárci: kolik dárců se v měsíci zaregistrovalo." (`aria-describedby` of the table) and under it the line "Noví dárci jsou nové registrace, i bez daru, takže jich může být víc než dárců. Celkem zahrnuje i platby, které ještě nemají dárce (Celkem = Přiřazené + Bez dárce)." (`.overview-columns`; Hana 30. 10. 2026 could not tell the two donor columns apart), or "Rok Y ještě nezačal." when the year has no started month. The backups card asks `backups()` on open (fails on its own with "Stav záloh se nepodařilo načíst." and "Zkusit znovu"): the line "Zálohování je v pořádku." in the success colour after a tick when there is no problem (the problems themselves are in the attention group), a `dl` "Poslední záloha" ("{time} ({size})", "selhala {time}", "zatím žádná") and "Zkušební obnova" ("{time}, N tabulek", "selhala {time}", "zatím žádná"), and the link "Otevřít zálohy" to `/sprava/zalohy`. A 401 `auth/unauthenticated` ends the session and opens the sign-in dialog at `/`; a 403 `auth/forbidden` (the role taken away meanwhile) goes to `/darce/prehled`. The card "Od posledního přihlášení" asks `previousSignIn()` (`GET /api/admin/donors/me/previous-sign-in`) and, when there was one, `overviewSince(previousSignInAt)` (`GET /api/admin/donations/overview/since?from=`), one after the other (`switchMap`): "Zjišťujeme, co je od minula nového…" (`role="status"`) while loading, a `role="alert"` "Co je od minula nového, se nepodařilo zjistit." with "Zkusit znovu" on failure; without a previous sign-in "Jste tu poprvé. Od příštího přihlášení tu uvidíte nové platby a nové dárce."; with nothing new "Poslední přihlášení {čas}. Od té doby nepřišla žádná platba a nikdo nový se nezaregistroval."; otherwise "Poslední přihlášení {čas}. Od té doby přišlo N plateb za X Kč." (one/few/other, "nepřišla žádná platba" with registrations only; `formatBankTime`) and a `dl`: "Přiřazené dárcům" ("N platby za X Kč", a link to `/sprava/platby?stav=prirazene&od=<den>`), "Čekají na přiřazení" (a link to `/sprava/platby?od=<den>`) and "Noví dárci" ("N nové registrace", a link to `/sprava/darci`), "žádná" without a link for a zero, the payment rows left out when no payment came; `<den>` is the Prague day of the sign-in (`pragueDay` in `donations/donation-date.ts`), so the payments page opens on the payments booked from that day, both tabs keeping the period. The new payments are split into the paired and the waiting ones because the payments page has no tab with both; the period filters the booking day while the card counts by import time, so a payment booked before the sign-in day but imported after it is counted and not listed (rare: the import runs hourly). Texts under `admin.home.since.*`. Texts under `admin.home.*`, the failure names `admin.home.import.failure.<kind>`, the attention card under `admin.home.attention.*`.
- `report/` (`/sprava/prehledy`, "Měsíční e-mail"): `h1` focused on open, an intro saying what the report is, that the page sets who gets it, when and what it carries, and that the outage alerts go to the same addresses. Three cards from `report()`. "Nastavení": the line "Příští přehled za {month} odejde {dueOn} v {hour}." (`nextReport` and `settings.sendHour`) or, when `nextReport.month` is still `lastEndedMonth` and its sending time has passed in Prague by the browser's clock (only this wording depends on it: the backend's `nextReport` keeps the date of the missed sending), "Přehled za {month} měl odejít {dueOn} v {hour}, ale zatím neodešel. Web ho zkusí poslat znovu v příští celou hodinu; poslat ho můžete i hned níže." (the hourly report check sends a due, unsent previous month; Pavel 30. 10. 2026 read the past date as an unanswered question), "Naposledy změněno {time}." or "Nastavení jste zatím neměnili.", and the form: the textarea "Příjemci" (one address per line, `EmailListValidator` in `email-list.ts` with the errors `emailList`, `emailListLength` for more than `MAX_REPORT_RECIPIENTS` = 10 and `emailListDuplicate`, whose messages name the offending address through `firstInvalidAddress` and `firstRepeatedAddress`; `parseEmailList` also accepts commas and semicolons), the select "Den odeslání" (1.–28., hint "Přehled za minulý měsíc odejde v tento den v měsíci."), the select "Hodina odeslání" (0:00–23:00, hint "Pražský čas; výchozí je 7:00."), the fieldset "Co přehled obsahuje" with the four checkboxes, and "Uložit nastavení" ("Ukládáme…"), which refuses an empty selection with the form-level "Vyberte prosím aspoň jednu část přehledu.", sends `changeReportSettings`, shows "Nastavení jsme uložili. Příští přehled odejde podle něj." right under the button (`.ui-form-status`, `role="status"`) and reloads quietly (the cards stay); a refusal shows `describeProblem` under the form. "Poslat přehled hned": the intro "Přehled za vybraný měsíc odejde hned, i když už jednou odešel. Poslat jde jen měsíc, který už skončil: přehled za {running month} půjde poslat od {its end}." (the month after `lastEndedMonth` through `followingMonth`, and the first day of the month after it; this replaced the small hint under the select, which Pavel overlooked), the line "Přehled dostanou: {recipients}. To jsou příjemci uložení v nastavení výše." (the saved `settings.recipients`, so an unsaved edit of the textarea does not count), the select "Měsíc" with the 24 months back from `lastEndedMonth` (`previousMonths` in `report-month.ts`; the label says "(odesláno {date})" or "(zatím neodesláno)"), and below it "Poslat přehled", which opens the `role="group"` "Poslat přehled?" asking "Poslat přehled za {month} na {recipients}?" with "Poslat přehled" ("Posíláme…") and "Zrušit" (focused; the select is disabled meanwhile, and cancelling, success or failure return the focus to the opening button); the result is a notice in this card: success says "Přehled za {month} jsme poslali na {recipients}." and reloads, a refusal (400 `report-month-not-ended`, 502 `report-not-delivered`) shows the backend title and detail as `role="alert"`, and after a 502 the link "Otevřít nastavení e-mailů" leads to `/sprava/nastaveni#settings-MAIL`. "Odeslané přehledy": a `ui-list` (`.sent-reports`) of rows, each one link to `/sprava/prehledy/{YYYY-MM}` with the month as title, "Odesláno {time}" (`formatBankTime`, the month's first sending) as meta and a chevron (Jakub 2026-11-10: a sent report opens), or "Zatím žádný přehled neodešel.". Loading, a failed load with "Zkusit znovu", 401 and 403 behave as on the other admin pages. Texts under `admin.report.*`. Specs in `admin-report.spec.ts`; Playwright `e2e/admin-report.spec.ts` (saving the settings, a malformed address, sending a month again, a refused e-mail, opening a sent month on a phone and the back arrow to the list, a recomputed month) and the axe case for the page with an invalid field and the open confirmation; `e2e/admin.spec.ts` covers the overview card and the year switch.
- `report/sent/` (`/sprava/prehledy/:month`, `parent` `/sprava/prehledy`, so the header's back arrow returns to the list on a phone and the sidebar keeps "Měsíční e-mail" current on a wide screen; nginx `~^/sprava/prehledy/[^/]+$`): `AdminSentReport` reads `sentReport(month)` (`GET /api/admin/donations/report/sent?month=`, contract "Monthly report settings and overview"). `h1` "Přehled za {month}" (focused on open) and the intro "Co v přehledu stálo, když odešel e-mailem. Nejnovější odeslání je nahoře.", then one `section.delivery` per kept sending, newest first: `h2` "Odesláno {time}" for a single one, otherwise "Naposledy odesláno {time}" and "Dříve odesláno {time}", the meta "Poslal správce ručně, {n} příjemce" or "Odeslal web podle nastavení, …", and the sections that sending carried as grouped inset rows (`h3.ui-group-title`, non-interactive `div.ui-list__row` with title, meta and value): "Dárci" (registered by the month's last day, new in the month), "Dary" (received in the month, its paired part, received since 1 January, each with the gift count and `formatMinorUnits` crowns), "Platby bez dárce", "Banka" (the last success or "Zatím žádné", and the last failure with its `IMPORT_FAILURE_LABELS` text, `import-failure-labels.ts`, shared with the administration home). A month sent before the sendings were kept (`recomputed`) shows one block "Odesláno {firstSentAt}" with the warning callout "Spočteno z dnešních dat, odeslaný e-mail se mohl lišit. Web tehdy ještě neukládal, co přesně odešlo, proto chybí i stav stahování z banky." and no "Banka". A month never sent (404 `donations/sent-report-not-found`) and an address that is no `YYYY-MM` (nothing is asked) say "Za {month} žádný přehled neodešel." or "Takový přehled neodešel." with the link "Zpět na měsíční e-mail"; another failure says "Přehled se nepodařilo načíst." with "Zkusit znovu" (`appNeedsConnection`; the administration's device copy holds no reports, so the page needs the server); 401 and 403 through `LogPageAccess`. Texts under `admin.sentReport.*`. Specs in `report/sent/admin-sent-report.spec.ts`; Playwright in `e2e/admin-report.spec.ts`, the axe case, `e2e/theme.spec.ts` and the device step `admin-sent-report`.
- `backups/` (`/sprava/zalohy`, "Zálohy"): `h1` focused on open, an intro saying that the site backs up every day and tries a restore once a week, and what the page offers. Two cards from `backups()`. "Stav záloh": a `role="alert"` box listing one plain sentence per problem (`BACKUP_PROBLEM_HELP` in `backup-texts.ts`: what it means for the data and what to do, usually a button on this page or copying the message for the technician and sending it to the server administrator, named as "správce serveru, který web nainstaloval" so the board knows whom to send it to (Pavel 30. 10. 2026), a wording that also fits the overview) with the technical sentence (`BACKUP_PROBLEM_TEXTS`) under "Technické podrobnosti" (the admin overview shows the same plain sentences, without the technical ones); when a problem needs the server administrator (`needsTechnician` in `backup-message.ts`: the folder missing, a missing, failed or stale backup, a failed test restore) the shared copy button "Zkopírovat zprávu pro technika" under the problems copies `technicianMessage(view)`, plain text with the plain sentences, the technical ones and the last backup and test restore (time, file, size or table count, or the failure with its error), never anything about the backup key, or the green "Zálohování je v pořádku."; a `dl` "Poslední záloha" ("{time} ({size})", "Nepovedla se {time}." or "Zatím žádná."), "Poslední zkušební obnova" (the same shapes with the table count, "Zatím žádná. Podle plánu běží v neděli 4:00." from the plan) and "Poslední obnova", each failure with its error under "Technické podrobnosti" (`RunLine`, rendered through one `ng-template`); "Soubory k obnově" only while the restore folder holds files, with their state (waits for the container's next start, restored, failed with the data kept, skipped); "Na vyřízení čeká N požadavků." (`role="status"`) when request files wait. The buttons "Zálohovat teď" (`.primary`) and "Ověřit obnovu teď" post a request, read "Zálohujeme…" / "Ověřujeme obnovu…" and poll `backups()` every 3 s (`JOB_POLL_MS`) up to 20 times (`JOB_MAX_POLLS`) until a run carrying the request id appears: a `role="status"` line ("Záloha {file} je hotová ({size}).", "Zkušební obnova zálohy {file} proběhla: N tabulek.") or a `role="alert"` line with the run's error; after 20 polls "Kontejner nfnn-backup si požadavek do minuty nevzal. …"; a refused request (409) shows the problem's title and detail. Both buttons are disabled while a job runs, while `restorePending` or when `available` is false (then a line says they wait for the problem above); while at least one backup is listed, a warning callout says the backups sit on the same disk as the site, recommends downloading the newest one once a month with Stáhnout to keep elsewhere and says they are encrypted (without a backup there is nothing to download, so it is left out). After the card "Zálohy na serveru" (below) the card "Klíč k zálohám" (`backup-key-card`, `app-backup-key-card`, `id="klic-k-zaloham"`, from `backups().key`): an intro (encrypted backups, restorable only with this key, every view confirmed by the second factor and recorded in the history); without a key a danger callout (no new backups, update the app in ZimaOS); unconfirmed a warning callout "Klíč si zatím nikdo neuložil. Uložit ho má někdo ze správní rady, kdo má přístup do správy, ne technik. …", confirmed "Uložení klíče jste potvrdili {time}."; "Zobrazit klíč…" opens a form in the card: for an administrator with the code app the card first asks `AuthStore.hasPasskeys()` (`GET /api/donors/me/passkeys`, a failure counts as none) when the browser supports passkeys: with a passkey it offers the passkey first (Jakub 2026-10-29: the note, "Zobrazit klíč" runs the ceremony and has focus, "Nebo bez přístupového klíče: Zadat kód z aplikace" switches to the code), otherwise "Kód z aplikace" (six digits, focused) plus "Nebo bez kódu: Potvrdit přístupovým klíčem" where passkeys work; for one with passkeys only a note and the passkey ceremony (`AuthStore.confirmWith(null)`, as on `/pripojeni`); "Zobrazit klíč" ("Ověřujeme…") posts `AdminApi.revealBackupKey` and "Zrušit" closes; a refusal stays as a `role="alert"` line with the code field focused and selected. The revealed key (focus moves to it) shows in `app-backup-key-panel` with "Mám uloženo, ověřit" and "Skrýt klíč"; the first hides the words and shows the three word fields of the positions the reveal drew (as in the setup, focus on the first), "Ukázat slova znovu" and "Potvrdit uložení" ("Potvrzujeme…", `AdminApi.confirmBackupKey` with `{ words }`; a 409 `backup-key-confirmation-expired` hides the key and says to show it again; success hides the key again, says "Děkujeme, uložení klíče jsme si poznamenali. Klíč jsme zase skryli." and replaces the view's `key`) and "Skrýt klíč"; focus returns to "Zobrazit klíč…". The key lives only in the component's signal and is never stored. "Zálohy na serveru" (after the status card, Jakub 2026-11-11 chose the designer's variant 2 over listing every backup): a `ui-list` of two rows, "Nejnovější záloha" (its Prague time · kind as meta, its size as value, a chevron), which opens the backup sheet, and "Všechny zálohy" ("Po dnech, nejnovější nahoře", the count as value), a link to `/sprava/zalohy/historie`; "Zatím žádná záloha." when empty. The page keeps its length however many backups the server holds (about 70 at a 30-day retention). The backup sheet (`backup-sheet`, `app-backup-sheet`, `<app-dialog presentation="sheet">`, also on the history page) names the backup's time as its heading, the kind ("Plánovaná", "Na požádání", "Před obnovou", "Jiná", `backup-kinds.ts`) and size (`backup-format.ts` `formatSize`: kB rounded up below 1 MB, else MB or GB with one decimal, NBSP before the unit) and the file name, then "Stáhnout" with "Záloha je zašifrovaná. Uložte ji mimo server; otevřít jde jen s klíčem k zálohám." and, apart from it, the danger outline "Obnovit web z této zálohy…" (disabled while a job runs, while a restore is pending or the folder is missing) with "Než obnova začne, ukážeme, co se ztratí a co zůstane, a zeptáme se znovu."; the restore button closes the sheet and opens `restore-dialog` (`app-restore-dialog` in `<app-dialog>`: "Obnovit databázi ze zálohy?", the file and its time, five points: everything since the backup is lost, except what changed for security or data protection (erased donors stay erased, passwords, passkeys, second factors and administrator roles stay as they are now and the administration history keeps every entry, issue #459), receipts issued since then disappear but their numbers are never handed out again (a new download gets a new number), the current state is saved first as a "Před obnovou" backup, the site is down for a few minutes, the restore ends every sign-in, so everyone, the administrator and the donors, signs in again and the connector in claude.ai has to be connected again; "Zrušit" focused, the danger button "Obnovit a restartovat web", "Předáváme obnovu…" while posting, a refusal as an alert in the dialog). Once accepted, the request id and file go to `sessionStorage` (`nfnn.restore-request`, in try/catch) and the card "Obnova ze zálohy" replaces the page with a `role="status"` line while `SetupApi.watchRestart(RESTORE_MAX_POLLS)` (300 × 3 s) waits for the restart, then reloads: the run with the stored request id gives "Databáze je obnovená ze zálohy {file}. Stav před obnovou je uložený jako záloha „Před obnovou“." or the alert with its error, no such run "Kontejner nfnn-backup si obnovu do pěti minut nevzal …"; when the wait runs out, "Web se ještě nevrátil …" with "Zkontrolovat znovu". Because the request survives a sign-in in `sessionStorage`, the notice also appears after the session was lost during the restart. The status card has a row "Obnova" with the last restore. "Stáhnout" in the sheet fetches the dump as a blob with the token and saves it through `saveBlob` (`backup-downloads.ts`, `BackupDownloads`, provided per page), "Stahujeme…" meanwhile, a failure as a `role="alert"` line in the sheet. Loading and failure as on the other pages; 401 ends the session and opens the sign-in dialog, 403 goes to `/darce/prehled`. After the key card the card "Plán záloh" (`backup-schedule-card`, `app-backup-schedule-card`): "Denní záloha" (`type=time`), "Zkušební obnova" with a day `select` (pondělí … neděle) and a time, "Uložit plán" ("Ukládáme…" while saving, disabled while `available` is false), a refusal as a `role="alert"` line in the card, and a warning callout when the container reports no plan ("Plán se na serveru zatím neprojevil …", with the older-image explanation under "Technické podrobnosti") or another one; a saved plan replaces the view's `schedule` and the card says under its button "Plán záloh jsme uložili: denní záloha 2:30, zkušební obnova v sobotu 5:15." (`backup-plan.ts`: `describePlan`, `formatPlanTime` without the leading zero, the locative days). The history page (`history/admin-backup-history`, `/sprava/zalohy/historie`, "Všechny zálohy", back to `/sprava/zalohy`): `h1` focused on open, "Nejnovější nahoře. Klepnutím na zálohu ji stáhnete nebo z ní web obnovíte.", the same `backups()` grouped by Prague day (`backup-days.ts` `backupDays`: a `ui-group-title` "sobota 7. 11. 2026 · 3 zálohy", then a `ui-list--inset` of rows with the time, the kind and the size), each row opening the backup sheet. No paging: the API answers every backup at once and the server keeps 30 days. The sheet's restore does not restore here: it navigates to `/sprava/zalohy` with the file in the navigation state (`restore`, `backup-paths.ts`), and that page opens its restore dialog for the file once the backups have loaded, so the confirmation, the wait for the restart and the outcome stay in one place. 401 and 403 as on the logs pages (`LogPageAccess`). Specs in `admin-backups.spec.ts`, `history/admin-backup-history.spec.ts`, `backup-days.spec.ts`, `backup-key-card.spec.ts`, `backup-schedule-card.spec.ts`, `backup-plan.spec.ts` and `backup-format.spec.ts`; Playwright `e2e/admin-backups.spec.ts` (the status, the newest backup and the history by day with a download from its sheet, the key shown with a code and its saving confirmed, a requested backup polled to its notice, a restore chosen in the history's sheet, confirmed in the dialog on the backups page and reported after the restart, the unavailable folder disabling the actions) and the axe case for the page with problems, the revealed key with a refused confirmation, the backup sheet, the restore dialog, a busy action, the history page and its sheet.
- `alerts/` (`/sprava/upozorneni`, "Upozornění", slice 2.28; the look of grouped rows since the alerts look PR, Jakub 2026-11-07 after Apple Music's "Oznámení"): `h1` focused on open, an intro saying what is watched and that an alert names no person or amount. Loads `push()`; 409 `operations/push-unavailable` shows a `role="alert"` box that the server cannot push, any other failure the retry box with the problem through `describeProblem`, 401 and 403 as on the other pages; the count of `openAlerts` (missing counts as none) goes to `AppBadge.show`. The page is `.ui-groups` of sections, each a `.ui-group-title` `h2` over a `ui-list--inset` group with `.ui-group-note` footnotes and its `app-live-notice look="note"`, so it can stand inside a sheet as well. "Co se hlídá": three rows, each a link to its page ("Import plateb z banky" → `/sprava`, "Zálohy" → `/sprava/zalohy`, "Odesílání e-mailů" → `/sprava/nastaveni`) with the badge "Bez poruchy" (green) or "Nefunguje" (amber) and, for an open alert, "Nefunguje od {Prague time}" (`formatBankTime`); where the group is narrower than `tabs` in the root text size (200 % text on a small phone) the badge moves under the title (container `alerts-watched`), so no title breaks inside a word. "Toto zařízení" (its `h2` takes the focus after turning on or off, `tabindex="-1"`): the subscription with `current: true` shows one switch row per area (`.ui-switch`, `role="switch"`, named by the area and described by what it reports: "Když se platby z banky přestanou stahovat a když se zase stahují.", "Když záloha selže nebo chybí a když je zase v pořádku.", "Když server e-mail neodešle a když zase odesílá."; each change `changePushAlerts` with the three switches, shown at once and put back on a failure; while one saves, the switches are `aria-disabled` rather than disabled, so the focused one keeps the focus, and a change meanwhile is undone; "Uloženo pro toto zařízení: {area} – zapnuto/vypnuto."), a footnote with its service and day, its last delivery ("Naposledy doručeno …" or "Zatím nic nedoručeno.", "Nedoručeno N× po sobě.") and the destructive row "Vypnout na tomto zařízení" (`removePushSubscription`, then `SERVICE_WORKER_PUSH.unsubscribe`). When the browser no longer holds a subscription (`browserSubscription()` answers `not-subscribed`, read after each load while a worker controls the page) a warning footnote says so and the action row "Zapnout znovu" follows; when notifications are now blocked it says how to allow them. Without a current subscription the answer's `deviceSession` decides first: when it is not `true` (missing counts as false; a sign-in whose token names no device, `sid`: one opened before slice 2.9) one footnote says that this sign-in is older than the site's list of devices, so alerts cannot be turned on in it, and that signing out and in again makes it possible, and the browser is never asked; otherwise `core/push/push-availability.ts` (`pushAvailability(window, push)`) decides: `available` offers the action row "Zapnout upozornění na tomto zařízení" described by its footnote, whose click handler first calls `SERVICE_WORKER_PUSH.requestPermission()` synchronously (Safari and Firefox prompt only inside the user's gesture) and only on `granted` `requestSubscription(publicKey)` and then `subscribePush` with the endpoint and the two keys (alerts left out, so all on), after which the list is read again (the server may have replaced a device of an older session with the same endpoint); a refusal of the server unsubscribes the browser again (409 `operations/push-subscription-limit` with the page's own sentence pointing to removing a device under "Další zařízení"), a refused permission or subscribe says how to allow notifications; otherwise one footnote says why: `home-screen` (an iPhone or iPad outside the home screen app: add the site to the home screen, open it from there and sign in there), `no-push` (no Push API), `no-service-worker` (no worker controls the page yet; re-read on every `controllerchange`) or `denied` (`Notification.permission`: allow notifications for the site in the browser settings). "Zkušební upozornění", shown while the administrator has any device: the action row "Poslat zkušební upozornění" (`testPush()`), described by its footnote (where it goes and what to check when it does not arrive within a minute: notifications allowed, no Do Not Disturb), its outcome a note between them ("Zkušební upozornění jsme předali k doručení na {n} zařízení.", its own sentence when it reached none, the failed and the removed devices in Czech plurals, `pluralCategory`, the list read again when a device was removed; the 429 of the limit through `describeProblem`). "Další zařízení": the other subscriptions as rows "{Google|Apple|Mozilla|Microsoft}, zapnuto {day}" with the last delivery and the failures in a row under it and "{n} ze 3" switches on, each opening a sheet (`app-dialog`, `aria-haspopup="dialog"`) titled the same with its three switch rows ("Uloženo pro {service} ({day}): …" in the sheet's note) and the destructive row "Odebrat zařízení" (`aria-label` "Odebrat zařízení {service}, zapnuto {day}", "Odebíráme…" while it runs; the sheet closes and the section's `h2` takes the focus after the removal), or the footnote "Na jiném zařízení upozornění zapnutá nemáte." without a group. Turning on here marks the device as the administrator's and turning off removes the mark (`device-alert-marks.ts`, below).
- `alerts/offer/` (`app-alerts-offer`, in `shell/admin-shell`, Jakub 2026-11-10): the sheet "Zapnout upozornění?" of the installed app. Once per start of the shell, after the first navigation, when `SESSION_JAR.installed`, the administration is unlocked (`ADMINISTRATION_LOCK`) and `Connectivity` is online, and not on `/sprava/upozorneni`: unless `DeviceAlertMarks.offerHidden()` or `pushAvailability` is other than `available`, it loads `push()` and, when the session names a device and none of the subscriptions is `current`, either subscribes without a question (permission `granted` and `ownedBy` this administrator: `requestSubscription`, `subscribePush`, no test) or opens the sheet (`presentation="sheet"`, title focused at each step): "Zapnout" (`appNeedsConnection`) asks the browser inside the tap, subscribes (all kinds on), marks the owner and sends `testPush()`, then "Upozornění jsou zapnutá" ("Poslali jsme zkušební upozornění…" or that the test could not go out) with "Hotovo" and "Upravit druhy" (`/sprava/upozorneni`); a refused permission or subscribe shows "Upozornění jsou vypnutá" with where to allow notifications (Nastavení → Oznámení on an iPhone) and hides the offer; a refusal of the server unsubscribes the browser and offers "Zkusit znovu" (409 `operations/push-subscription-limit` with its own sentence); "Teď ne" or dismissing the first step hides it. `alerts/device-alert-marks.ts` (`DeviceAlertMarks`, root): `offerHidden`/`hideOffer` over `nfnn.alerts-offer.hidden-until` (30 days), `ownedBy`/`markOwner`/`clearOwner` over `nfnn.alerts.device-owner` (SHA-256 of `nfnn.alerts.device-owner:` and the donor id, hex); storage that throws counts as empty.
- `logs/` (`/sprava/logy`, "Logy a diagnostika", redone as iOS-style pages in #543, variant A of the designer's three, Jakub 2026-11-07): a hub and a page for each job, each with its `h1` focused on open, the header's back button to the hub (the routes' `parent` is `ADMIN_LOGS_PATH`) and its own loading line and failure with "Zkusit znovu", so one failed request never blanks the others; 401 ends the session and opens the sign-in dialog and 403 goes to `/darce/prehled` (`log-page-access.ts`, `LogPageAccess.leavesOn`). The hub (`admin-logs`) loads `logFiles()` and `diagnostics()` separately into three `ui-list ui-list--inset` groups under `h2.ui-group-title`: "Stav serveru", one row to `/sprava/logy/stav` with `app-health-icon` (a circle with a tick, a triangle or an octagon with a cross, coloured success, warning or danger, so the shape alone tells them apart) and "Bez poruchy", "Něco potřebuje pozornost" or "Porucha" over the first finding (or "Databáze odpovídá a složky jsou připojené."); "Varování a chyby dnes" (`today/logs-today`, from variant C, Jakub 2026-11-07), shown once the files hold `backend.log`: the 50 newest warnings and errors of `backend.log` from the existing search (`levelPattern('spring', 'warnings')`), only those of the server's Prague day (`diagnostics().now`, the browser's day while the diagnostics are not there), the same level, class and message counted once at its newest time (`today/today-problems.ts`, an amber "n×"), at most five as rows with the level icon, the level word hidden for screen readers first, the message in two lines and the time · class, each leading to the reader at that line (`?od=<offset>`), then "Všechna varování a chyby" (the reader with `?uroven=varovani`), "Dnes žádná varování ani chyby" when none and "Prohledáno posledních 50 varování a chyb." when the 50 do not reach back to the day's start; its own loading line and retry (`currentLogFiles`: a name equal to its series, in the order `backend.log`, `nginx-access.log`, `backup.log`, `mcp.log`, then any other) with the name in mono and "Aplikace", "Požadavky na web", "Zálohy" or "Konektor" · size · changed (`log-files.ts`), then "Starší soubory" with their count to `/sprava/logy/soubory`, a red callout when the backend does not see the folder and "Složka logů je prázdná." when empty; "Nástroje", rows to "Úrovně logování" ("n dočasně" while a level is raised) and "Výpis vláken". `server-health.ts` (`serverHealth`): a failure when the database does not answer or a folder is not mounted, attention when the heap is at 90 % of its maximum or more (`HEAP_ATTENTION_SHARE`), threads wait for a database connection, or a mounted folder has less than a tenth free (`DISK_ATTENTION_FREE_SHARE`), each with its Czech sentence. `/sprava/logy/soubory` (`files/admin-log-files`, "Starší soubory"): the rotated files grouped by series under the series' Czech name (`olderLogFileGroups`), newest first, each row the name, size and time. `/sprava/logy/soubor?nazev=<file>` (`reader/admin-log-reader`, the file name as the `h1`; the name rides in the query because the service worker serves the app shell only for addresses whose last segment has no dot; redone as the iOS-style reader of variant A in the logs reader PR, Jakub 2026-11-07): the address may also open the window around a byte offset (`od`, the entry there highlighted and focused) or a level filter (`uroven=varovani|chyby`); the file's lines read as entries newest first (`reader/log-lines.ts`: the format follows the series, `backend.log` Spring Boot with level, thread and class, `mcp.log` time, level and message, `nginx-access.log` a request whose 5xx is an error and 4xx a warning, `backup.log` and any other file time and message; a line that does not start with a time belongs to the entry above it, so a stack trace stays with its entry), grouped under their Prague day (`ui-group-title`) in `ui-list--inset` rows (the look since the log look PR, an iOS designer's spec, Jakub 2026-11-12: "Logy nejsou pěkně formátované a barevné"): a button per entry with `reader/level-icon` in the first column (a filled octagon with an exclamation mark for ERROR in `--color-danger`, a filled triangle for WARN in `--color-log-warn`, an outlined circle with an i for INFO and a small ring for DEBUG and TRACE, both in `--color-text-soft`; CanvasText under `forced-colors`), then a line with the level's Czech word hidden for screen readers, the class's last name (semibold, one line with an ellipsis) and the Prague time at its end (`pragueTime`, `H:MM:SS`), the message in three lines at `--text-lg` (muted for DEBUG and TRACE) split by `reader/message-segments.ts` (`segmentsOf`, pure, rendered by bindings only) into prose and code: file names, URLs, instants, UUIDs, dates and date ranges, numeric ranges, `key=value`, qualified names, constants, CamelCase, `HTTP 422`, numbers with a unit and slash paths in `--font-mono` and `--color-log-code`, an opening bracket or closing punctuation kept with its code so it never stands alone at a line end; an ERROR row has a `--color-danger` bar at its start on `--color-danger-bg`, a WARN row a `--color-log-warn` bar (a double bar for ERROR under `forced-colors`); under the message the exception's simple name (`exceptionOf`) and "+ n řádků" counting only its non-blank frames, or only the non-blank lines under a message without one. The two tokens are in every theme, light, dark and with more contrast, and `check-themes.mjs` keeps `--color-log-code` at 4.5:1 on the cells, the danger tint and the focused row, the body texts at 4.5:1 on `--color-danger-bg`, and `--color-log-warn` and `--color-danger` at 3:1 as bars and icons. The tail (20 kB, `WINDOW_BYTES`) loads first; "Načíst novější řádky" above the list asks from the end ("Žádné nové řádky." when the file has not grown) and "Načíst starší řádky" under it the window ending at the start, merged by byte offset (`reader/log-timeline.ts`, each line once) with the focus on the first older entry. "Hledat v souboru" (a visible label, the backend's case-insensitive regular expression, at most 200 characters with the level filter) and, where the format writes levels, the segments Vše / Varování (warnings and errors) / Chyby (`appRadioGroup`): a filter or a search asks `searchLog` (`levelPattern`, combined as `<level>.*(?:<text>)`), lists the matches the same way newest first with "Načíst starší řádky" by `before = scannedFrom`, and a refused or too slow pattern clears them and shows the problem at the field (`role="alert"`); "Chyby z prohlížečů" (only in `backend.log`) searches `Browser error`. Tapping an entry opens a sheet (`app-dialog presentation="sheet"`): "{Chyba} v {time}", Kdy, Vlákno, Třída (as the server writes it, shortened by Spring Boot's pattern) or Požadavek, "Zpráva" with the same segments, "Výjimka" with the package, the simple name in bold, its message and the frames as a list ("Výpis zásobníku", `frameOf`: the package muted, `Class.method` semibold, the place in `--color-log-code`, wrapping with a hanging indent) or, without an exception, the lines under the message in a `pre`, "Kopírovat záznam" (the entry as written) and, for a match, "Zobrazit okolí", which loads the window around it into the list with the filter back on Vše and the entry highlighted and focused. Under the list `<app-copy-button what="načtené záznamy">` copies what is loaded. The loading line and a failure with "Zkusit znovu" (a missing file shows the backend's problem) as on the other pages; nothing of the file is stored in the browser and nothing is shared beyond the clipboard. `/sprava/logy/urovne` (`levels/admin-log-levels`, "Úrovně logování", redone in the log levels PR, variant A, Jakub 2026-11-07): the intro (raise only while looking for a fault; it goes back by itself after the time and after a restart; audited), then "Dočasně změněno" while a level is raised: a row per override with the logger in mono, "{before} → {level} · vrátí se samo za n min ({end})" (`levels/level-texts.ts`, `remainingText`, hours and minutes above an hour, "vrací se právě teď" once the time is up; the page counts down every 30 s, `COUNTDOWN_TICK_MS`, a display only: the backend resets the level) and "Vrátit" (`aria-label` "Vrátit úroveň loggeru {name}", "Vracíme…"); "Sledované loggery": a row per logger the backend lists with what it writes in Czech (`loggerDescription`: Vše ostatní, Aplikace, Import z Fio, Požadavky na API, Přihlašování a oprávnění, Dotazy do databáze, bez hodnot, Změny schématu databáze, Odesílání e-mailů), the name in mono, "zděděno" when inherited and the effective level, and "Jiný logger…". A row opens a sheet (`app-dialog presentation="sheet"`): "Úroveň loggeru {name}" (or the field Logger, ROOT or a dotted Java name, for another), the levels TRACE…OFF as a radio list with a tick (`appRadioGroup`; the raised level or DEBUG chosen; TRACE disabled with "TRACE jen pro cz.novanadeje.app" outside the site's own code, `canTrace`, as the backend refuses it), "Na jak dlouho" 15 min / 30 min / 1 h / 4 h (`DURATIONS`, 30 min chosen) and "Nastavit na {time}", which puts the level, closes the sheet, says "Logger {name} má úroveň {level} do {time}." and reloads the list; a refusal (a protected logger) stays in the sheet as an alert. `/sprava/logy/stav` (`state/admin-server-state`, "Stav serveru"): the verdict as an inset row (`h2` with the icon, "k {time}", the findings listed), "Obnovit stav" (a failed refresh keeps the last state with "Stav serveru se nepodařilo obnovit."), then rows like the iOS Settings' About with only a short value at the right and the rest in the line under the title, so nothing runs off at 200 % text: Aplikace (Paměť "22 %" over used of max and outside the heap, Vlákna, Zatížení "na 1 jádře / na n jádrech", Úklid paměti "n×" over the total time, Java, Běží od), Databáze (Odpovídá "za n ms" or "ne" over the attempt's time, Spojení "n v práci" over "volná n z m", Čekající vlákna), Složky (per folder "n % volno" over free of total, or "není připojená"), and Dočasné úrovně while one is raised. `/sprava/logy/vlakna` (`threads/admin-thread-dump`, "Výpis vláken"): the dump's threads counted by state (`thread-states.ts`: Běží, Zablokovaná, Čekají, Čekají na čas, Nová, Skončená, an unknown state under its own name) under "Celkem", the whole dump wrapped in a `pre` with `<app-copy-button what="výpis vláken">`, and "Načíst znovu". Nothing of a log, a dump or the diagnostics is stored in the browser. Specs beside each page over `logs/testing/log-page-harness.ts` (the route opened through `RouterTestingHarness`, the text read with a space between elements, as a screen reader reads it); Playwright `e2e/admin-logs.spec.ts` with the mocks in `e2e/admin-logs-api.ts` (backend.log from the hub: the newest entries, older lines, the errors filter and an error's surroundings, the older files on a phone, a level raised and reset, the server state and the thread dump on a phone), the axe walk over all six pages, the theme check and the device steps `admin-logs`, `admin-log-file` and `admin-server-state`.
- `people/` (`/sprava/lide-fondu`, "Lidé fondu"; the API in `features/content/people-api.ts`, `PeopleApi`: `load()`, `mine()`, `add`, `update`, `remove`, `order`, `saveNote`, `saveFootnote`, `setWebSignature`, `removeWebSignature` over `/api/admin/content/people…`; Jakub 2026-10-30): `h1` focused on open, an intro saying where the people show and that the site keeps its own block while nobody is here. The card "Lidé v pořadí na webu" is an ordered list in the shared block `.ui-person-list` (`styles/ui/_person-list.scss`, used by the administrators too): a small round portrait (`app-avatar`, the initials without one), the name, the role and badges ("Karta s portrétem", "Ve správní radě (…)", "Podepisuje potvrzení", "Propojeno s účtem na webu", a `ul`), under them the person's signatures as labelled previews (Jakub 2026-10-31, so they can be checked on a phone; each a `figure` with its `figcaption`, side by side and wrapping at 390 px): "Podpis na webu", the public web signature (`?w=480`, at most 48 px high like the site's, drawn as ink through `.ui-ink` as in O nás, `alt` "Podpis na webu: <name>", `loading="lazy"`), and "Podpis na potvrzení", the receipt signature read from the browser-only `GET /api/admin/donations/receipt-signatures/{personId}` and drawn by `app-signature-artwork` (the vector through `[attr.d]`, a scan as `img`, label "Podpis na potvrzení: <name>", at most 160 px wide). The receipt previews are fetched for the people the signatory view lists in `signatures`, once per person and upload (kept by `personId@uploadedAt`, so reading the signatory again fetches only a changed or new signature); while one loads the line says "Načítáme náhled…", and a failed one only "Náhled se nepodařilo načíst." under its caption, never an alert, and the buttons "Výš", "Níž" (`aria-label` "Výš: <name>", disabled at the ends; the order is saved at once and focus stays on the moved person) and "Upravit" (`aria-expanded`, `aria-controls="person-editor"`), then "Přidat osobu" (disabled at 30). The editor card under it holds the name (required), the role, the checkboxes for the card and the board line, the board note while on the board, the fieldset "E-maily jen pro správu" with the personal and the fund e-mail (each optional, checked against the backend's shape, never shown on the site), the portrait (`FileButton` and `NewsApi.uploadImage`, preview in an `app-avatar`, "Bez portrétu"), and saving ("Přidat a ukázat na webu" / "Uložit a ukázat na webu") or closing; for a saved person also the web signature (uploaded and set at once, PNG with a transparent background preferred, replaced or removed; the hint says it is never the receipt signature and only the browser sets it) then the part "Na potvrzení o daru" (slice 1.16, reads `receiptSignatory()` of `admin-api.ts` and keeps its state: "Načítáme, kdo potvrzení podepisuje…" while it loads and, when it fails, the alert "Nepodařilo se zjistit, kdo podepisuje potvrzení." with "Zjistit znovu", which moves the focus to the part's heading "Na potvrzení o daru" (`tabindex="-1"`) while it asks again, never a claim that nobody signs; until it is known the part offers no choice and "Odebrat osobu z webu" stays disabled; when it arrives after the signatory's editor opened, their receipt role is filled in): for the person who signs the line that they sign and that only one person signs; otherwise the checkbox "Podepisuje potvrzení" with a hint naming who signs now or that nobody does; then the field "Funkce na potvrzení" (required, at most 120, Jakub 2026-10-31: the receipt's own role, the role on the site stays) and "Uložit pro potvrzení" (`chooseReceiptSignatory`, `PUT /api/admin/donations/receipt-signatory`; "Potvrzení vystavená od teď podepisuje … s funkcí …"), and the receipt signature `<app-receipt-signature [personId] [signs] (busyChange)>` (`features/admin/receipt-signature/`, moved from the settings in 1.16; it drops its pending requests when another person is shown, reports a running upload or removal so the page blocks switching people and "Zavřít bez uložení" until it answers, moves the focus to its heading on "Zkusit znovu", and for a person who does not sign says "Podpis je uložený. Potvrzení ho ponesou, až je bude tato osoba podepisovat.", asks "Tato osoba potvrzení teď nepodepisuje. Odebrat její podpis?" and answers "Podpis je odebraný."; per person `GET`/`POST`/`DELETE /api/admin/donations/receipt-signatures/{personId}`; the hint says it is printed on the receipts this person signs and never public, best an SVG of filled shapes up to 200 kB, else a JPEG/PNG/WebP scan up to 10 MB; "Podpis není nahraný. …" or the scan or vector in a dashed frame with `forced-color-adjust: none` and "Nahráno …"; "Nahrát podpis na potvrzení" / "Nahrát jiný podpis na potvrzení", a refused SVG with the backend's title and detail, "Odebrat podpis z potvrzení" with the group "Odebrat podpis z potvrzení?"; texts under `admin.signature.*`, spec `receipt-signature.spec.ts`); a list row of the signing person carries the badge "Podepisuje potvrzení"; and the removal (its intro says the portrait and the web signature leave the site and the database, from backups within 30 days, that the receipt signature goes on no further receipt, and that issued receipts keep their copies of the signature and the portrait) with an inline confirmation ("Odebrat a smazat", "Zrušit"), which for the person who signs is replaced by a warning that another signatory must be chosen first. The card "Slovo zakladatelů" has a textarea of at most 600 characters, and the card "Řádek pod správní radou" one text field of at most 200 characters ("Uložit řádek"); an empty text removes either. Every outcome is a line in `app-live-notice` (list, editor, signature, note, line); a 401 sends to the sign-in, a 403 to `/darce/prehled`. Spec `admin-people.spec.ts`, `people-api.spec.ts`; e2e `e2e/admin-people.spec.ts` (with choosing the signatory and the receipt signature: a scan, a vector, a refused SVG, a scan and a vector replacing each other, the vector in a dark forced-colours mode), an axe case in `accessibility.spec.ts`, a line in `theme.spec.ts`.
- `focal-point-picker/` (slice 2.23b): `<app-focal-point-picker inputId [imageId] [shapes] [hint] [(point)]>` shows the photo (`?w=960`) as one button "Fotka, klepnutím nastavíte bod výřezu"; a tap places the point where it lands (percent of the image's box, kept within 0–100; a click from the keyboard, `detail` 0, is ignored), the arrow keys move it by 5 from the centre or the point, a marker shows it and "Zrušit bod" removes it. A polite live line says "Bod výřezu: X % zleva, Y % shora." or "Bez bodu výřezu: web fotku ořízne jako dosud.", and a figure per shape in `shapes` previews the crop live (`?w=480`, `appFocalPoint`). The news and project editors show it in place of their former preview with the shapes "Karta v Aktualitách"/"Karta v Pomáháme" and "Nahoře v článku", the About page without previews and with the hint that the site still shows that photo whole; on saving, each page sends a moved point to `PUT /api/admin/content/media/{id}` first (`PhotosApi`, `features/content/photos-api.ts`), then saves the item only when something else changed too and the point went through; a placed point becomes the saved one at once, so a failed save of the item afterwards sends only the item again. The news and project editors count a moved point as unsaved (the leave guard and `beforeunload`); the About page, which has no leave guard, sends it with its Uložit button.
- `about/` (`/sprava/o-nas`, "Fotka v sekci O nás"; the API in `features/content/about-api.ts`, `AboutApi`: `load()` → `GET /api/admin/content/about`, `save(request)` → `PUT`, `AboutResponse`, `AboutPhotoResponse`, `AboutRequest`; Jakub 2026-10-30: the photo shows a child, so it is uploaded here instead of living in the repository): `h1` focused on open, an intro saying where the photo shows, that the stock photo stays until one is saved and that it can be removed at any time. The card "Fotka" shows the saved or the freshly uploaded photo as a preview (`?w=480`) or "Fotka zatím není nahraná, web ukazuje ilustrační fotku.", the `app-file-button` "Nahrát fotku" / "Vyměnit fotku" (JPEG, PNG or WebP up to 10 MB checked before the upload, which goes at once through `NewsApi.uploadImage`; "Nahráváme fotku…", then "Fotka je nahraná. Na web se dostane, až ji uložíte."), and with a photo the fields "Popis pro čtečky (nepovinné)" and "Popisek pod fotkou (nepovinné)" (200 characters each) and "Uložit a ukázat na webu" ("Ukládáme…"; the texts trimmed, blank as null), then "Uloženo. Fotka je na webu v sekci O nás." or the backend's refusal. The danger card "Odebrání fotky" (only with a saved photo) says the photo leaves the site and the database at once and the backups within 30 days, and "Odebrat fotku z webu" asks inline "Opravdu fotku odebrat z webu a smazat?" with "Odebrat a smazat" ("Odebíráme…") / "Zrušit" (focused on open); the removal `PUT`s no photo and no texts, says "Fotku jsme z webu odebrali a smazali. Sekce O nás ukazuje zase ilustrační fotku." and focuses the upload button. 401 and 403 as on the other admin pages. Specs in `admin-about.spec.ts`, e2e in `e2e/admin-about.spec.ts`.
- `news/` (`/sprava/aktuality`, "Aktuality"; the API in `features/content/news-api.ts`, `NewsApi`, with `slugFromText` and `mediaUrl(id, width)`): `admin-news` lists every article from `GET /api/admin/content/news` as a table (cards up to 640 px) Titulek (a link to the editor), Štítek ("bez štítku"), Stav ("Koncept" amber, "Na webu od {date}" green when `live`, "Zveřejní se {date}" accent while the date is still ahead), Upraveno (`formatBankTime`); "Nová aktualita" opens the editor; "Zatím tu žádná aktualita není. …" when empty. After a deletion the list says "Aktualitu „{title}“ jsme smazali." (the title comes in the navigation's `state.deleted`, read through `Router.currentNavigation()`). `admin-news-editor` (`/sprava/aktuality/nova` and `/sprava/aktuality/:id`, `h1` "Nová aktualita" / "Úprava aktuality" focused on open; the link "← Aktuality"): the card "Obsah" with the form Titulek (required, 120), Adresa na webu (nepovinné) (made from the title with `slugFromText` until the administrator types into it; the hint names `nfnn.cz/aktuality/<slug>` and, for a published article whose address changed, adds "Pozor: dosavadní odkaz nfnn.cz/aktuality/{old} po uložení přestane fungovat."; pattern and 80 characters; a 409 `content/slug-taken` marks this field "Tuto adresu už má jiná aktualita nebo příběh. Zvolte prosím jinou." and focuses it), Štítek (nepovinné) (40, hint: the word above the date), Krátký souhrn (required, 300, hint: shown on the card in the list), Text (the Markdown toolbar above the textarea, required, 20 000; the hint says to separate paragraphs with an empty line, format with the buttons and check the look with the switch Náhled at the top) and Podepsáno: a `<select>` "Bez podpisu" and the people of the foundation from `PeopleApi.load()` (the article's own signer stays among them when the list cannot be read, so saving keeps it); a new article preselects the person linked to the administrator (`PeopleApi.mine()`), sent as `signerId`. Fotka (nepovinné): `<app-file-button>` "Nahrát fotku" / "Vyměnit fotku" (`accept` JPEG, PNG, WebP) that uploads at once through `POST /api/admin/content/media` ("Nahráváme fotku…", then "Fotka je nahraná. K aktualitě se připojí, až ji uložíte." in a `role="status"` region), refuses another type or more than 10 MB before sending, shows the backend's words on a refusal (413 `upload-too-large` included) and the preview `<img>` from `/api/content/media/{id}?w=480` with "Odebrat fotku" and, only with a photo, the field "Popis fotky (nepovinné)" (200, hint: what is on the photo for people who cannot see it; sent as `coverImageAlt`, null when blank or without a photo) with the line "Fotka zatím nemá popis pro nevidomé." while it is blank. Under the `h1` the `ui-segmented` group "Zobrazení editoru" switches "Upravit" / "Náhled" (`aria-pressed`, Jakub 2026-10-24): "Náhled" hides the fields (`[hidden]`, so the form keeps its values, touched and dirty state) and shows `features/admin/content-preview` (`app-content-preview [content]`, `{ kind, item }`): the line "Takhle to uvidí návštěvníci webu, včetně neuložených změn. …", the heading "Karta na úvodní stránce a v seznamu aktualit" over the public `app-news-card` without its link on the home section's background, and "Stránka aktuality" over the public `app-content-article` in preview mode; the item is a snapshot of the form taken when the preview opens (the address made from the title when blank, the uploaded photo, the chosen date), so nothing is saved or sent. The last field of the form is "Datum zveřejnění" (`type="date"`, today for a draft, the published date otherwise; its hint says who sees the article: "Aktualitu zatím vidíte jen vy. Tlačítko Uložit a zveřejnit ji pustí na web s tímto datem, …", "Aktualita je na webu, změny se ukážou hned. …" or "Na web se aktualita dostane {date}, do té doby ji vidíte jen vy. …"). The form ends in the sticky action bar (`.ui-action-bar` from `styles/ui/_action-bar.scss`, `position: sticky` at the bottom of the viewport while the form scrolls; `shared/ui/action-bar` (`appActionBar`) writes its height into `--action-bar-height` on `html`, whose `scroll-padding-bottom` then keeps a focused field above the bar on phones). The bar shows the state from the API's `live` (never the browser's clock): "Koncept" (amber), "Na webu" (green) or "Zveřejní se {date}" (`ui-badge--accent`), then "Máte neuložené změny." or "Naposledy uloženo {at}."; its buttons: for a new article or a draft "Uložit koncept" (the first submit button, so Enter in a field saves a draft) and the primary "Uložit a zveřejnit" (creates or saves, then publishes with the date; a missing date marks the field "Vyberte prosím datum zveřejnění."); for a published one the primary "Uložit a ukázat na webu" ("Uložit změny" while it waits for its date), which saves and publishes again when the date changed, "Stáhnout z webu" ("Zrušit zveřejnění" while scheduled) and, only when `live`, the link "Otevřít na webu" (`/aktuality/{slug}`, new window). A running request names itself ("Ukládáme…", "Zveřejňujeme…", "Stahujeme…") and disables the bar. Results show in the bar's `app-live-notice`: "Aktualitu jsme uložili jako koncept. Na web ji dostanete tlačítkem Uložit a zveřejnit." (the URL becomes `/sprava/aktuality/{id}` without a reload and the tab title turns to the edit title), "Změny jsme uložili.", "Změny jsme uložili a na webu už jsou.", "Změny jsme uložili. Aktualita se na webu ukáže {date}.", "Aktualita je na webu s datem {date}.", "Aktualita se na webu ukáže {date}.", "Aktualitu jsme z webu stáhli. Zůstává tu jako koncept.", and refusals through `describeProblem`. An invalid form submitted from the preview switches back to "Upravit" and focuses the first invalid field. The card "Smazání" (saved articles only): "Smazat aktualitu" opens the `role="group"` "Smazat aktualitu?" asking "Opravdu smazat aktualitu „{title}“?" with "Smazat natrvalo" ("Mažeme…") and "Zrušit" (focused when it opens; cancelling or a failure, shown in this card, returns the focus to "Smazat aktualitu"); the deletion returns to the list with its notice. Leaving the editor with unsaved changes (edited fields, or a photo uploaded and not yet saved) is held by `features/admin/unsaved-changes/unsaved-changes-guard.ts` (`unsavedChangesGuard`, a `canDeactivate` on the four editor routes in `admin.routes.ts`, calling the page's `canLeave()` from `GuardsUnsavedChanges`), which opens `unsaved-changes-dialog` ("Máte neuložené změny", "Když stránku teď opustíte, rozepsané změny i nově nahraná fotka se ztratí.", "Zůstat a uložit" focused, which closes the dialog and submits the form, and "Odejít bez uložení"; Esc, × and the backdrop stay); closing or reloading the tab meanwhile triggers the browser's own `beforeunload` question. Texts under `admin.unsavedChanges.*`. Loading, a failed load with "Zkusit znovu", 401 and 403 behave as on the other admin pages; a 404 `content/news-not-found` returns to the list. Texts under `admin.news.*` and `admin.newsEditor.*`. Specs `admin-news.spec.ts`, `admin-news-editor.spec.ts`, `unsaved-changes-guard.spec.ts`, `features/content/news-api.spec.ts` (fixtures in `features/content/testing/news-fixtures.ts`); Playwright `e2e/admin-news.spec.ts` (the list and the way to the editor, a new article with a photo checked in the preview and published, a missing title, a taken address at its field, withdrawing and deleting with the list's notice, leaving with unsaved changes, the action bar on a phone, a scheduled article) and the axe case for the list, the editor with its errors, its preview and the deletion confirmation.
- `projects/` (`/sprava/projekty`, "Pomáháme"; Jakub 2026-11-10: the section is „Pomáháme“ and one item „příběh“ everywhere; the API in `features/content/projects-api.ts`, `ProjectsApi`, the same calls against `/api/admin/content/projects`): `admin-projects` and `admin-project-editor` mirror the news pages with the project wording ("Nový příběh", "Úprava příběhu", "Příběh jsme uložili jako koncept. …", "Smazat příběh?", "Příběh „{title}“ jsme smazali.", the taken address "Tuto adresu už má jiný příběh nebo aktualita. …", the public link to `/projekty/{slug}`, the link "← Pomáháme", a 404 `content/project-not-found` returns to the list; the same unsaved-changes guard) and add the year and the category: the list has the columns Rok and Kategorie (Děti, Dospělí, Zvířata, Ostatní from `projectCategoryLabel` in `features/content/project-category.ts`, ids `content.project.category.*`) after the title and its intro points to the card Řazení na webu under the list (`project-order-card`, its own `GET`/`PUT /api/admin/content/project-order`; Jakub 2026-10-30: every choice says how it orders): three radio buttons from `PROJECT_ORDERS`, each named by its label and described (`aria-describedby`) by a sentence from `project-order.ts` (`projectOrderLabel`, `projectOrderDescription`, ids `admin.projects.order.*`), the button Uložit řazení (Ukládáme… while it runs), the saved line or the refusal in a `LiveNotice`, its own loading line and retry; a saved order makes the page ask for the list again without its loading line, so the table shows the new order; the choices use the global block `.ui-choice` (`styles/ui/_form.scss`, `__title`, `__detail`, `.is-selected`), which the pairing dialog's donor results use too; the form has the choice Kategorie after Rok (a `select` over `PROJECT_CATEGORIES`, Děti for a new project, the hint says whom the project helped and that the order is chosen in the list); the form has the number field Rok after the title (required, 2005–2100, this year for a new project; messages "Vyplňte prosím rok příběhu.", "Rok nemůže být před založením fondu, tedy před 2005.", "Zadejte prosím rok nejvýše 2100."), the tag hint names a project area ("Kompenzační pomůcky"), the perex hint the project card, the address hint `nfnn.cz/projekty/<slug>`, and the date hint says the projects on the same place of the order come from the one published last; the preview shows the public project card (with the year) and project page under "Karta na úvodní stránce a v Pomáháme" and "Stránka příběhu". Texts under `admin.projects.*` and `admin.projectEditor.*`. Specs `admin-projects.spec.ts`, `admin-project-editor.spec.ts`, `features/content/projects-api.spec.ts` (fixtures in `features/content/testing/project-fixtures.ts`); Playwright `e2e/admin-projects.spec.ts` (the same five journeys with the year in the table and the requests, and choosing the order by category, with the description of the choice and the list asked again; `project-order-card.spec.ts` for the card) and the axe case with a year before 2005 among the errors.
- `settings/` (`/sprava/nastaveni`, "Nastavení"): `h1` focused on open, an intro (what the page holds, the technical values at the bottom, that secrets are stored encrypted and never shown, that saving restarts the site). The page is ordered by how often a part is needed (Jakub 2026-10-24), not by the backend's groups: the cards "Potvrzení o daru" (`RECEIPT_FIRST_YEAR`), "Kdo potvrzení podepisuje" (below), "Kontaktní formulář" (`CONTACT_RECIPIENT`), "Odesílatel e-mailů" (`MAIL_FROM`) and "Přihlášení" (`SESSION_REMEMBER_DAYS`, plan v3 2.10, `nastaveni-prihlaseni`), each with a one-line intro, then the card "Technické nastavení (pro správce serveru)" with the warning callout "Chybná hodnota tady může zastavit odesílání e-mailů nebo stahování plateb z banky. Než změny e-mailů uložíte, pošlete si zkušební e-mail." and a `<details id="technicke-nastaveni">` (summary "Adresa webu, server pro odesílání e-mailů a napojení na banku", folded unless a secret is unreadable, a refused value is technical or the address points into it) holding the parts "Adresa webu" (`PUBLIC_URL`), "Server pro odesílání e-mailů" (`MAIL_HOST` … `MAIL_STARTTLS`) and "Banka" (`FIO_*`, `PAIRING_EXCLUDED_ACCOUNTS`) as `h3`. The sections are `EVERYDAY` and `TECHNICAL` in `admin-settings.ts`; every field keeps the labels of `setting-labels.ts`, the fields render from one `ng-template`, and the changes are sent in the backend's order. Headings take `tabindex="-1"` with Czech ids `nastaveni-<part>` (`nastaveni-potvrzeni`, `-kontaktni-formular`, `-odesilatel`, `-technicke`, `-adresa-webu`, `-odesilani-emailu`, `-banka`); `FRAGMENT_ALIASES` keeps the older addresses, so `/sprava/nastaveni#settings-MAIL` (the link from the report page) opens the technical settings and focuses "Server pro odesílání e-mailů". Fields: `MAIL_AUTH` and `MAIL_STARTTLS` as checkboxes, `FIO_HISTORY_FROM` as a date, a secret as a `role="group"` labelled by its state line "<label>: Nastaveno / Nenastaveno" (amber "Uložená hodnota nejde přečíst, zadejte ji prosím znovu." when `unreadable`) with the field named after the secret ("Nové heslo nebo klíč SMTP", "Nový token Fio API", else "Nová hodnota: {label}") in `<app-password-reveal>` and, when a row is stored, the checkbox "Odebrat uloženou hodnotu", which once ticked shows what the removal stops (`REMOVAL_EFFECTS`: no e-mail at all without the SMTP password, no bank import without the Fio token); every other setting a text field with its value. The mail server part ends with the hint "Pošle e-mail na vaši adresu {own e-mail} s údaji z formuláře, i když ještě nejsou uložené, …" and "Poslat zkušební e-mail na moji adresu" ("Posíláme zkušební e-mail…"): the form's server, port, user, sign-in, STARTTLS and sender (a blank password means the stored one, which the backend uses only for the running server), the result from `testEmailNotice` beside it. The card "Kdo potvrzení podepisuje" (`settings/receipt-signatory/`, `<app-receipt-signatory>`, slice 1.16, inside the form after "Potvrzení o daru" but with its own requests and no control of the form) reads `receiptSignatory()` and the people (`PeopleApi.load()`, for the portrait) and, when the signatory has a receipt signature, `receiptSignature(personId)`: "Načítáme, kdo potvrzení podepisuje…", a failed load with "Zkusit znovu", then the preview of the 3B signature block on the grey card of the token `--color-surface-muted` without a border as a `figure` named by its caption "Náhled podpisu na potvrzení" (the round portrait `?w=240`, or no portrait at all, as the receipt drops it, "Za Nová naděje - nadační fond:", the signature drawn by `<app-signature-artwork>` with the label "Podpis na potvrzení", at most 220 px wide, over a rule spanning the text column in the token `--color-border-rule` (`#b8bec6` as in 3B, about 1.7:1 on the card; decorative, not text), the name and the receipt role; `forced-color-adjust: none`, so a dark forced-colours mode keeps the paper and the ink) and a warning callout naming what is missing (portrait, signature or both) or, with `portraitPrintable` false, that the portrait cannot be prepared for print and receipts go out without it ("Portrét této osoby nejde připravit pro tisk…"), together with the missing signature in one sentence when that is missing too; with nobody chosen a static danger callout (no live role, since it shows on every visit) "Potvrzení o daru teď nikdo nepodepisuje, a tak je web dárcům nevystaví. …"; always the link "Otevřít lidi fondu" to `/sprava/lide-fondu`, where the signatory, the receipt role and the signature are changed. Texts under `admin.signatory.*`, spec `receipt-signatory.spec.ts`. After it the card "Klíč k ověřovacím QR kódům" (`settings/receipt-signing-key/`, `<app-receipt-signing-key>`, its `h2` `#klic-k-overovacim-qr` with `tabindex="-1"`, the fragment the overview's red row opens): an intro in the present tense since receipts are signed (1.6: "Server podepisuje ověřovací QR kódy na potvrzeních o daru svým klíčem.", worded so it still reads true while that key is withdrawn; the site publishes its public part so that receipts can be verified, if it may have leaked, withdraw it and the server makes a new one), "Načítáme klíč…", then a `dl` of "Klíč serveru" (the key id), "Zaregistrovaný" (`formatBankTime`) and "Stav" ("Zneplatněný", "Zveřejněný" or "Nezveřejněný"), the line "Zveřejněné jsou i dřívější klíče: <id> (zaregistrovaný <time>)." for the published keys of earlier servers (", zneplatněný" inside the brackets for a withdrawn one) (when the server's own row is missing, the `dl` shows its id and "Nezveřejněný" only), and in one `role="alert"` region a red callout per warning: the overview's sentence for every key whose seal fails, "Klíč serveru <id> je zneplatněný a server zatím nemá nový, …" while the server's own key is withdrawn and no restart is under way, and one sentence per audit mismatch (`settings/receipt-signing-key/key-warnings.ts`: "Klíč <id> byl ve správě zneplatněný, ale jeho záznam v databázi je znovu aktivní …" for `reverted`, "Klíč <id> je v historii správy, ale jeho záznam v databázi chybí …" for `missing`). Below, a small danger-outline button per key that can be withdrawn (published and `active`, or `revoked` only by the trail while its row is `reverted`): "Zneplatnit klíč serveru…" first, then "Zneplatnit klíč <id>…" for earlier keys, "Zneplatnit klíč <id> znovu…" for a reverted one. It asks `AuthStore.hasPasskeys()` first (the button stays focusable, `aria-disabled` meanwhile, so the dialog returns focus to it) and opens `<app-key-withdrawal-dialog>` (`key-withdrawal-dialog.ts`, on `<app-dialog>`, labelled "Zneplatnit klíč <id>?" with the id in `ui-mono`): a lead (withdraw only when it may have leaked; it cannot be undone), points (the site keeps publishing it as withdrawn, so its QR codes are not to be trusted, earlier receipts included and not re-signed; for the server's key the site restarts at once and makes a new key, about a minute without receipts; for an earlier key nothing restarts; what to answer donors who write about a receipt under that key (the verification page no longer verifies it and the web cannot issue a new one yet, so the administrator confirms its data by its number; slice 1.21); it is recorded in the history and e-mailed to every administrator), then the fresh second factor exactly as the backup key card asks it (the code field with `autofocus`, "Nebo bez kódu: Potvrdit přístupovým klíčem" only when the administrator has a passkey, or the passkey first with "Zadat kód z aplikace"), the refusal in a `role="alert"` line, "Zneplatnit klíč" (`ui-button--danger`, "Zneplatňujeme klíč…" while sending) and "Zrušit" (`autofocus` when no code field is shown). The answer replaces the card's keys; the card focuses its `h2` and an `<app-live-notice>` says "Klíč <id> je zneplatněný." or, with `restarting`, "Klíč <id> je zneplatněný. Web se restartuje a vytvoří si nový klíč, obvykle do minuty." while `watchKeyRenewal` (`settings/receipt-signing-key/key-renewal.ts`) asks for the keys every 3 s (`RESTART_POLL_MS`), at most 100 times (`KEY_RENEWAL_MAX_POLLS`, five minutes, room for a slow mail run before the backend restarts, review 1.13 r2). It decides only once the server went down and answered again, or the answer names another `currentKeyId`; an answer that still names the withdrawn key from a server that never went down (the notices are still being sent) claims nothing and keeps waiting. Only an unreachable web counts as down (status 0, 502, 503 or 504, what the frontend nginx answers while the backend restarts, review 1.13 r3); any other failure (a 500, a 429, a 401 the refresh could not mend, a 403) proves no restart and does not end the wait, so a single blip before the restart can no longer turn the withdrawn key that follows into the "no new key" alert. Then "Klíč <id> je zneplatněný a web po restartu podepisuje novým klíčem." when the new key is published (`currentKeyPublished`) and not withdrawn; the alert "Klíč <id> je zneplatněný a web po restartu má nový klíč <new-id>, který ale zatím není zveřejněný." when it is not published; and when the web came back with the same key the alert "Klíč <id> je zneplatněný, ale web po restartu nový klíč nemá, takže se potvrzení o daru nestáhnou; …" (the server signs nothing while its key is withdrawn), which replaces the card's own warning about it. When the polls run out, the restarting line goes and only an alert stays, with "Zkusit znovu", which watches again: "Klíč je zneplatněný, ale web se po restartu zatím nespustil. …" when the web went down and has not answered since, and "Klíč <id> je zneplatněný, ale web se zatím nerestartoval a nový klíč nemá, takže se potvrzení o daru nestáhnou. Počkejte pár minut a zkuste to znovu; když se nic nezmění, obraťte se prosím na technika serveru." (`admin.signingKey.notRestarted`) when it never went down in the whole wait (the request for a new seed could not be written, or the notices never finished), so the card never claims a restart that did not happen. a failed load says "Klíč se nepodařilo načíst." with "Zkusit znovu". Texts under `admin.signingKey.*`, spec `receipt-signing-key.spec.ts`. Without a settings key a `role="alert"` box says in plain words that secrets cannot be saved and the server administrator helps with the key (the missing `secrets/settings-key` under "Technické podrobnosti"), and their fields are disabled; with a key, unreadable secrets are named in a `role="alert"` box at the top ("Tyto tajné údaje jsou uložené jiným klíčem, než má tento server, třeba po obnově ze zálohy na novém serveru: {labels}. Dokud je znovu nezadáte a neuložíte, nebudou fungovat."), which is how a restore of an older backup on a new server asks for the SMTP password and the Fio token again (Jakub 2026-10-23; since 2026-10-29 a backup carries them under the backup key). "Uložit nastavení" computes the changes (trimmed; a secret only when typed or removed); nothing changed says "Nic jste nezměnili." right under the button (`.ui-form-status`); otherwise the `role="group"` "Uložit nastavení?" beside the button lists "Mění se: {labels}." (a removed secret as "{label} (odebrat)"), a danger callout per removed secret with its effect, a warning when the mail server settings or the sender changed without a sent test e-mail for exactly those values ("Zkušební e-mail jste s novými údaji neposlali. …"), and asks "Uložením se web restartuje a asi minutu nebude odpovídat. Uložit?" with "Uložit a restartovat" ("Ukládáme…") and "Zrušit" (focused; cancelling returns the focus to "Uložit nastavení"). A 400 lists the refused values by label under the form; success shows the restart line with a spinner while `watchRestart()` runs, reloads the settings and says "Nastavení je uložené a web po restartu běží s ním.", or after 100 polls an alert saying the site has not come back yet, to wait and then ask the server administrator (the container log under "Technické podrobnosti"), with "Zkusit znovu". Loading, a failed load, 401 and 403 behave as on the other admin pages. Texts under `admin.settings.*`.
