Specs `admin-settings.spec.ts`; Playwright `e2e/admin-settings.spec.ts` (a value and a secret changed, only the changes sent, the restart waited for; the order of the parts and a test e-mail with a new sender before saving; the report page's link opening the mail server part; the signatory: the preview of the signature block, the warning when nobody signs, the block in a dark forced-colours mode) and the axe case for the page with its confirmation and a refused test e-mail.

- `changed-meanwhile/` (plan v3 2.18, contract "Stale writes"): `ChangedMeanwhileDialog`, an `<app-dialog>` sheet "Mezitím se to změnilo" shown when a versioned write answers 409 `…/changed-meanwhile` (`isChangedMeanwhile`, `versionInRefusal`, `savedAtInRefusal` and `ifMatch` in `core/version/row-version.ts`; every versioned call of `admin-api.ts`, `news-api.ts` and `projects-api.ts` takes the `version` of the record it was read with and sends it as `If-Match`). It has three forms. On a page of actions (payments, older-symbol answers, the donor card's bank accounts), and in an editor whose refused action had no unsaved edits, it says the change was not made and offers "Načíst aktuální stav", which reads the page again (closing it does the same); its lead names what changed (`subject`: a payment, a donor's account link, an older-symbol answer, an article or project). In a form whose save was refused (the monthly report settings, the news and project editors; `keepsDraft` with `canOverwrite`) it says the edits stayed in the form and offers "Uložit svou verzi místo novější", which sends the form again with the version from the refusal (a refused publish keeps its intent and publishes after saving), and "Zahodit své úpravy a načíst novější verzi", which reads the record again; no button has the focus at opening, so Enter only closes the sheet, and closing it keeps the form as typed. When a publish, withdrawal or deletion is refused while the editor holds unsaved edits (`keepsDraft` without `canOverwrite`), it says the change was not made and the edits stay, tells how to go on (save them first or discard them), and offers "Nechat úpravy ve formuláři" and the discard button; after a refused deletion focus returns to "Smazat aktualitu" or "Smazat příběh". Where the refusal's record carries it, the sheet says when the newer version was saved. Its buttons wrap and stack below the phone breakpoint (the device steps open it in every pose, `admin-changed-meanwhile`), and its lead is tied to the dialog by `describedBy` (an input of `<app-dialog>` bound to `aria-describedby`). After a reload each page announces it in its status line once the data has arrived and moves focus there (the editors and Přehledy to their heading); the report form is pristine again (`DirtyForms`), and a repeated refusal while saving over is announced through `DialogAnnouncer`.

## Theme, fonts, assets

- The theme is a folder chosen at build time: `src/themes/nova-nadeje/_theme.scss` declares the `@font-face` rules and every token on `:root` (inventory in "Frontend conventions in detail", UI, below), `styles.scss` loads it as `@use 'theme'` through `stylePreprocessorOptions.includePaths` (`src/styles` and the theme folder), and the configuration `control-theme` swaps in `src/themes/control/` (a deliberately different look with system fonts, built and checked by `e2e/theme.spec.ts` in CI only; `scripts/check-themes.mjs`, part of `npm run lint:styles`, keeps the token sets equal). The files of a theme's look live in its `assets/` folder and are served at the site root (each build configuration maps its theme's `assets/` with `output: "/"`, plan v3 2.22): `favicon.svg`, `favicon.ico`, `apple-touch-icon.png`, `app-icon.svg` and `images/logo.svg` and `images/logo-on-dark.svg`, at the addresses they had in `public/`; the control theme carries copies of the same files for now. `check-themes.mjs` fails when a theme lacks a file of `nova-nadeje` or has one more, when a theme file is also in `public/` (the build would serve one of them unseen), and when a build serves no `assets/` of its own theme or another theme's. The receipt PDF still draws the logo from `receipt-logo.ts` (`receipt-logo.spec.ts` compares it with the `nova-nadeje` logo), so a theme with another logo also changes that file. The theme also carries the tokens of the app layout (2.22, used from 2.23): the floating tab bar of decision 2.1 (Jakub 2026-10-31 08:29 and 13:43: `--color-tab-bar` navy glass at 66 % with `--blur-bar` 22 px and `--shadow-tab-bar` `0 8px 24px` at 25 % as the variant C mockup draws them (its `saturate(160%)` and 1.5 px rim are left to 2.23, which draws the bar) and the rim `--color-tab-bar-rim` white at 35 %, the active pill `--color-tab-pill` navy at 88 % with `--color-tab-active` `#93c5fd`, labels `--color-on-tab-bar`, focus ring `--color-tab-focus-ring` white, `--tab-bar-height` 62 px as a minimum that grows with the text, `--tab-bar-gap` 20 px to the sides and also the installed app's bottom offset (`max(var(--tab-bar-gap), env(safe-area-inset-bottom) - 13px)`, iOS expert 2026-10-31, so 2.23 adds no token for it), `--tab-bar-gap-browser` 16 px above Safari's row, `--tab-pill-inset` 2 px (5 px until the pill was drawn without a rim, Jakub 2026-11-06, so it fills the bar as in a native iOS tab bar), `--color-tab-pill-rim` transparent (the control theme keeps its bar's rim on the pill), `--shadow-tab-bar`; solid under `prefers-reduced-transparency`: the bar the glass over white, `#616672` (white labels 5.75:1), the pill navy `#0f172a` (3.1:1 against the bar), the control theme its glass over white `#5c6662` with a pill darkened to `#0b110e`, because its plain bar colour gave 2.99:1; even the decided glass leaves its pill under 3:1 against the bar, so 2.23 marks the active tab by a shape or weight too, not by its colour alone), the sheet (`--sheet-max-width`, `--radius-sheet`, `--shadow-sheet`, `--duration-sheet`, `--color-sheet-grabber`, `--sheet-grabber-width`/`-height`), the list rows (`--list-row-min-height`, `--list-group-title-top`) and the dialog values that were raw in `dialog.scss` (`--dialog-max-width`, `-wide`, `--dialog-close-inset`, `--dialog-close-size`, `--blur-backdrop`). There are no top bar tokens: Jakub chose variant E on 2026-11-05 07:43 (no fixed bar at the top; the logo only in the corner of the landing photo and the sign-in button or avatar at the top right, both scrolling away), and what the app shell still needs there (the back button one level deeper) waits for its mockups in 2.23. `check-themes.mjs` requires the tab bar colours as literal `#rrggbb` (the glass colours also as `color-mix(in srgb, #rrggbb N%, transparent)`) and fails when, over a white page (the worst case for light labels), `--color-on-tab-bar` on the bar or `--color-tab-active` on the pill over the bar falls below 4.5:1, or the focus ring on the bar below 3:1, in the top-level `:root` and in every media query that sets one of them. Fonts: Playfair Display and Inter as self-hosted variable WOFF2 (latin and latin-ext from Fontsource, OFL licences beside them in `src/themes/nova-nadeje/fonts/`); the build hashes them into `/media/`, which nginx caches as immutable. Type roles (design plan slice 2, Jakub 2026-11-06): `--font-sans` puts the system face first (`-apple-system, BlinkMacSystemFont, 'Inter'`, so SF Pro on Apple devices and Inter elsewhere; deliberately not `system-ui`, which would give Segoe or Roboto), `--font-display` (the serif) carries the hero, each page's `h1`, public article and section titles and the O nás numbers, `--font-title` (the sans, set semibold) the card, dialog and panel titles and the titles of the cards and dialogs of `/darce` and `/sprava`, `--font-figures` every amount, account number, IBAN, variable symbol and receipt number through the block `.ui-figures` (`_figures`: `--numeric-figures` plus `--figure-features` `'tnum' 1`, never wrapping inside a number; `.ui-figures--groups` wraps only between its child spans: the IBAN's groups of four in O nás, its halves in Přispět, a list of symbols), and `--font-mono` (`ui-monospace, 'SF Mono', Menlo`) only logs, key ids, codes and file names. Labels: `.ui-eyebrow` only on public marketing sections; labels in cards and sheets sentence case at `--text-md` in `--color-text-soft`; table headers sentence case at `--text-sm` semibold in `--color-text-secondary`; the side panel's group titles sentence case; the inset-list section headers of `/sprava/vice` small uppercase (`--text-xs`, `--tracking-slight`). Long-form text (`shared/ui/markdown`) is `--color-text-secondary`. Type sizes, weights, line heights, letter spacing, the spacing scale (`--space-unit` × n) and transition durations are tokens there too; `src/styles/_breakpoints.scss` holds the named widths and the `bp.down`/`bp.up` mixins (on the include path, so components write `@use 'breakpoints' as bp;`), and the named heights with `bp.height-down` (`short` 500 px, a phone on its side, and `hero-lead` 680 px, an iPhone SE upright, where the landing hero gives way); `core/layout/breakpoints.ts` (`BREAKPOINTS`, `downQuery(name)`, `HEIGHTS`, `heightDownQuery(name)`) holds the same widths and heights for code, kept equal by `scripts/breakpoints-parity.test.mjs` (run by `npm run lint`), and `core/layout/soft-keyboard.ts` (`SoftKeyboard`, root, started by the first component that asks: the tab bar, a dialog or a form field) counts the on-screen keyboard open while a text field has the focus and `visualViewport` is at least 150 px shorter than the window (`isOpen()`, `visibleArea()`), and then marks the root `keyboard-open` with `--keyboard-covered-height` (iOS Safari ignores `interactive-widget`, measured on iOS 27.2); `core/layout/compact-layout.ts` (`CompactLayout.isCompact()`, root) follows `matchMedia(downQuery('nav'))`, the width of `bp.down(nav)`, false where the browser has no `matchMedia`, and keeps its answer while the window is 1 px wide or less (Chromium reports a 1 × 1 viewport for a moment while it captures a page beyond its viewport, and under load the screen spec's full-page shots caught the phone layout it set for that frame), so a page renders the compact list or the table with `@if`, never both hidden by CSS (2.22; used from 2.25). `core/layout/short-viewport.ts` (`ShortViewport.isShort()`, root) follows `matchMedia(heightDownQuery('short'))` the same way, for what a phone on its side cannot fit (the landing hero's actions). `src/styles/ui/` holds the shared building blocks (global `ui-` classes for page head, card, notice and load state, badge, button, table, form errors and segmented choices, the title, lead and points of a confirmation dialog (`_dialog`: the restore, unsaved changes and key withdrawal dialogs), the list of people with a round portrait, name, a line under it, badges and actions (`_person-list`: the people of the foundation and the administrators), the list rows of the app layout (`_list`, 2.22, unused until 2.25, which also settles the rest of the block: a group is `section > h3.ui-list__group-title + ul.ui-list` (a title inside an `li` does not stick), a second action sits beside its row as `__item` with `__action`, rows take grid areas so a row without a lead or value keeps no empty gap, the value moves under the title at large text, and rows get `scroll-margin-top` so focus is not hidden under a sticky title), the standalone panel pages, and the public site's section width, eyebrow, page intro band and calls to action, list in "Frontend conventions in detail", UI, below), used by every page of the administration, the standalone pages (password reset, e-mail verification, connected application, first setup), the sign-in dialog, the cards of `/darce` and the public pages (home sections, news and project detail, privacy policy, not found, footer); their SCSS is linted with the components. `npm run lint:styles` fails on a raw colour, `font-family`, font size, weight, line height, letter spacing, `border-radius`, shadow, `px` spacing (except 1 px), transition duration or `width` media query outside the theme.
- Light and dark appearance (#347; decided 2026-11-06 under Jakub's instruction of that day that the app behave like a native iOS app): the site follows the device's Light / Dark setting. `index.html` declares `<meta name="color-scheme" content="light dark">`, so the browser paints its canvas dark before the styles load, and each theme ends with a block `@media (prefers-color-scheme: dark) { :root { color-scheme: dark; … } }` that only overrides colour tokens (and `--filter-ink`, `--blend-ink`), so the light look is the top-level `:root` unchanged and native controls (date fields, selects, checkboxes, scroll bars) follow through `color-scheme`. `nova-nadeje` dark, as an iOS designer and an accessibility expert agreed: the page and the cells `#121826` (a navy-tinted near-black, not pure black: less halation for astigmatic readers and the brand's navy stays visible; `#10141f` until the design slice s3), `--color-surface-alt` `#080b12` for quiet grounds inside them, the navy bands `#13213f` and the footer `#05070c`, text `#e6e8ee` (not white, less glare) to soft `#9aa3b2`, links and focus ring `#6ea8ff`, softened danger, warning and success colours, a darker backdrop, and its own block for `prefers-contrast: more` in the dark (placed after the light one, which would otherwise win). A colour used both as text and as a fill splits in two: `--color-accent` (text, links, borders) becomes the light blue, while `--color-accent-fill` (the filled buttons, the selected amount, the stats band, badges, and the glow shadows) stays `#2563eb` with white text (5.17:1), since no single blue reads both on the dark page and under white text; `--color-danger-fill` likewise for danger buttons, and `--color-surface-quote` for the quote bubble that used `--color-text` as its ground. Each new token equals the old value in the light look. What must stay light stays light: the QR tokens (dark modules on white, so banks scan them) and the logo tokens that paint its star, every `--receipt-*` token (the PDF never depends on the device), the manifest colours (checked against the top-level `:root` only) and the receipt-signature previews of `/sprava` on `--color-paper` and `--color-paper-muted`, which show the ink as it prints. Photos are not dimmed; the header and the sign-in dialog show `logo-on-dark.svg` through a `<picture>` source with `media="(prefers-color-scheme: dark)"` (the header only where its page head is light; a dark head always takes it); every web signature takes the block `.ui-ink` (`src/styles/ui/_ink.scss`: `filter: var(--filter-ink)` and `mix-blend-mode: var(--blend-ink)`), under the founders' note in O nás and in the administration's Lidé fondu (the row's "Podpis na webu" and the editor's preview), because the server flattens every upload onto white (`invert(1) hue-rotate(180deg)` and `screen` in the dark, so blue ink turns light blue and a white upload background disappears; both off under `inverted-colors: inverted`, where Smart Invert turns the page light again). The status bar needs nothing: the site sets no `theme-color` (above), and the untinted bar follows the page. The static launch screen follows `--color-surface`, so it is dark in the dark, because it shows on every load in a browser tab too; the `apple-touch-startup-image` PNGs of an app opened from the Home Screen stay light (drawn from the manifest's `background_color`), so a cold start of the installed app in the dark still begins white for a moment: dark startup images wait until a device shows whether iOS honours `prefers-color-scheme` in their `media`. `scripts/check-themes.mjs` (`appearanceProblems`) fails a theme without a dark block and, in the light and the dark appearance, each with and without `prefers-contrast: more` (blocks applied in source order as the cascade does), any text token below 4.5:1 on the grounds it is used on (text, secondary, muted and accent on the eight light-family grounds, the grouped ground and the elevated surface among them; soft, subtle and danger on the page, the quiet ground, the muted ground, the grouped ground and the elevated surface; the accent on its own 14 % tint; white on the fills, the text of the filled warning badge on it, the glyph of each icon tile at 3:1 on it, the navy bands and the quote; each status colour on its own ground), the focus ring below 3:1 on the page, the quiet ground, the grouped ground and the navy band, and the field border below 3:1 on the page and grouped ground everywhere but the light default (Jakub kept that border soft); `var()` references are followed and a translucent mix is laid over the page first. `e2e/dark-appearance.spec.ts` checks that a light device still gets the white page and dark logo, that a dark one gets the dark page, light text, the light logo and `color-scheme: dark`, that the payment QR keeps dark modules on a light ground, and runs axe in the dark on O nás, Přispět (also on a phone with the QR unfolded), the privacy policy, the news and project lists and pages, the sign-in dialog and the donor's overview; `e2e/theme.spec.ts` opens the public pages and the sign-in dialog of the control theme in the dark too. The `visual` job shoots the light look only.
- Surfaces, dark levels and accent (design slice s3 of the designers' joint plan, sections 2.1, 2.5 and 2.6; Jakub's decision J6, 2026-11-06): the app areas `/darce` and `/sprava` stand on `--color-surface-grouped` (`#f2f3f7`, dark `#05070c`): `DonorShell` carries the block class `ui-grouped`, its pages paint that ground, and `html:has(.ui-grouped) body` takes it too, so Safari's overscroll and the strip under its toolbar match. Inside `.ui-grouped` a `.ui-card` and a `.ui-list--inset` are flat cells (`--radius-group` 22 px, `--shadow-group` `none`, no border, in the dark too; a danger card keeps its red border, and a card or inset list inside a dialog keeps the public look); inside `.ui-grouped` the quiet ground `--color-surface-alt` points one level up to `--color-surface-muted` (`#f1f3f5`, dark `#1b2130`), so the boxes, table heads and totals inside a cell rise from it instead of sinking below it in the dark; elsewhere a card is a public card with `--shadow-card` (`0 1px 2px` at 4 % and `0 4px 16px` at 5 % of the navy) and a `--color-border` hairline. Three levels in the dark: the grouped ground `#05070c`, cells and pages `--color-surface` `#121826`, and `--color-surface-elevated` `#1a2232` for dialogs and sheets (`#ffffff` in the light). Hairlines between rows of a card's bar and footer, table rows and the list of people are `--color-separator` (navy at 10 %, white at 8 % in the dark); the rows of `.ui-list` keep `--color-divider` until the rows slice. `--color-fill` (the iOS tertiary fill), `--color-badge-warning` with `--color-on-badge-warning` and the icon tiles `--color-tile-accent`, `-success`, `-warning`, `-neutral` with `--color-on-tile` are tokens for the later control and row slices. `--color-media-rim` frames the photos of Přispět and O nás with a 1 px inner outline, white at 10 % in the dark and transparent in the light. The light `--color-accent` is `#1d5fc9` (logo-family blue, 5.94:1 on white, 4.84:1 on its 14 % tint) for text, links, eyebrows, outlines and tints, with `--color-accent-soft` `#edf2fb`, `--color-accent-bg` and `--color-surface-accent` `#f1f5fc` and `--color-border-accent` `#c0d2f0` mixed from it, while `--color-accent-fill` stays `#2563eb` for filled buttons and selected states (white text 5.17:1; the plan's dark `#3b82f6` would give white text only 3.68:1, so the fill stays `#2563eb` in the dark too) and the dark accent stays `#6ea8ff`; the manifest's `theme_color` follows the accent. The e-mail templates take the same values (`EmailTemplateColoursTest`): links `#1d5fc9`, filled buttons `#2563eb`, the tinted note `#edf2fb` with `#c0d2f0`, grey text `#676f7d`. The light soft and subtle text darkens to `#676f7d`, the lightest grey that keeps 4.5:1 on the grouped ground. The control theme mirrors every token in its own way (square groups with a 1 px rim, a warm grey ground, a teal accent `#0e7068` for text beside its fill `#0f766e`).
- `public/images/`: hero in 1024/1536 px with `srcset` (the source photo is 1536 px wide, so wider screens scale it up), `sizes="max(100vw, 150vh, 900px)"` and `fetchpriority="high"`; about and donate in 720/1440 px; project and news cards at 600 px; every photo below the hero has `loading="lazy"` written before `src`/`srcset` (Angular sets attributes in template order) plus `width`/`height`, so photos hidden on phones are never downloaded there. Convert a new photo with Pillow or `cwebp` at quality about 78; no file is kept that no template names.
- Icons: `favicon.svg` is the white star of the logo (its traced outline, PR #228) in a flat circle of the logo blue `#005dab` (viewBox 1024 wide, Jakub 2026-10-30); the face (`g.face`) stays at every size, also at 16 px (Jakub 2026-10-30; `e2e/home-assets.spec.ts`). `favicon.ico` holds 16, 32 and 48 px renders of it. The app icon is Jakub's white tile with the blue star outline of the logo (2026-10-30, the PWA icon of `nfnn-podklady/logo/ikona-pwa/` in project files): `app-icon.svg` is the tile with rounded corners (`rx` 230 of 1024) for the ZimaOS app list (ZimaOS shows SVG, Jakub 2026-10-30, so there is no PNG of it); `apple-touch-icon.png` is 180 px on opaque RGB, the white square without rounded corners because iOS rounds them itself (iOS takes no SVG). The cloud ImageMagick has no SVG renderer: render with Playwright's Chromium (`omitBackground`) and pack with `convert 16.png 32.png 48.png favicon.ico`.
- Launch screen (#420; the white tile on the dark surface fixed 2026-11-06 at Jakub's report): each theme carries `assets/launch-icon.svg`, the star of `logo-on-dark.svg` (its white body and blue outline with the face) placed as in `app-icon.svg` but without the tile, so it stands on the light and on the dark surface alike. Before the app's code runs, `src/index.html` shows it inside `<app-root>` (`.launch-screen`, `src/styles/_launch-screen.scss`: the SVG at `--launch-icon-size` in the middle of `--color-surface`, which follows the device's appearance; `e2e/install-app.spec.ts`). The iPhone and iPad home-screen app first shows the `apple-touch-startup-image` iOS picks by media query, and iOS takes only a raster there, so the `postbuild` step `scripts/make-startup-images.mjs` draws one PNG per screen of `STARTUP_SCREENS`, orientation and appearance into `startup/` of the build: the theme's `launch-icon.svg`, drawn by `scripts/svg-raster.mjs` (filled paths, `matrix` transforms and the `nonzero` and `evenodd` rules only; anything else fails the build and `check-themes.mjs`, which also fails a launch icon that paints its corners) at `--launch-icon-size` times the screen's scale, on the manifest's `background_color` for `startup/<w>x<h>.png` and on the dark `--color-surface` of the built stylesheet for `startup/<w>x<h>-dark.png`, whose links add `and (prefers-color-scheme: dark)` and follow every light one. iOS chooses one when the app is added to the home screen (the iOS 27.2 simulator added in the dark appearance fetched only the dark image, 2026-11-06), so a later change of the appearance may keep the image of that moment until the app is added again; the launch screen of `index.html` follows the appearance at every start. No raster of the launch is kept in the repository.
- Web app manifest (plan v3 2.4): each theme carries `assets/manifest.webmanifest`, linked from `index.html` (`<link rel="manifest">`), with `id` and `start_url` `/aplikace`, `scope` `/`, `display` `standalone`, `lang` `cs`, the name "Nová naděje – nadační fond" and the short name "Nová naděje" under the icon (#386: at most the 12 characters an iPhone shows there, and equal to `apple-mobile-web-app-title` in `src/index.html`, which names the app in Add to Home Screen), `theme_color` the theme's `--color-accent` and `background_color` its `--color-surface`, and the icons `icon-192.png`, `icon-512.png` (`any`) and `icon-maskable-512.png` (`maskable`, the star inside the safe circle), Jakub's PWA icon of `nfnn-podklady/logo/ikona-pwa/` (the control theme carries copies, since every theme carries the same file names). `scripts/check-themes.mjs` checks each theme's manifest: those fields, both colours equal to the tokens of the top-level `:root`, and every icon a PNG in `assets/` whose real size is the one its `sizes` names, with a 192 and a 512 px `any` icon and a 512 px `maskable` one, and the short name of at most 12 characters equal to the `apple-mobile-web-app-title` of `src/index.html`.

## Tests

Specs beside the code; `src/test-setup.ts` gives jsdom `showModal()`/`close()`, `src/test-providers.ts` the `cs` locale, `FakeLiveUpdates` in place of `LiveUpdates` and no tab locks or session channel (`TAB_LOCKS` null, `SESSION_CHANNEL` a factory of null), so no spec opens a real `BroadcastChannel`; specs of the tabs override them with `FakeTabLocks` and `FakeSessionChannel` from `core/auth/testing/fake-session-tabs.ts`, and `e2e/session-tabs.spec.ts` (Chromium only, because WebKit's request interception shows no `Cookie` header) runs two tabs over one rotating refresh cookie and a refresh whose answer never arrives. Shared cases (`docs/system-overview.md`, Regression against v2 and shared cases, Shared cases): the Vitest global setup `src/testing/shared-cases-setup.ts` (`vitest.config.mts`) reads `../shared-cases` in Node and `src/testing/shared-cases.ts` hands specs frozen, schema-checked cases (`sharedCases(file)`); `donation-amount.spec.ts` runs the Czech receipt amounts through `formatMinorUnits`. `core/i18n/plural-category.ts`: `pluralCategory(count)` → `one` | `few` | `other` for text built in TypeScript. Every e2e spec imports `test` from `e2e/fixtures.ts`, whose automatic fixture answers an unmocked `/api` request with a 404 problem. `e2e/devices/` opens the main pages, dialogs and menus in the poses of `poses.ts` (iPhones upright, on their side and with large text, iPads, a Pixel), checks each layout from its boxes and keeps a screen of each in `test-results/device-report/` (`.claude/rules/testing-frontend.md`, Phones and tablets); what it finds today is listed in `known-findings.ts`. Playwright in `e2e/` by journey (`chunk-load-recovery.spec.ts`, through `LostChunks` in `lost-chunks.ts`, answers 404 for every chunk the first page did not load, until a given document request or for good: the deploy path where the automatic load shows the page, the page of its own when that load fails too, an address with a fragment, a throwing `sessionStorage` (no automatic load, the bar), `context.setOffline` (no automatic load), chunks whose requests are cut off while `navigator.onLine` stays true (`cutOffEveryChunkNotLoadedYet`: the probe fails, so no automatic load and the bar) and a dirty news editor left for a lost chunk (one question in the page, no `beforeunload` prompt, the list loads from the server); `accessibility.spec.ts` runs axe over the bar and the page of its own, `accessibility-keyboard.spec.ts` checks at 320, 390 and 1280 px that the bar hides neither the focused control nor the end of the page, and `theme.spec.ts` covers both states; `site-metadata.spec.ts` reads `robots.txt` and the sitemap and checks the canonical link, `og:url` and the preview image on a page): `donor-session.ts` holds the sample donor and `administrator` (the same donor with the `ADMIN` role), `signIn(page, user?)`, `session(status, user?)`, `openSignInDialog`, `donationItem(overrides)` and `bankAccountItem(overrides)` (the API's full item shapes) and `mockDonorApi` (payment details (from `shared-cases/payment/details.json`), summary and list, bank check, empty bank account list; with `receipts` `receipt-issuing.ts` answers `POST /api/donations/me/receipts` with an issued receipt made from a case of `shared-cases/receipts/v1/frozen.json`, and the synthetic signature and portrait by their digests, so the real worker draws the PDF), which every donor spec registers first so nothing escapes to the dev proxy; `qr-matrices.ts` reads the payment matrices (level H) the browser must draw from the shared cases `shared-cases/qr/payment.json` and the payment details the mocks serve from `payment/details.json`, by case name (found from the file's own folder, `__dirname`, since Playwright loads the specs as CommonJS, where `import.meta` is a syntax error), and `drawnModulesMatch(qr, matrix)` compares the drawn dots with one; `second-factor.spec.ts` decodes the drawn authenticator QR with jsQR back to `otpauthUri` (the backend sends no matrix since 1.4); the automatic fixture of `e2e/fixtures.ts` serves the details and fails any journey that asks for another `/api/donations/payment-*` path, and `donate-qr.spec.ts` checks the same dots for 500 and 1 000 Kč, one request in all, the redraw after typing and the saved PNG; `password-reset.spec.ts` covers the dialog's reset tab and the page; `email-verification.spec.ts` the confirmation page, and `donor-account.spec.ts` the registration's "check your e-mail" panel and the resend button; `donor-dashboard.spec.ts` also the password and e-mail change forms; `connect-app.spec.ts` the consent page (sign-in on the page, the consent for claude.ai with the fresh code and the return with the authorization code, a used code refused and a new one accepted, a plain donor declining, an incomplete request, `mockConnectApi` answers the client description and swallows `https://claude.ai/**`) and the connections in the account card (`claudeConnection`, "Odpojit"); `older-symbol.spec.ts` answers the older-symbol question both ways; `admin-administrators.spec.ts` the administrators page, `admin-donors.spec.ts` the donor search and card, `admin-symbol-claims.spec.ts` the older-symbol answers, `admin-actions.spec.ts` the history, `admin-news.spec.ts` the news, `admin-projects.spec.ts` the projects, `content-pages.spec.ts` the home page with published content, both detail pages and an unknown slug (`mockDonorApi` answers `/api/content/home` with empty lists, so every other journey sees the placeholders); `admin.spec.ts` covers the administration (the link from the overview, the import state and its outage, the retry, a remembered session, a donor without the role sent to the overview, a 403 after the role was taken away, a visitor sent to the sign-in dialog); `passkeys.spec.ts`, `second-factor.spec.ts`, `add-bank-account.spec.ts`, `contact-form.spec.ts` (also copying the IČO and the IBAN from "Údaje pro účetní") and `privacy-policy.spec.ts` cover those journeys, `device-copy.spec.ts` the device copy in Chromium and WebKit (`mockDonorSync` of `donor-session.ts` serves the manifest and chunks; the password wrap made by the Argon2id worker under the production headers, the raw database scanned for the donor's gifts, e-mail and password, the copy kept over a reload and a sign-out, "Načíst data znovu" and the deletion with the database gone), `receipt-pdf.spec.ts` the receipt drawn in the browser (through `receipt-donations.ts`: the progress line while the data is held and the saved PDF read back with `pdf-reader.ts`, a worker chunk gone after a deploy with the one reload that resumes the download, still gone after it, an emulated iPhone with `canShare` and the second tap, the focus kept on the pressed button through the drawing and the save, and the 120-gift year whose median warm drawing in the worker stays within 1 s at CPU 1×, with the cold and warm times and their ×4 values as annotations (`system-overview.md`, receipt PDF)), `verify-receipt.spec.ts` the verification page (through `receipt-verification.ts`, which reads the proofs from `shared-cases/verification/payload.json`, serves or keeps a key list, cuts the network after load with `context.setOffline` and takes WebCrypto's Ed25519 away: the signed result and the only requests that leave, the status answers that are not green, a yearly receipt, a changed amount, a tampered key list, a revoked key, a cut-off link, both signature paths, the kept keys offline, no keys offline and the retry, an unknown key offline, the server unreachable while online), and the other specs are named with their page above; downloads use `Promise.all([page.waitForEvent('download'), click])` and `download.suggestedFilename()`; `accessibility.spec.ts` runs axe on every page and dialog.

## Frontend conventions in detail

The full text of the frontend rules as they stood on 2026-11-06, with every list of files, tokens, blocks and checks, kept here when the rules were cut to their constraints (`.claude/rules/frontend.md`, `.claude/rules/testing-frontend.md`). A change updates the rule's constraint there and the description here.

### Angular 22 (standalone, zoneless, signals)
- Components: flat names `hero.ts/.html/.scss`, class `Hero` (no `Component` suffix), selector `app-*`, no `standalone: true` (default), no NgModules. Change detection is OnPush (Angular 22's default; the existing components still spell it out, a new one may omit it); never `ChangeDetectionStrategy.Eager`/`Default` (lint rejects it). So a template may read only signals, inputs and what its own events change: state that arrives otherwise (the forms API, a subscription) goes through a signal, as `appFormControl` turns the control's events into `showsError()` and `errorKey()`.
- State in `signal`/`computed`/`linkedSignal`; `input()`, `output()`, `model()` instead of decorators; `inject()` instead of constructor parameters; a parameterless constructor holds the start-up work (initial load, `effect`, `afterNextRender`, `DestroyRef.onDestroy`); `host: {}` metadata instead of `@HostListener`/`@HostBinding` (lint: `prefer-host-metadata-property`); no `any`; strict TS and templates stay on.
- Templates: `@if/@for/@switch/@defer`; `[class.x]`/`[style.x]` (no `ngClass`/`ngStyle`); import only the directives and pipes used (never `CommonModule`); logic in the class.
- Forms: template-driven `ngModel` inside `<app-form-field label hint [messages]>` with `appFormControl` (needs `id` + `ngModel`) and `form[appFocusFirstInvalid]`; the field shows its own first error after touch or submit (`aria-invalid`, `aria-describedby`), a field left by a pointer press shows it only when the press ends, so the message cannot move the button away from under the pointer, and a required field the visitor only passed through without typing says nothing until submit (experts' decision for #359: on iOS the tap ends before the focus moves, so the message jumped the form under the finger); every text field names its return key with `enterkeyhint` (`next` before another field, `go` or `send` on the last; a textarea keeps its new line), and with the on-screen keyboard open `next` moves to the next field unless the form is already valid; a form-level alert only for what no field owns (passwords differ, an API refusal). Signal Forms are stable in Angular 22 but not used here: switching means migrating `form-field`, `appFormControl` and every form at once, so only by decision.
- Local first (`backend.md`): compute and render here whatever the data already in the browser allows; the backend only serves it.
- HTTP and data: one `providedIn: 'root'` service per backend resource, `<name>-api.ts` in its feature folder (sign-in and account calls: `core/auth/auth-api.ts`), relative `/api/...` only, DTO interfaces mirroring `docs/api-contract.md`; a component calls it and `subscribe`s into signals, as every existing page does (no `httpResource`/`rxResource` piecemeal). The donor's gifts are the one read that may come from the device: Moje dary, Přehled and Potvrzení read them only through `DonorDonationsRepository` (`features/donations/device-copy/`, 2.15), which answers from the device copy while it agrees with its summary and from `donations-api.ts` otherwise, and reaches the copy only through `DonorCopyLink`; every write, and every other read, goes to the `-api` service. Every call passes `keptPublicAnswersInterceptor` first (`core/navigation/kept-public-answers.ts`: a GET of the public content, the home answer, the news and project lists and pages, a subset of the service worker's same-for-everyone list that its spec pins, is answered from memory when the tab already had it within 10 minutes, and asked again in the background; any write under `/api/admin/content/` forgets them all; a new kept path stays inside that list), then `authInterceptor` (Bearer header, problem+json Blob parsing, one refresh on `auth/expired-token`) and then `versionInterceptor` (`core/version/`: the app's `Nfnn-App` and `Nfnn-App-Api` on every `/api` call, the server's `Nfnn-Revision` and `Nfnn-Api` read from every `no-store` answer into `ServerVersion`, and while the server speaks another API generation every write but the session refresh, sign-out and receipt status answered locally with the problem `app/update-required`, which the server also answers to a write from another generation; a page that keeps its own failure text recognises the problem, as the contact form and the receipt download do); only the API service of a public endpoint may send its request without the token, with the context `WITHOUT_ACCESS_TOKEN` (`core/auth/auth-interceptor.ts`), and ESLint's `no-restricted-imports` in `eslint.config.js` allows that import only in the files listed there (today `payment-details-api.ts`, the receipt verification's `receipt-keys-api.ts` and `receipt-status-api.ts`, `setup-api.ts` for the setup status the restart wait polls, and `core/connectivity/connectivity-probe-api.ts`, whose probe asks the same status): a new public endpoint that should skip the token adds its service to that list; errors become one line through `describeProblem` (`core/auth/api-problem.ts`). The live stream (`core/live/live-api.ts`, `GET /api/live`) is an `HttpClient` call too, a text stream read through download progress, so it carries the token and refreshes like any call; a page listens through `LiveUpdates.changes(feeds)` and keeps working when no stream is open. Three calls bypass it with a raw `fetch`: `core/errors/error-reporter.ts`, whose browser error report goes with `keepalive` and `credentials: 'omit'`, so it never carries a token, survives the page closing and cannot itself fail through the interceptor into another report; `core/reload/chunk-probe.ts`, whose `HEAD` asks the server for a lost chunk (`cache: 'no-store'`, aborted after 4 s) and is no API call; and `receipt-pdf/receipt-fonts.ts` in the receipt PDF worker, which reads the theme's TrueType files from `/receipt-fonts/` (static files of the build, no API call; a worker has no `HttpClient`). Nothing else calls `fetch`. The HTTP backend is XMLHttpRequest (`withXhr()` in `appHttpClient`, `app.config.ts`, for the whole app): fetch, Angular 22's default, reports no upload progress and `reportUploadProgress: true` under it throws NG02824, while the first setup's backup upload shows a progress bar (`reportUploadProgress`; `reportProgress` is deprecated since 22.0).
- Own history (#431, Jakub 2026-11-06): `core/navigation/own-history.ts`, installed in `main.ts` before Angular, turns every `pushState` into a replace of the browser's one entry of the site and keeps the steps itself (`sessionStorage` `nfnn.app-history`), on every device, so the browser's Back and Safari's swipe leave the site and the site steps back only through Zpět, a dialog's ×, `history.back()` and the swipe of the home-screen app (`edge-swipe-back.ts`, on iPhone and iPad only there). Nothing else may add a browser entry: a full page load inside the site goes through `ExternalNavigation.loadFromServer` or `history.replaceState` + `location.reload()`, never `location.assign` or a plain `href` to a page of the site (content links go through `Markdown`'s router click); e2e steps back with `stepBack(page)` of `e2e/app-history.ts`, and `page.goBack()` is the browser leaving the site.
- Routing: lazy per feature (`<feature>.routes.ts`, `loadChildren`/`loadComponent`); only the routes marked `LOADED_AHEAD` (`core/navigation/tab-preloading.ts`: the public shell, the content routes and the four tab pages) are loaded ahead once the first page has opened, so a tab opens without waiting for its code, and every other page stays lazy (the chunk-load journeys rely on that), `title` via `$localize`, addresses and section ids in Czech without diacritics (`/darce/prehled`, `/sprava`, `/prispet`, `/o-nas#kontakt`; only `/api/...` stays English; a page that moves keeps its old address as a redirect, as `oldSectionRedirect` does for the former `/#…` sections), `/darce/**` behind `donorGuard`, `/sprava/**` behind `adminGuard`, public shell with header and footer, unknown paths show `not-found`; nginx answers the app's routes with 200 and every other path that is no file with the same `index.html` and 404 (the map `$app_page` in `frontend/nginx.conf`, kept equal to the routes by `app.routes.spec.ts`: a new route or a renamed one changes its line there in the same PR), and an unknown slug of a detail route with 200, where the page sets `noindex`.
- Formatting is Prettier's (`.prettierrc`: 100 columns, single quotes, Angular parser for templates): run `npm run format` before committing; CI fails on a file it would change.
- Lint is ESLint with angular-eslint (`eslint.config.js`: recommended TypeScript and template rules, template accessibility, `prefer-inject`, `prefer-signals`, `prefer-output-readonly`, `prefer-on-push-component-change-detection`, `prefer-control-flow`, `prefer-self-closing-tags`): run `npm run lint` before committing; CI fails on a finding. `eslint-confinement.js` is the frontend twin of `AdapterLibraryRulesTest`: its table `CONFINED_IMPORTS` names each library or module family with the scopes allowed to import it (`SCOPES`: `app`, `testSupport`, `routes` and the adapters; a narrower scope names the one it lies `within`, which then ignores its files, so every file is in exactly one scope whatever the order of the config objects), refuses it elsewhere statically, as a type or by `import()` of a string or template literal, refuses every `import()` of a computed name outside route files, and with `lazyOnly` allows a library in its adapter only through `import()` (types still pass). ESLint runs with `noInlineConfig` and `ng lint` fails on a warning (`maxWarnings: 0`): no `eslint-disable` directive, since it is a comment (`CLAUDE.md`, No comments); an exception is a scoped config object or a named helper in a file the rule ignores (`e2e/fixed-waits.ts`, `testing.md`, Frontend e2e). `npm run lint` also runs `scripts/eslint-confinement.test.mjs`, which lints an example of every entry in every scope and fails on a scope that matches no file; a new library or adapter adds its entry or scope and its examples there. Two scoped exceptions, both for the native `<dialog>`: `autofocus` is allowed in `*dialog*.html` because the dialog moves focus to it on open, and `shared/ui/dialog/dialog.html` closes on a backdrop click without key events because Esc closes it natively.
- Folders: `features/{content,donors,donations,contact,operations}` mirror backend contexts; `features/admin` holds every `/sprava` page whatever context it calls (a new page: `features/admin/<page>/`, calls in `admin-api.ts` unless the context already has a feature service for both sides, as content's `news-api.ts` and `projects-api.ts`, a route in `admin.routes.ts`, an entry in `ADMIN_SECTIONS`); singletons in `core/`; a behaviour-only piece used twice moves to `shared/ui/` (`form-field`, `dialog`, `copy-button`, `share-button` for the Web Share API with the copy button as its fallback, `password-reveal`, `qr-code`, `avatar` for a person's round portrait or initials, `zoom-viewer` for a photo or QR code opened large and zoomable (`app-zoomable`), `markdown` for content bodies, `side-nav` for the side panel of `/sprava` and `/darce`, `account-username` for the hidden user name of a form that asks for the current password).
- Trusted Types are enforced (`security.md`, Browser): no string reaches a markup, script or script-address sink (`innerHTML`, `insertAdjacentHTML`, `DOMParser`, `script.src`, `importScripts`, `eval`); text goes in through bindings or `textContent`, a worker starts only as `new Worker(new URL('./x.worker', import.meta.url), { type: 'module' })`, which the build turns into `/worker-<hash>.js` and the site's `default` policy (`core/security/trusted-types.ts`, installed in `main.ts`) admits, and a new library is checked for sinks under the e2e suite before it lands.
- No CDN, inline script or third-party resource (CSP rule in `security.md`): fonts, images and scripts come from `public/` or the build, and QR codes are inline SVG drawn by `shared/ui/qr-code` from the module matrix (the saved PNG goes through a `data:` image and a canvas), which the browser encodes itself with `qr-encoder.ts` (no library; the authenticator QR since 1.3, the payment QR from 1.4).
- Web as an app (plan v3 2.4, `docs/frontend-overview.md` `core/updates/`): Angular's `ngsw-worker.js`, registered only by `core/updates/angular-service-worker.ts` (`@angular/service-worker` is confined to it in `eslint-confinement.js`; the rest asks `AppUpdates` through `SERVICE_WORKER_UPDATES`). `ngsw-config.json` keeps the shell and, of the API, only answers that are the same for everyone (`security.md`, Browser): a new cached path joins `ServiceWorkerCachedAnswersTest`'s probes and its list of public handlers, which fail otherwise, and the anchored list of `core/updates/service-worker-bypass.ts`, without which the app sends it with `ngsw-bypass` (every other `/api` request does, because Angular matches a data group URL anywhere in the address). A new version takes over only at a safe moment (`AppUpdates`): before the first touch after the start, or at the next navigation, never while `DirtyForms.any()`; every form registers through `appFocusFirstInvalid` (its host directive `TrackedForm`), and a form without it carries `appTrackedForm`, so no half-typed text is lost to an update. A reload the app starts by itself goes through `ReloadGuard.claim` with its reason. The app's start address is `/aplikace` (`appEntryGuard`), and the manifest is a theme asset checked by `check-themes.mjs`. The built worker begins with the site's own payment route (`frontend/service-worker/payment-route.js`, prepended by the `postbuild` of `npm run build`, so build with `npm run build`, never `ng build` alone, for anything that ships or runs `e2e/pwa/`): it serves only a payment the page reserved by a message (`LocalPaymentRoute`), once, at `/platba.spayd?id=<id>` on the device, and any other code in the worker is a security change (`security.md`, Browser).
- Receipt PDF: drawn in the browser by `features/donations/receipt-pdf/` in a module worker (`receipt-pdf.worker.ts`, its own chunk); pdfkit only in `receipt-layout.ts`, and that file only in the worker and tests (`eslint-confinement.js`), so no other bundle carries it; the layout takes its colours, type sizes, leading and margins from `ReceiptStyle` and its fonts from the theme (it owns only the geometry of its blocks, as a component owns its structure), and every word from the frozen receipt or the captions passed in.
- Receipt verification (`/overit`, `features/donations/verification/`, `docs/frontend-overview.md`): the proof is read and checked in the browser, with WebCrypto `Ed25519` and, only where the browser lacks it, `@noble/curves` from `noble-ed25519.ts` through `import()` (a lazy chunk); the key list kept in `localStorage` is replaced whole by each trusted list, never merged; a code is read from the browser's `BarcodeDetector` or else jsQR, which only `qr-decoding.ts` imports and only the module worker `qr-decoder.worker.ts` loads (`eslint-confinement.js`), so it is in no page bundle; the camera (`Permissions-Policy camera=(self)`) starts only on the visitor's press and stops on a read, on stop, after two minutes and when the tab is hidden or the page is left; only the exact prefix `https://nfnn.cz/overit#` with digits is accepted, and nothing read is ever opened or navigated to.
- Device copies (`core/device/crypto/`, plan v3 2.13, `docs/security-overview.md` "Device copies"): only WebCrypto and the Argon2id worker; every key imported or generated is non-extractable, the root key lives only in an open `OpenCopy` and is zeroed by `close()`; every record and wrap is sealed with AES-GCM and the length-prefixed additional data of `framed`, so a new kind of record or wrap names its own label and fields there; hash-wasm only in `argon2.ts`, which only `argon2.worker.ts` loads (`eslint-confinement.js`), and the app derives a password key only through `WorkerPasswordKeyDerivation` with its parameters stored beside the wrap; no e-mail, name or user handle in the clear outside an open copy (the registry keeps tags); the core stores nothing itself (lint refuses it every browser store, `scripts/device-crypto-storage.test.mjs`); the PRF input is per device, never per copy, so one passkey ceremony opens every copy; a PRF output never leaves the device, so `PasskeyAuthenticator` keeps sending no `clientExtensionResults`. The store (`core/device/store/`, 2.14) is the only code that keeps a copy: idb only in `idb-device-storage.ts` (`eslint-confinement.js`), every change one transaction, the `DeviceVault` the only writer and reached by the session only through `SESSION_DEVICE` (`device-copies.ts` loads it lazily); a new kind of stored value goes through it, and its tests scan the raw storage for a canary (`testing/raw-scan.ts`).
- Browser storage names (IndexedDB databases, `localStorage` and `sessionStorage` keys, Web Lock and `BroadcastChannel` names) start with `nfnn.` (the older `nfnn-refresh` and `nfnn-session` stay as they are) and are never renamed, because a tab of the previous build and the data a device already holds use them.
- Sessions: access token in memory (`AuthStore`; after a refresh posted to the other tabs over the `BroadcastChannel` `nfnn-session`, never stored), refresh cookie set by the browser, `localStorage` flag `nfnn.donor-session` only says a session may exist (a "Cizí počítač" session keeps it in `sessionStorage` and `PublicSessionIdle` signs it out after an hour without input, plan v3 2.10); the installed app has its own lock, channel and flag (`core/auth/session-jar.ts`) and names its own refresh cookie with `Nfnn-Session: APP` on refresh and sign-out; nothing else adds auth headers.

### i18n
- Czech source: every visible string has `i18n="@@<area>.<page>.<key>"`, the first segment one of `home`, `content`, `donor`, `admin`, `operations`, `layout`, `ui` (follow the neighbouring component); attributes `i18n-alt`, `i18n-aria-label`, `i18n-placeholder`; TS text with `$localize\`:@@id:text\``. Ids are stable, never renamed. There is no translation file yet.
- The source locale is `cs` (`angular.json` `i18n.sourceLocale`, so the build carries the Czech locale data and `LOCALE_ID` is `cs`; specs get the same from `src/test-providers.ts`). Format through the cs-CZ helpers, one place per format, not Angular's locale pipes: `formatMinorUnits`/`formatCrowns` (`donation-amount.ts`), `formatDonationDate`/`formatBankTime` (`donation-date.ts`), `formatMonth`/`formatMonthRange` (`donation-month.ts`), `formatReportMonth` (`admin/report/report-month.ts`); ICU plurals use the Czech categories `one`, `few` and `other` (`=0` only for a sentence of its own); TypeScript picks a form with `pluralCategory(count)` (`core/i18n/plural-category.ts`, over `Intl.PluralRules('cs')`).
- Czech copy: no vocative or declension of names (show the name alone); sentence case; the donate action is "Přispět"; amounts from the API (hellers) go through `formatMinorUnits(minor)` (crowns typed by a visitor through `formatCrowns`), which gives `1 000` or `1 000,50`, followed by `&nbsp;Kč` in a template and `\u00a0Kč` in TS; dates `15. 3. 2026` and times with a date `15. 3. 2026 8:05`, without leading zeros except the minutes (ČSN 01 6910, Jakub 2026-11-06), the receipt PDF too; bank times in Prague time; backend titles and details are shown as they come (already localized).

### UI (the look)
- The look is a swappable theme, independent of behaviour (Jakub 2026-10-23, plan `/mnt/project-files/nfnn-ux/oddeleni-vzhledu.md`). The theme owns every value of the look; a component owns only its structure (which grid, flex or order, which widths of its own content, which token for which part). Replacing the theme must restyle the whole site without touching a template, a component's SCSS or any TypeScript.
- A theme is a folder `src/themes/<name>/` with `_theme.scss` (its `@font-face` rules and every token on `:root`, plus at most rules that restyle the `ui-` blocks), its fonts, `assets/` (the files of its look, served at the site root by its build configuration with `output: "/"`: the tab icons `favicon.svg` and `favicon.ico`, `apple-touch-icon.png`, `app-icon.svg`, `launch-icon.svg` (the star without a tile, from which the launch screen and the iOS launch images are drawn), `images/logo.svg` and `images/logo-on-dark.svg`; every theme carries the same file names and none of them is also in `public/`) and `receipt/` (the receipt PDF's TrueType fonts `body.<hash>.ttf`, `body-bold.<hash>.ttf` and `heading.<hash>.ttf` with their licence, made by `scripts/make-receipt-fonts.sh`, which also writes their names into the theme's `--receipt-font-*` tokens; `angular.json` copies them to `/receipt-fonts/`, the control theme's under its configuration; the `--receipt-color-*` tokens are literal `#rrggbb`); `styles.scss` loads it as `@use 'theme'` from the include path, so the build picks the theme: `nova-nadeje` by default, `control` with the configuration `control-theme` (`ng build --configuration=production,control-theme`, `ng serve --configuration=control-theme`). The control theme is deliberately different (teal, system fonts, square corners, 5 px spacing unit) and exists only to prove the swap: CI builds it and `E2E_THEME=control npx playwright test e2e/theme.spec.ts` opens every public page, the sign-in dialog, every `/darce` section and every `/sprava` page and fails on any colour or font of `nova-nadeje` that the control theme does not share. A new token goes into every theme in the same PR: `npm run lint:styles` runs `scripts/check-themes.mjs`, which fails when a theme misses a token the styles use or `nova-nadeje` sets, when a receipt colour is not a literal `#rrggbb` or its text or QR colour falls below 4.5:1 on white, and when a receipt font token names a file that is missing or whose content does not match the hash in its name, or a font sits in `receipt/` that no token names, or a `--receipt-*` token is set outside the theme's top-level `:root` block (a media query would make the receipt depend on the device), when a tab bar colour is not literal or is set anywhere but the top-level `:root` or a `@media` block of `:root`, its labels fall below 4.5:1 (the focus ring 3:1, against the bar and the pill) over a white or a black page, or a solid pill below 3:1 against a solid bar, and when the themes' `assets/` differ, shadow a file of `public/` or a build serves another theme's. A new page gets a line in `e2e/theme.spec.ts`.
- Tokens live only in the theme (`src/themes/nova-nadeje/_theme.scss`) on `:root`: colour by role (`--color-accent*` (`--color-accent` for text, links, outlines and tints, `--color-accent-fill` for filled buttons and selected states), `-text*`, `-surface*` (`-surface-grouped` the ground of the app areas, `-surface-elevated` dialogs and sheets), `-border*`, `-separator` for hairlines between rows, `-fill`, `-media-rim`, `-focus-ring`, `-danger*`, `-warning*`, `-success*`, `-badge-*`, `-tile-*`, `-on-*`), fonts (`--font-serif` Playfair, `--font-sans` the system face then Inter, the roles `--font-display`, `--font-title`, `--font-figures` and `--font-mono` for logs and key ids only, `--numeric-figures`, `--figure-features`), type size in `rem` (`--text-2xs` 11 px … `--text-6xl` 44 px at the default 16 px, and fluid roles `--text-heading`, `-section-title`, `-section-title-compact`, `-cta-title`, `-article-title`, `-page-title`, `-hero`), weight (`--weight-regular` … `-extrabold`), line height (`--leading-none`, `-display`, `-heading`, `-snug`, `-normal`, `-relaxed`, `-loose`), letter spacing (`--tracking-tight`, `-slight`, `-wide`, `-widest`), spacing (`--space-<n>` = n × `--space-unit` 4 px, half steps `-0-5`, `-1-5` …, so a theme changes density with one value), shape (`--radius-*`, `--radius-group` for the cells of the app areas, `--radius-qr-ground` the white ground of the payment QR inside its frame, concentric with it: `--radius-card` less the frame's 2 px border and `--space-2-5` padding, 4 px, about one module of the smallest drawn QR and so well inside its 4-module quiet zone), depth (`--shadow-*`, one per role: `--shadow-card` for public cards, `--shadow-group` for the flat cells of the app areas), motion (`--duration-fast`, `-base`, `-slow`, `-entrance`), layout (`--content-max`, `--header-height`, `--page-top` (the top of a page's first section), `--floating-notice-height`, `--side-nav-layout-max`, `--side-nav-width`, `--dialog-max-width`, `-wide`, `--dialog-close-inset`, `-size`, `--back-button-size`, `--scroll-edge-fade`, `--touch-target-size`, `--list-row-min-height`, `--list-group-title-top`), blur (`--blur-backdrop`, `--blur-bar`), the app layout of plan v3 2.1 (the floating tab bar: `--color-tab-bar`, `-bar-rim`, `-pill`, `-pill-rim`, `-active`, `--color-on-tab-bar`, `--color-tab-focus-ring`, `--shadow-tab-bar`, `--tab-bar-height`, `--tab-bar-rendered-height` (the theme's height, replaced on the root by the bar's measured one), `-gap`, `-gap-browser`, `--tab-pill-inset`, solid under `prefers-reduced-transparency`; the sheet: `--sheet-max-width`, `--radius-sheet`, `--shadow-sheet`, `--duration-sheet`, `--color-sheet-grabber`, `--sheet-grabber-width`, `-height`; no top bar, Jakub 2026-11-05), the receipt PDF (`--receipt-*`: colours, sizes in `pt`, `--receipt-leading`, margins in `mm`, read by `features/donations/receipt-pdf/receipt-style.ts`, whose list `check-themes.mjs` requires in every theme).
- Breakpoints only through `@use 'breakpoints' as bp;` (`src/styles/_breakpoints.scss`, on the include path): `@include bp.down(phone)` is `max-width`, `bp.up(cards)` is `min-width` one pixel above; names `tabs` 208 (only for the tab bar's container: four labels side by side at the root text size), `pager` 312 (only for the pager's container: both labelled steps and the position side by side at the smallest text, with room to spare), `tiny` 360, `narrow` 420, `phone` 480 (dialogs full-screen), `cards` 640 (tables as cards), `tablet` 768, `nav` 899 (side panel collapses). A new width is a new name there, never a raw `@media`, and the same name and width in `core/layout/breakpoints.ts` (`scripts/breakpoints-parity.test.mjs` fails otherwise). Code that must choose a layout asks `CompactLayout.isCompact()` (`core/layout/compact-layout.ts`, `bp.down(nav)`) and renders one layout with `@if`, never two hidden by CSS; a list that is a table on wide screens asks `CompactLayout.listsAsRows()` instead (also true where the window is narrower than `nav` in the root text size, so a table that would not fit enlarged text becomes rows, plan v3 2.25). Where a layout must give way when the visitor enlarges the text, `bp.container-down(<container>, name)` and `bp.container-up` ask a size container the same width in `rem` of the root text size: the payment panel (`payment-panel`), the donor's table of gifts (`donations-table`) and the administration's months (`admin-overview`) keep their viewport query and add `container-down(…, phone)`, so at the default text size nothing changes and at a large one they stack (`e2e/devices/`). A container is a containing block for its fixed descendants, so it goes on a component's own box, never on a shell that holds a fixed bar.
- `npm run lint:styles` (in CI) rejects in components a raw colour, font, font size, weight, line height, letter spacing, radius, shadow, a `px` spacing other than a 1 px hairline (`padding`, `margin`, `gap`), a transition duration and a `width` media query. A missing value is a new token in the theme (pick the nearest existing one first; a value used once is not a reason for a token), never an inline value or a lint exception.
- Shared building blocks are global classes in `src/styles/ui/` (prefix `ui-`, BEM modifiers, loaded by `styles.scss` after the form controls): `_page` (`.ui-page`, `-head`, `-title`, `-intro`, `.ui-crumbs`), `_card` (`.ui-card`, flat with `--radius-group` and `--shadow-group` inside `.ui-grouped`, the class of `DonorShell` on `/darce` and `/sprava`, where `--color-surface-alt` points to `--color-surface-muted`, `--danger`, `--padded` for a card whose content sits on its padding, `__title` with `--large`, `__subtitle`, `__bar` for a heading strip over a table or list, `__body`, `__intro`, `__empty`, `__footer`; `.ui-data-list` for a `dl` of label and value), `_notice` (`.ui-notice--status|--alert|--warning`, `--floating` for a bar fixed at the bottom of the screen with `__text` beside its button (the page keeps room for it through `:root.has-floating-notice` and the layout token `--floating-notice-height`, `0rem` in the theme and measured by the component that shows it), `.ui-load-state` `--error`, `--inset`, `.ui-badge--success|--warning|--accent`, `.ui-mono`, `.ui-callout--danger|--warning` for a framed message inside a card), `_figures` (`.ui-figures` for every amount, account number, IBAN, variable symbol and receipt number, never wrapping inside; `--groups` wraps only between its child spans), `_ink` (`.ui-ink` for an uploaded picture of ink on white, a web signature: the theme's `--filter-ink` and `--blend-ink`, so in the dark the ink turns light and the white disappears), `_button` (`.ui-button` soft accent, `--primary`, `--small`, `--quiet`, `--large` beside a primary, `--danger`, `--danger-soft`, `--danger-outline`, `--block` for a full-width form submit; `.ui-link-button`; `.ui-pill-button` with `--done`, the small outlined pill of `copy-button` and `share-button`), `_table` (`.ui-table`, cards at `bp.down(cards)` and wherever `.ui-table--stacked` asks, as the donor card's parts do in rows), `_form` (`.ui-field-error` under a form, `.ui-segmented` choice of two or three buttons with `.is-active`, `.ui-form-status` for the saved line after a form), `_action-bar` (`.ui-action-bar`: the sticky bar at the bottom of the viewport that ends a long form, with `appActionBar` from `shared/ui/action-bar` so `scroll-padding-bottom` keeps the focused field above it), `_dialog` (inside `<app-dialog>`: `.ui-dialog__title` for its `h2`, `__lead` for the sentence under it, `__points` for the list of what a confirmation does, `__actions` for its row of buttons, sticky at the bottom of a sheet so they stay in reach, and a field the focus moves to under it is scrolled above it), `_list` (`.ui-list` of rows `__row`, an `a` or `button`, one target per row with a second action as its own named button beside it, never inside: `__lead`, `__title`, `__meta`, `__value` with tabular figures, `__count` for a count pill beside the title (`aria-hidden`, its description visually hidden; `--warning` amber for what waits on an administrator), `__chevron` with `aria-hidden`, `__group-title` a sticky heading, `--inset` for grouped rows on a card; `--grouped` for the inset grouped cards of iOS settings on a `[grouped]` dialog's `--color-sheet-grouped` (`--color-sheet-group`, `--radius-sheet-group`, dividers that start at the title, regular titles; the account sheet), with `__row--danger` for a destructive row whose title stands centred in `--color-danger`; `__state` for a badge under `__value`, `__extra` for what spans the row under the title (a receipt download, a summary); a row without `__meta` stands on one line, and rows inside a `.ui-card` take its padding; the lists of `CompactLayout.listsAsRows()` since 2.25), `_group` (settings in grouped rows, the alerts page and the section rows of the administration: `.ui-groups` stacks the groups, `.ui-group-title` the small upper-case heading above a group, `.ui-group-note` the footnote under it with `--status`, `--alert` and `--warning`; a `label.ui-list__row` row holding `.ui-switch`, a native `input type="checkbox" role="switch"` drawn as the iOS switch from the `--switch-*` and `--color-switch-*` tokens, its description in `__meta` named by `aria-describedby`; `__row--action` takes `--color-accent` in an `--inset` list too), `_person-list` (`.ui-person-list` of `.ui-person-row`: `__avatar` on an `app-avatar`, `__name`, `__meta` for the line under the name, `__badges`, `__note` as a sibling of the text block under it, across the columns beside the portrait, so the portrait centres on the name, the line under it and the badges only, `__actions` at the right of that first row and under the row up to `cards`, `__detail` across the row for an inline confirmation; the people of the foundation and the administrators), `_panel` (a standalone page such as the password reset: `.ui-panel-page` around `.ui-panel`, `__title`, `__lead`; `.ui-status` with `__icon` (`--success` green, `--caution` amber, `--danger` red for a verdict; the older `--warning` is red too, so an amber warning takes `--caution`), `--spinner` for a result or a wait), `_section` (the public site: `.ui-container` for the content width of a section, `.ui-eyebrow` above a section title with `--on-inverse` and `--accent-on-inverse` on a dark background, `.ui-intro` for the dark band that opens a page (`--light` for the light one of Aktuality) with `__title` (`--article` for a news or project title) and `__lead`, `.ui-cta` for a large call to action with `--ghost` and, on top of it, `--on-inverse` or `--on-image`), `_pager` (the paging row at the end of Pomáháme and Aktuality, like an iOS toolbar: `.ui-pager` is a three-column grid and a size container `pager`, `__step` a `.ui-button` link at an edge, `--newer` in the first column and `--older` in the last, each with an inline chevron `__icon` (`aria-hidden`) beside its `__label`, and `__position` "Strana N z M" centred in the middle column; a direction that does not exist is left out while its column stays, so the position stays in the middle; below the container width `pager` the steps become 44 px round chevrons with the label visually hidden, so the row never wraps on one side, and the position may balance onto two lines). A page, card, notice, button or table uses them in its template and never copies their declarations; its SCSS keeps only layout and the rare override (a margin, a narrower width). An element keeps its own semantic class beside the `ui-` ones where specs or layout need a hook (`class="ui-button ui-button--primary profile-save"`). A look used in two features becomes a new block there, not a copy. Behaviour with a template stays an Angular component in `shared/ui/`.
- Translucency with `color-mix()`, never `rgba()`. Fonts self-hosted variable WOFF2; nothing loads from another origin.
- Touch like a native app (#380, #385; `docs/frontend-overview.md`, Touch): no tap flash, and controls (`a[href]`, `button`, `summary`, `[role="button"]`, `[role="tab"]`) are neither selectable nor draggable and have no long-press callout, while content text, the bank details, fields and the classless links of running text keep selection and the system menu (`src/styles/_controls.scss`). Every enabled control shows a pressed state on `:active` at once, through `@use 'pressed'` (`when-pressed` or `look` with `tints`, `darkens` or `shrinks`, so `forced-colors` and `prefers-reduced-motion` keep holding) and the theme's pressed tokens (`--color-pressed`, a tint of `currentColor`, `--pressed-brightness`, `--pressed-scale`); `PressedOnTouch` keeps the touch listener Safari needs for it. Hover looks stand only inside `@media (hover: hover)`, which `npm run lint:styles` enforces (`nfnn/hover-only-with-hover`), so a tap leaves no hover behind.
- Tables become cards at `bp.down(cards)` (`data-label` per cell); fluid sizes are `clamp()` tokens; `prefers-reduced-motion` stops animations (global rule in `styles.scss`).
- Short screens (a phone on its side, an iPhone SE): heights are named like widths, in `$heights` of `_breakpoints.scss` with `bp.height-down(name)` and in `HEIGHTS` of `core/layout/breakpoints.ts` (`short` 500, `hero-lead` 680; the parity test covers both maps), never a raw `max-height` query; code asks `ShortViewport.isShort()` (`core/layout/short-viewport.ts`) and renders with `@if`. A full-screen block is at least `100svh` (Safari's `100vh` is the viewport with its toolbar retracted, so it scrolls) and grows with its content; its text keeps `max(<space>, env(safe-area-inset-left|right))` beside it as `.ui-container` does, the header's room above and `--tab-bar-clearance` under it. Safari paints the area outside the page (under its floating toolbar) with the `body`'s background, not the `html`'s, so a page that wants that strip dark colours `body`. Text never runs over the face in a photo: the landing hero measures it in `e2e/first-screen.spec.ts`; on short screens `.ui-intro` stands its title and lead side by side and the hero shrinks its headline (`--text-hero-short`) and only visually hides its lead.
- Windows of an iPad (#396): in a window smaller than the screen the iPadOS window controls float over the top-leading corner of the home-screen app and WebKit reports no inset for them, so `WindowControls` (`core/layout/window-controls.ts`) marks the root `has-window-controls` and content at that corner keeps `max(<its own space>, var(--window-controls-inset-inline))` beside it (the header, the back button, the skip link) or `var(--window-controls-inset-block)` above it (the sticky side panel); a new fixed or sticky control at the top-leading corner does the same (`e2e/window-controls.spec.ts`).
- Images: everything that can be SVG is SVG (Jakub 2026-10-30: the logo, the tab icon `favicon.svg`, icons, drawings); raster only where no SVG can do (photos, and where a platform takes only raster: `og-image.jpg`, `apple-touch-icon.png`, `favicon.ico` at the default address, unlinked, for services such as claude.ai's connector list, and the iOS launch images, which the build draws from the theme's `launch-icon.svg`, never kept as files). Photos are WebP in `public/images/` (except `og-image.jpg` for link previews); the logo, `logo.svg` and `logo-on-dark.svg` for a dark background, is a theme asset in `src/themes/<name>/assets/images/`, served at `/images/…`, and its star keeps its face, since a filter that whitens the logo would whiten the face too; photos come in sized variants with `srcset`/`sizes`, `width`/`height`, `loading="lazy"` written before `src` below the fold, `fetchpriority="high"` for the hero, decorative photos `alt=""`; icons inline SVG `aria-hidden="true" stroke="currentColor"`; no emoji in UI text.
- Budgets in `angular.json`: initial bundle warns at 500 kB, component styles at 4 kB.

### UX (how it behaves)
- Offline (plan v3 2.7, `docs/frontend-overview.md` `core/connectivity/`): whether the app is offline comes only from `Connectivity`, which judges the API's answers and its own probe, and trusts the browser only when it says offline (`navigator.onLine` false, which a phone in airplane mode reports while its requests hang; Jakub 2026-11-06), never when it says online; `browserSaysOffline()` serves the error reporter and the chunk-load recovery. Nothing waits on the network when `Connectivity` says offline: a public page shows the service worker's copy at once and is refreshed behind it (`keptAnswerRefreshInterceptor`), and the session restore gives up at once. Every control that needs the server (every submit button, every button without a `type`, every `ui-button--danger` or `--danger-soft` button and every button whose `(click)` reaches a `subscribe`, `firstValueFrom` or `lastValueFrom` through its component's own methods) carries `appNeedsConnection`, and its component imports `NeedsConnection`: offline it stays visible and focusable, `aria-disabled` with the hint "Potřebuje připojení", and nothing is sent; `scripts/needs-connection.mjs` (run by `npm run lint`) fails on one without it, and a control that works offline goes into its `WORKS_WITHOUT_CONNECTION` (a template, or `template#handler`) with the reason; an entry that names no control fails too. A call the check cannot see (a click that hands the work to another service) still gets the directive by hand. The fixed offline pill keeps room for itself through `html.is-offline` and `--offline-pill-height`, so a new fixed or sticky bar at the bottom rises above it as `.ui-action-bar` does. Public data shown from a copy (the service worker's or the payment details kept in `localStorage` under `nfnn.payment-details`) says how old it is and that it "nemusí být aktuální".
- Every action shows loading, success (`role="status"` line) and failure (`role="alert"` line with the backend title/detail or a Czech sentence saying what to do next); a running request disables its button and names what happens ("Připravujeme PDF…"). Put `role="status"` on an element that is always rendered and the `@if` inside it (`<p role="status">@if (msg()) {<span>{{ msg() }}</span>}</p>`, as in `passkeys-card.html`): a status element inserted together with its text is often not announced. `role="alert"` may be inserted with its text. A page's success-or-failure line is a `Notice` (`{ kind: 'status' | 'alert', text }`) shown by `<app-live-notice [notice]="…" />` (`shared/ui/live-notice`), which keeps both regions rendered; elsewhere wrap the `@if` in `<div role="status">`. A line that appears with a page or with a whole new view (the first loading line, a wizard step that takes focus) may stay inside its `@if`. A notice of the app shell that may appear on any page (`update-notice`) is an always rendered `aria-live="polite"` region without `role="status"`, so each page keeps exactly one status region. Specs read these regions as `[role="status"]:not(:empty)`, e2e as `getByRole('alert').filter({ hasText: /\S/ })`.
- Forms: visible labels, hint before error, error at the field in its own words, focus to the first invalid field, placeholders never the only label, every password field inside `<app-password-reveal>`.
- Dialogs through `<app-dialog>` on native `<dialog>` (`showModal()`: focus trapped, Esc, backdrop and × dismiss, focus returns to the opener); shared open state in a service (`LoginDialogService.open(mode, options)`); every dialog keeps a step in the site's own history through `DialogHistory`, so a step back (Zpět, the home-screen app's swipe, `history.back()`) closes it (a stack: a dialog over a dialog has its own step, and a step back closes only the top one); the browser's Back leaves the site (Routing) (only a dialog whose opener keeps the history itself sets `[pushesHistory]="false"`: the sign-in dialog and the unsaved-changes dialog of a navigation guard); `presentation` (`auto` from the area's `DIALOG_PRESENTATION`, `centered` today, `sheet`, `full`) chooses the look below `nav`, a sheet keeps its ×. A sheet goes down under the finger as on iOS (#382): by its grabber, and by its content when that is scrolled to its top; a release far enough or a flick closes it exactly like ×, otherwise it springs back; every close of a sheet (the release, ×, Esc, the backdrop, a step back) slides it down from where it is with its veil and blur fading, and only then removes it, except under `prefers-reduced-motion`; a sheet that cannot close now, or whose page sets `[unsavedInput]` for input that took effort and is kept nowhere else, only stretches, and the latter asks "Zahodit rozepsané údaje?" first. The sign-in dialog stays full-screen on phones without the gesture (experts' decision for #382: the public part stays a web, roadmap 2.23). A control inside a sheet that needs its own vertical drag is one `canPullSheetFrom` (`sheet-drag.ts`) leaves alone. A modal dialog makes the page behind it inert, so something behind it that must be heard while it is open (the chunk-load bar) announces through `DialogAnnouncer` (`shared/ui/dialog`), whose always rendered `role="status"` region sits inside every `<app-dialog>`; e2e therefore reads a dialog's status as `getByRole('status').filter({ hasText: /\S/ })`.
- Accessibility (Jakub 2026-10-23): WCAG 2.2 AA for keyboard, screen readers, zoom and system settings, without changing the default look or behaviour; a visible change made for it needs Jakub's OK (he declined darker field borders and stopping the hero scroll dot: those change only under `prefers-contrast: more` and `prefers-reduced-motion`).
  - Semantics: native elements first; one `main#obsah` per shell behind the skip link "Přeskočit na obsah" (top-left, Jakub 2026-10-18), named `nav` landmarks, one `h1`, headings in order; exclusive choices use `appRadioGroup` (`shared/ui/radio-group`: arrows, Home/End, roving `tabindex`), toggles `aria-pressed`, disclosures `aria-expanded`/`aria-controls`; an `aria-label` contains the visible text; live regions `role="status"`/`role="alert"`, and a notice of the app shell an always rendered `aria-live="polite"` region (UX above).
  - Focus: the 3 px `--color-focus-ring` outline on `:focus-visible` for every control, never removed without a replacement of the same colour; after a route change the shell focuses the page `h1`, after an in-page link the target section (`tabindex="-1"`, no ring); dialogs and panels return focus to the opener; an overlay closes when focus leaves it; `scroll-padding-top` keeps focus from under the fixed header.
  - Size: type tokens in `rem`, so the browser's text size applies; pages hold at 320 px and with WCAG text spacing (nothing cut off, single grid columns `minmax(0, 1fr)`); targets at least 24 px, and on a touch screen (`pointer: coarse`) a small control reaches the 44 pt of Apple's guidelines without growing visibly: `@include touch-target.reaches-finger-size` (`src/styles/_touch-target.scss`, a `::before` of `--touch-target-size` that also positions the control, or `($positioned: true)` for one positioned already; `e2e/touch-targets.spec.ts`).
  - Zoom (Jakub 2026-10-31 00:56, plan v3 2.23d): the site and the installed app behave like an app, so the whole page never zooms: the viewport meta keeps `maximum-scale=1` (Android honours it), `PagePinchLock` (`core/zoom/`) cancels Safari's `gesture*` events (iOS ignores the meta) and `html` keeps `touch-action: manipulation` (no double-tap zoom). Never `user-scalable=no`, never a text size that ignores the root: WCAG 1.4.4 is met by text resizing (Safari aA, Android font size, Ctrl + on a computer; an app opened from the home screen of an iPhone or iPad has no aA menu, so there alone, `navigator.standalone` with a touch screen, `SystemTextSize` (`core/zoom/`) marks `html.follows-system-text-size` and the root takes `font: -apple-system-body`, the system text size (Dynamic Type; 17 px at the default size, Jakub 2026-11-05, Q13), with the theme's font family; Safari in a tab, a Mac's Dock app and every other browser keep the browser's root size), which the `rem` tokens honour, so a layout must hold at 200 % text as at 320 px. What a visitor may need to see larger (a content photo, a payment QR) sits in `<app-zoomable [content]>` (`shared/ui/zoom-viewer`), whose full-screen viewer zooms by pinch, double tap, wheel, keys and buttons; a new photo or QR shown to visitors gets it too. axe's `meta-viewport` rule is off for this decision alone (`PAGE_PINCH_STOPPED_JAKUB_2026_10_31` in `e2e/accessibility.spec.ts`); the trade-off Jakub accepted: people who pinch to read must use the text size instead.
